===== src/core/forms/form.py =====
# src/core/forms/form.py
"""Form — the only in-memory data structure.

An n-dimensional tensor with named axes, per-axis label maps, and
an info dictionary.

Axis-parallel info
------------------
Some info entries are arrays whose length equals the size of a named
axis (for example ``chrom`` and ``pos`` for the ``variant`` axis of
a VCF). Such entries must be declared in ``axis_info[dim]`` so that
``subset_axis`` can keep them in sync when the axis is trimmed. No
inference by length is performed.

Label coverage
--------------
Every position along every axis must have a label. ``validate()``
refuses a form whose labels do not cover positions 0..size-1.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field

import numpy as np

from src.core.errors import ValidationError


@dataclass
class Form:
    """An n-dimensional tensor with named axes and label maps.

    Parameters
    ----------
    data : np.ndarray
        The tensor. No headers.
    dims : list[str]
        Axis names, in axis order. ``len(dims) == data.ndim``.
    labels : dict[str, dict[str, int]]
        One entry per dim. Maps ``{dim_name: {label: position}}``.
        Every axis position must appear as a value in the mapping.
    info : dict
        Form-level attributes. Non-array values are passed through
        unchanged.
    axis_info : dict[str, list[str]]
        ``{dim_name: [info_key, ...]}``. Each listed info key must be
        a list or 1-D array whose length equals the size of that
        axis. ``subset_axis`` trims those entries along with data.
    """

    data: np.ndarray
    dims: list[str]
    labels: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    info: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    axis_info: dict[str, list[str]] = field(default_factory=dict)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
    # Lookups
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

    def axis(self, name: str) -> int:
        try:
            return self.dims.index(name)
        except ValueError:
            raise ValidationError(
                "Form",
                f"unknown dim '{name}'",
                f"available: {self.dims}",
            )

    def get_parallel(self, field: str, dim: str) -> np.ndarray | None:
        """Return an array parallel to ``dim`` identified by ``field``.

        Looks first for a data column with that name in ``labels[dim]``.
        Then looks in ``info`` if ``field`` is declared in
        ``axis_info[dim]``. Returns None if neither applies.
        """
        if dim in self.labels and field in self.labels[dim]:
            pos = self.labels[dim][field]
            axis = self.dims.index(dim)
            return np.take(self.data, pos, axis=axis)
        if dim in self.axis_info and field in self.axis_info[dim]:
            if field in self.info:
                return np.asarray(self.info[field])
        return None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
    # Subsetting
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

    def subset_axis(self, dim: str, indices) -> "Form":
        """Return a new form restricted to ``indices`` along ``dim``.

        Trims data, labels, and only those info entries declared in
        ``axis_info[dim]``. Nothing else is touched.
        """
        if dim not in self.dims:
            raise ValidationError("Form", f"unknown dim '{dim}'")
        axis = self.dims.index(dim)
        indices = np.asarray(indices, dtype=np.intp) if len(indices) == 0 else np.asarray(indices)
        axis_size = self.data.shape[axis]
        if indices.ndim != 1 or indices.dtype.kind not in "iu" or len(set(indices.tolist())) != len(indices):
            raise ValidationError("Form", "subset indices must be unique integers")
        if np.any(indices < 0) or np.any(indices >= axis_size):
            raise ValidationError("Form", "subset index out of range")

        # Labels must cover every position before subsetting.
        pos_to_label = {v: k for k, v in self.labels[dim].items()}
        missing = [i for i in range(axis_size) if i not in pos_to_label]
        if missing:
            raise ValidationError(
                "Form",
                f"dim '{dim}' has unlabeled positions",
                f"first missing: {missing[:5]}",
            )

        new_data = np.take(self.data, indices, axis=axis).copy()

        new_dim_labels = {
            pos_to_label[int(old)]: new_pos
            for new_pos, old in enumerate(indices)
        }
        new_labels = {k: dict(v) for k, v in self.labels.items()}
        new_labels[dim] = new_dim_labels

        from .metadata import project_metadata
        new_info, new_axis_info = project_metadata(self, {dim: indices})
        return Form(
            data=new_data,
            dims=list(self.dims),
            labels=new_labels,
            info=new_info,
            axis_info=new_axis_info,
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
    # Validation
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

    def validate(self) -> None:
        if not isinstance(self.data, np.ndarray):
            raise ValidationError("Form", "data must be a numpy array")
        if not isinstance(self.info, dict) or not isinstance(self.axis_info, dict):
            raise ValidationError("Form", "info and axis_info must be dictionaries")
        if any(not isinstance(dim, str) or not dim for dim in self.dims):
            raise ValidationError("Form", "dimensions must be nonempty strings")

        if len(self.dims) != self.data.ndim:
            raise ValidationError(
                "Form",
                f"len(dims)={len(self.dims)} != data.ndim={self.data.ndim}",
            )

        if len(set(self.dims)) != len(self.dims):
            raise ValidationError("Form", "dims contain duplicates")

        for dim_name in self.dims:
            if dim_name not in self.labels:
                raise ValidationError(
                    "Form",
                    f"dim '{dim_name}' has no labels entry",
                    f"labels cover: {sorted(self.labels)}",
                )

        for dim_name, mapping in self.labels.items():
            if dim_name not in self.dims:
                raise ValidationError(
                    "Form", f"labels reference unknown dim '{dim_name}'"
                )
            size = self.data.shape[self.axis(dim_name)]

            # Every position must be labeled.
            seen = set()
            for lab, pos in mapping.items():
                if not isinstance(pos, int) or isinstance(pos, bool):
                    raise ValidationError(
                        "Form",
                        f"labels.{dim_name}.{lab} must be an int",
                    )
                if not 0 <= pos < size:
                    raise ValidationError(
                        "Form",
                        f"labels.{dim_name}.{lab}={pos} out of range "
                        f"(axis size {size})",
                    )
                if pos in seen:
                    raise ValidationError("Form", f"dim '{dim_name}' has multiple labels for position {pos}")
                seen.add(pos)
            unlabeled = set(range(size)) - seen
            if unlabeled:
                raise ValidationError(
                    "Form",
                    f"dim '{dim_name}' has unlabeled positions",
                    f"first missing: {sorted(unlabeled)[:5]}",
                )

        bound_fields = set()
        for dim_name, keys in self.axis_info.items():
            if not isinstance(keys, list) or any(not isinstance(key, str) for key in keys):
                raise ValidationError("Form", "axis_info entries must be lists of field names")
            if dim_name not in self.dims:
                raise ValidationError(
                    "Form",
                    f"axis_info references unknown dim '{dim_name}'",
                )
            size = self.data.shape[self.axis(dim_name)]
            for k in keys:
                if k in bound_fields:
                    raise ValidationError("Form", f"info.{k} must bind to exactly one axis")
                bound_fields.add(k)
                if k not in self.info:
                    raise ValidationError(
                        "Form",
                        f"axis_info['{dim_name}'] lists '{k}' but it is "
                        f"not in info",
                    )
                v = self.info[k]
                if isinstance(v, np.ndarray) and v.ndim != 1:
                    raise ValidationError("Form", f"info.{k} must be a one-dimensional array")
                if isinstance(v, list) and any(isinstance(item, (list, tuple, np.ndarray)) for item in v):
                    raise ValidationError("Form", f"info.{k} must be a one-dimensional list")
                if not isinstance(v, (list, np.ndarray)):
                    raise ValidationError(
                        "Form",
                        f"info['{k}'] declared parallel to '{dim_name}' "
                        f"but is not a list or array",
                    )
                if len(v) != size:
                    raise ValidationError(
                        "Form",
                        f"info['{k}'] declared parallel to '{dim_name}' "
                        f"but has length {len(v)} != {size}",
                    )
        encoders = self.info.get("encoders", {})
        if not isinstance(encoders, dict):
            raise ValidationError("Form", "info.encoders must be a dimension mapping")
        for dim, fields in encoders.items():
            if dim not in self.dims or not isinstance(fields, dict):
                raise ValidationError("Form", f"invalid encoder dimension {dim}")
            for key in fields:
                if key not in self.labels[dim] and key not in self.axis_info.get(dim, []):
                    raise ValidationError("Form", f"orphan encoder {dim}.{key}")

===== src/core/forms/metadata.py =====
"""Axis-aware metadata operations. Inputs and their nested maps stay immutable."""
import numbers

import numpy as np

from src.core.errors import StepError


def project_metadata(form, selections=None, *, keep_dims=None, rename=None, derived=False):
    selections, rename = selections or {}, rename or {}
    kept = set(form.dims if keep_dims is None else keep_dims)
    info = dict(form.info)
    axis_info = {}
    for dim, fields in form.axis_info.items():
        if dim not in kept:
            for field in fields:
                info.pop(field, None)
            continue
        axis_info[rename.get(dim, dim)] = list(fields)
        if dim in selections:
            for field in fields:
                value = info[field]
                indices = selections[dim]
                info[field] = value[indices].copy() if isinstance(value, np.ndarray) else [value[int(i)] for i in indices]
    encoders = {}
    for dim, fields in info.get("encoders", {}).items():
        if dim not in kept:
            continue
        labels = set(form.labels.get(dim, {}))
        if dim in selections:
            positions = set(map(int, selections[dim]))
            labels = {label for label, pos in form.labels[dim].items() if pos in positions}
        if derived:
            labels = set()  # Original data columns no longer contain encoded values.
        fields = {key: encoder for key, encoder in fields.items()
                  if key in labels or key in form.axis_info.get(dim, [])}
        if fields:
            encoders[rename.get(dim, dim)] = fields
    if encoders:
        info["encoders"] = encoders
    else:
        info.pop("encoders", None)
    if derived:
        for key in ("representation", "ploidy", "alphabet"):
            info.pop(key, None)
    return info, axis_info


def equal_metadata(left, right):
    if left is right:
        return True
    if hasattr(left, "mapping") and hasattr(right, "mapping"):
        return type(left) is type(right) and equal_metadata(left.mapping, right.mapping)
    if isinstance(left, dict) and isinstance(right, dict):
        return left.keys() == right.keys() and all(equal_metadata(left[k], right[k]) for k in left)
    if isinstance(left, (list, tuple, np.ndarray)) and isinstance(right, (list, tuple, np.ndarray)):
        return len(left) == len(right) and all(equal_metadata(a, b) for a, b in zip(left, right))
    if isinstance(left, numbers.Real) and isinstance(right, numbers.Real) and np.isnan(left) and np.isnan(right):
        return True
    return bool(left == right)


def concat_metadata(forms, axis):
    """Union aligned annotations; refuse conflicting annotations/encodings."""
    ref = forms[0]
    if any(not equal_metadata(f.info.get("missing_code"), ref.info.get("missing_code")) for f in forms[1:]):
        raise StepError("concat: missing_code differs; normalize missing values before concatenation")
    bindings = {}
    for form in forms:
        for dim, fields in form.axis_info.items():
            for key in fields:
                if key in bindings and bindings[key] != dim:
                    raise StepError(f"concat: info.{key} is bound to different axes")
                if any(key in other.info and key not in other.axis_info.get(dim, []) for other in forms):
                    raise StepError(f"concat: info.{key} mixes scalar and axis annotations")
                bindings[key] = dim
    info = {key: value for key, value in ref.info.items()
            if key not in bindings and key != "encoders" and all(key in f.info and equal_metadata(value, f.info[key]) for f in forms[1:])}
    if not all(f.info.get("representation") == ref.info.get("representation") for f in forms):
        info.pop("representation", None)
        info.pop("ploidy", None)
    axis_info = {}
    for key, dim in bindings.items():
        if dim == axis:
            info[key] = [value for f in forms for value in f.info.get(key, [None] * f.data.shape[f.axis(dim)])]
        else:
            values = [f.info[key] for f in forms if key in f.info]
            if any(not equal_metadata(values[0], value) for value in values[1:]):
                raise StepError(f"concat: conflicting info.{key} on aligned axis {dim}")
            info[key] = values[0].copy() if isinstance(values[0], np.ndarray) else list(values[0])
        axis_info.setdefault(dim, []).append(key)
    encoders = {}
    for form in forms:
        for dim, fields in form.info.get("encoders", {}).items():
            for key, encoder in fields.items():
                existing = encoders.setdefault(dim, {}).get(key)
                if existing is not None and not equal_metadata(existing, encoder):
                    raise StepError(f"concat: incompatible encoders for {dim}.{key}")
                encoders[dim][key] = encoder
    if encoders:
        info["encoders"] = encoders
    return info, axis_info

===== src/core/contracts.py =====
"""Versioned, JSON-serializable port contracts and conservative type inference.

Declarations live on Reader/Step classes. This module is deliberately independent
of discovery, the web API and the executor. An unknown fact never proves a type.
"""
from copy import deepcopy
import math
import numbers

import numpy as np

from src.core.errors import StepError

VERSION = 1
MISSING = {"type": ["number", "null"]}


def param(name, default=None):
    return {"param": name, "default": default}


def port(*, info=None, dims=None, labels=None, parallel=None, ndim=None, values=None):
    result = {"version": VERSION, "numeric": True, "info": info or {},
              "dims": dims or [], "labels": labels or [], "parallel": parallel or []}
    if ndim is not None:
        result["ndim"] = ndim
    if values:
        result["values"] = values
    return result


def reader_contract(*, representation=None, ploidy=None, coordinates=False, ndim=2, extra=None):
    info = {"missing_code": MISSING, "encoders": {"type": "object", "optional": True}, **(extra or {})}
    if representation:
        info["representation"] = {"type": "string", "const": representation}
        info["ploidy"] = {"type": "integer", "min": 1, **({"const": ploidy} if ploidy else {})}
    if coordinates:
        info.update(chrom={"type": "array", "items": "string"}, pos={"type": "array", "items": "integer"})
    return {**port(info=info, ndim=ndim, values="allele_count" if representation else None),
            "axis_fields": [{"axis": 0, "fields": ["chrom", "pos"]}] if coordinates else [],
            "info_open": False}


def resolve(value, params):
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        if "param" in value:
            resolved = params.get(value["param"], resolve(value.get("default"), params))
            return str(resolved) + value["suffix"] if resolved is not None and "suffix" in value else resolved
        return {key: resolve(item, params) for key, item in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [resolve(item, params) for item in value]
    return value


def unknown():
    return {"version": VERSION, "dims": None, "labels": {}, "info": {},
            "axis_info": {}, "info_open": True, "axis_info_open": True}


def _type(value):
    if value is None:
        return "null"
    if isinstance(value, (bool, np.bool_)):
        return "boolean"
    if isinstance(value, numbers.Integral):
        return "integer"
    if isinstance(value, numbers.Real):
        return "number"
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return "string"
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple, np.ndarray)):
        return "array"
    return "object"


def describe_metadata(metadata, shape, numeric=True):
    info = {}
    for key, value in metadata.get("info", {}).items():
        rule = {"type": _type(value)}
        if rule["type"] in ("string", "integer", "number", "boolean", "null"):
            if isinstance(value, numbers.Real) and not math.isfinite(value):
                rule["display"] = str(value)
            else:
                rule["const"] = value.item() if isinstance(value, np.generic) else value
        info[key] = rule
    return {"version": VERSION, "ndim": len(shape), "numeric": numeric,
            "dims": list(metadata["dims"]),
            "labels": {dim: list(mapping) for dim, mapping in metadata["labels"].items()},
            "info": info, "axis_info": deepcopy(metadata.get("axis_info", {})),
            "info_open": False, "axis_info_open": False}


def describe_form(form):
    return describe_metadata(vars(form), form.data.shape, form.data.dtype.kind in "biuf")


def source_schema(contract, dims):
    if not contract:
        return {**unknown(), "dims": list(dims), "ndim": len(dims)}
    return {"version": VERSION, "ndim": contract.get("ndim", len(dims)), "numeric": True,
            "dims": list(dims), "labels": {dim: None for dim in dims},
            "info": deepcopy(contract.get("info", {})),
            "info_open": contract.get("info_open", True), "axis_info_open": False,
            "axis_info": {dims[rule["axis"]]: rule["fields"] for rule in contract.get("axis_fields", [])
                          if rule["axis"] < len(dims)}}


def compatible(schema, requirement):
    """Compare guarantees with requirements: compatible / incompatible / unknown."""
    errors, pending = [], []
    def check(ok, message):
        if ok is False:
            errors.append(message)
        elif ok is None:
            pending.append(message)
    if requirement is None:
        return {"status": "unknown", "reasons": ["算子尚未声明输入契约"]}
    if "ndim" in requirement:
        check(None if "ndim" not in schema else schema["ndim"] == requirement["ndim"],
              f"需要 {requirement['ndim']} 维数据")
    if requirement.get("numeric"):
        check(schema.get("numeric"), "需要数值张量")
    dims = schema.get("dims")
    for dim in requirement.get("dims", []):
        check(None if dim is None or dims is None else dim in dims, f"需要维度 {dim or '（待设置）'}")
    for key, rule in requirement.get("info", {}).items():
        actual = schema.get("info", {}).get(key)
        if actual is None:
            if not rule.get("optional"):
                check(None if schema.get("info_open", True) else False, f"缺少 info.{key}")
            continue
        if actual.get("optional") and not rule.get("optional"):
            check(None, f"info.{key} 读取后确认")
        expected_types = rule.get("type", [])
        expected_types = expected_types if isinstance(expected_types, list) else [expected_types]
        actual_types = actual.get("type", [])
        actual_types = actual_types if isinstance(actual_types, list) else [actual_types]
        if expected_types:
            allowed = set(expected_types) | ({"integer"} if "number" in expected_types else set())
            check(True if actual_types and set(actual_types) <= allowed else
                  False if actual_types and not set(actual_types) & allowed else None,
                  f"info.{key} 需要 {' / '.join(expected_types)}")
        expected = rule.get("enum", [rule["const"]] if "const" in rule else None)
        if expected is not None:
            options = actual.get("enum", [actual["const"]] if "const" in actual else None)
            check(None if options is None else True if all(v in expected for v in options)
                  else False if not any(v in expected for v in options) else None,
                  f"info.{key} 需要 {' / '.join(map(str, expected))}；上游为 {options if options is not None else '待读取'}")
        if "min" in rule:
            check(actual["const"] >= rule["min"] if isinstance(actual.get("const"), numbers.Real)
                  else True if actual.get("min", -math.inf) >= rule["min"] else None,
                  f"info.{key} 需要 ≥ {rule['min']}")
    for rule in requirement.get("labels", []):
        dim, values = rule.get("dim"), rule.get("values")
        if rule.get("optional") and values is None:
            continue
        values = values if isinstance(values, list) else [values]
        known = schema.get("labels", {}).get(dim)
        for field in values:
            check(None if field is None or known is None else field in known,
                  f"labels.{dim} 需要 {field or '（待设置）'}")
    for field in requirement.get("labels_any", []):
        candidates = list(schema.get("labels", {}).values())
        check(True if any(field in labels for labels in candidates if labels is not None) else
              None if field is None or not candidates or any(labels is None for labels in candidates) else False,
              f"需要字段 {field or '（待设置）'}")
    for rule in requirement.get("labels_axis", []):
        axis = rule["axis"]
        dim = dims[axis] if dims and axis < len(dims) else None
        known = schema.get("labels", {}).get(dim)
        values = rule.get("values", [])
        for field in values if isinstance(values, list) else [values]:
            check(None if field is None or known is None else field in known,
                  f"第 {axis + 1} 维需要字段 {field or '（待设置）'}")
    for rule in requirement.get("parallel", []):
        fields = rule.get("fields", [])
        fields = fields if isinstance(fields, list) else [fields]
        for field in fields:
            dim = rule.get("dim")
            axis_match = any(field in keys for d, keys in schema.get("axis_info", {}).items()
                             if dim is None or d == dim)
            labels = schema.get("labels", {}).get(dim)
            label_match = rule.get("allow_label") and labels is not None and field in labels
            uncertain = field is None or schema.get("axis_info_open", True) or (rule.get("allow_label") and labels is None)
            check(True if axis_match or label_match else None if uncertain else False,
                  f"需要 {field or '（待设置）'} 的轴注释" + (f"（{dim}）" if dim else ""))
    return {"status": "incompatible" if errors else "unknown" if pending else "compatible",
            "reasons": errors or pending}


def validate_form(form, requirement, where):
    form.validate()
    result = compatible(describe_form(form), requirement)
    if result["status"] != "compatible":
        # An undeclared extension still gets Form's structural validation.
        if requirement is None:
            return
        raise StepError(f"{where} contract: " + "; ".join(result["reasons"]))
    for key, rule in (requirement or {}).get("info", {}).items():
        if key in form.info and rule.get("items"):
            types = rule["items"] if isinstance(rule["items"], list) else [rule["items"]]
            if any(_type(value) not in types and not (_type(value) == "integer" and "number" in types)
                   for value in form.info[key]):
                raise StepError(f"{where} contract: info.{key} requires {rule['items']} items")
    validate_values(form.data, form.info, requirement, where, form=form)


def validate_values(data, info, requirement, where, *, form=None):
    """Data checks use bounded buffers, including non-contiguous tensors."""
    check = (requirement or {}).get("values")
    if check == "pvalue" and form is not None:
        rule = requirement["labels"][0]
        data = np.take(data, form.labels[rule["dim"]][rule["values"]], axis=form.axis(rule["dim"]))
    if check not in ("allele_count", "hard_call", "pvalue"):
        return
    with np.nditer(data, flags=["external_loop", "buffered", "zerosize_ok"],
                   op_flags=["readonly"], buffersize=65536) as iterator:
        for block in iterator:
            # external_loop may yield an unbuffered contiguous array; slice it
            # again so temporary masks are always bounded by 64K elements.
            for start in range(0, block.size, 65536):
                values = block[start:start + 65536]
                code = info.get("missing_code", np.nan)
                missing = np.isnan(values) if check == "pvalue" or (isinstance(code, numbers.Real) and np.isnan(code)) else values == code
                values = values[~missing]
                high = 1 if check == "pvalue" else info.get("ploidy", 2)
                invalid = ~np.isfinite(values) | (values < 0) | (values > high)
                if check == "hard_call" or (check == "allele_count" and info.get("representation") == "hard_call"):
                    invalid |= values != np.floor(values)
                if invalid.any():
                    raise StepError(f"{where} contract: invalid {check} value {values[invalid][0]!r}")


def infer_output(rule, inputs, params):
    """Metadata transfer rules; never inspect or allocate a data tensor."""
    if not rule or rule.get("mode") == "unknown":
        return unknown()
    mode = rule["mode"]
    if mode == "choose":
        choice = params.get(rule["param"], rule.get("default"))
        return infer_output(rule.get("cases", {}).get(str(choice), rule.get("otherwise")), inputs, params)
    if mode == "declare":
        # A plugin can state a conservative, parameter-dependent output schema
        # without adding a named branch to the engine. Runtime checks promises.
        return {**unknown(), **resolve(deepcopy(rule.get("schema", {})), params)}
    result = deepcopy(inputs[0] if inputs else unknown())
    result.pop("reader", None)
    if mode == "project":
        keep = resolve(rule.get("keep_dims", result.get("dims")), params)
        if keep is None:
            return unknown()
        dropped = resolve(rule.get("drop_dims", []), params)
        keep = [d for d in keep if d not in dropped]
        for axis, fields in list(result["axis_info"].items()):
            if axis not in keep:
                for key in fields:
                    result["info"].pop(key, None)
        result["axis_info"] = {d: f for d, f in result["axis_info"].items() if d in keep}
        result["labels"] = {d: f for d, f in result["labels"].items() if d in keep}
        result["dims"], result["ndim"] = keep, len(keep)
        if rule.get("derived", False):
            for key in ("representation", "ploidy", "alphabet", "encoders"):
                result["info"].pop(key, None)
        # Added axes are parameter-dependent or reference an axis of an input.
        # No tensor data is read to infer these guarantees.
        for side in ("prepend_dims", "append_dims"):
            added = []
            for declaration in rule.get(side, []):
                name = resolve(declaration.get("name"), params)
                reference = declaration.get("input_axis")
                if reference is not None:
                    source = inputs[reference[0]].get("dims") if reference[0] < len(inputs) else None
                    name = source[reference[1]] if source and reference[1] < len(source) else None
                if name is None:
                    return {**unknown(), "ndim": len(keep) + len(rule.get("prepend_dims", [])) + len(rule.get("append_dims", [])), "numeric": True}
                added.append(name)
                labels = resolve(declaration.get("labels"), params)
                if "labels_from" in declaration:
                    labels = result["labels"].get(resolve(declaration["labels_from"], params))
                result["labels"][name] = [labels] if isinstance(labels, str) else labels
            result["dims"] = added + result["dims"] if side == "prepend_dims" else result["dims"] + added
        result["ndim"] = len(result["dims"])
        if rule.get("labels_unknown"):
            result["labels"] = {d: None for d in result["dims"]}
        if rule.get("axis_info_unknown"):
            for fields in result["axis_info"].values():
                for field in fields:
                    result["info"].pop(field, None)
            result["axis_info"] = {}
            result["axis_info_open"] = True
        if "encoders" in result["info"]:
            result["info"]["encoders"]["optional"] = True
    dims = result.get("dims")
    dim = params.get("dim")
    if mode == "subset" and dim:
        result["labels"][dim] = None
    elif mode == "select" and dim:
        result["labels"][dim] = params.get("labels") or None
    elif mode == "drop" and dim:
        known = result["labels"].get(dim)
        result["labels"][dim] = [v for v in known if v not in params.get("labels", [])] if known is not None else None
    elif mode == "rename":
        old, new = params.get("from"), params.get("to")
        if old and new:
            result["dims"] = [new if d == old else d for d in dims] if dims else None
            for key in ("labels", "axis_info"):
                if old in result[key]:
                    result[key][new] = result[key].pop(old)
    elif mode == "transpose":
        result["dims"] = params.get("order") or None
    elif mode == "transform":
        for key in ("ploidy", "representation", "encoders", "alphabet"):
            result["info"].pop(key, None)
        result["info"].update(missing_code=MISSING, representation={"type": "string", "const": rule["representation"]})
    elif mode in ("cox_regression", "logrank_test"):
        for fields in result["axis_info"].values():
            for key in fields:
                result["info"].pop(key, None)
        for key in ("ploidy", "representation", "encoders", "alphabet"):
            result["info"].pop(key, None)
        axis = "covariate" if mode == "cox_regression" else "comparison"
        columns = ["coef", "HR", "se", "z", "P", "CI_low", "CI_high"] if mode == "cox_regression" else ["chi2", "df", "P"]
        result.update(ndim=2, dims=[axis, "statistic"], labels={axis: None, "statistic": columns}, axis_info={})
        result["info"].update(missing_code=MISSING, representation={"type": "string", "const": mode})
    elif mode in ("pca", "regress", "compare_groups"):
        kept = next((d for d in dims or [] if d != dim), None) if mode == "pca" else dim
        added = "component" if mode == "pca" else "statistic"
        for axis, fields in list(result["axis_info"].items()):
            if axis != kept:
                for key in fields:
                    result["info"].pop(key, None)
                del result["axis_info"][axis]
        for key in ("ploidy", "representation", "encoders", "alphabet"):
            result["info"].pop(key, None)
        result["info"].update(missing_code=MISSING, representation={"type": "string", "const": "scores" if mode == "pca" else "comparison" if mode == "compare_groups" else "regression"})
        result.update(ndim=2, dims=[kept, added] if kept else None)
        # PCA clips n_components to matrix rank; actual count is not guaranteed.
        known = result["labels"].get(kept)
        if mode == "regress" and known is not None:
            known = [v for v in known if v not in [params.get("response"), *params.get("covariates", [])]]
        result["labels"] = {**({kept: known} if kept else {}), added: None if mode == "pca" else ["BETA", "SE", "P", "N"]}
        if mode == "compare_groups":
            result["labels"][added] = ["log2FC", "mean_a", "mean_b", "stat", "P", "N"] + (["Q_VALUE"] if params.get("method") == "deseq2" else [])
            if params.get("mode", "two_groups") != "two_groups":
                result.update(ndim=3, dims=[kept, "comparison", added] if kept else None)
                result["labels"]["comparison"] = None
    elif mode == "correction":
        dim = params.get("dim", "statistic")
        known = result["labels"].get(dim)
        result["labels"][dim] = [*known, "Q_VALUE", "SIGNIFICANT"] if known is not None else None
        result["info"]["correction_method"] = {"type": "string", "const": params.get("method", "bonferroni")}
    elif mode == "attach":
        target = params.get("target_dim")
        fields = params.get("fields", [])
        if target and fields and len(inputs) > 1:
            for key in fields:
                result["info"][key] = {"type": "array"}
            result["axis_info"][target] = sorted(set(result["axis_info"].get(target, [])) | set(fields))
    elif mode == "concat":
        # Label intersections, missing annotations and scalar conflicts depend
        # on every input. Retain only common scalar guarantees at plan time.
        result["labels"] = {d: None for d in dims or []}
        result["axis_info"] = {}
        result["axis_info_open"] = True
        result["info"] = {key: value for key, value in result["info"].items()
                          if value.get("type") != "array" and all(other.get("info", {}).get(key) == value for other in inputs[1:])}
        result["info_open"] = any(other.get("info_open", True) for other in inputs)
        if "representation" not in result["info"]:
            result["info"].pop("ploidy", None)
    result["info"].update(deepcopy(rule.get("info", {})))
    if mode in {"subset", "select", "drop", "concat", "rename", "transpose", "attach"} and "encoders" in result["info"]:
        # Removing the last encoded field also removes its empty encoder map.
        result["info"]["encoders"]["optional"] = True
    return result


def validate_output(form, expected, where):
    """Check output promises before handing the Form to the cache manager."""
    required = {"numeric": expected.get("numeric", True), "info": expected.get("info", {}),
                "dims": expected.get("dims") or [], "labels": [], "parallel": []}
    if "ndim" in expected:
        required["ndim"] = expected["ndim"]
    for dim, labels in expected.get("labels", {}).items():
        if labels is not None:
            required["labels"].append({"dim": dim, "values": labels})
    for dim, fields in expected.get("axis_info", {}).items():
        required["parallel"].append({"dim": dim, "fields": fields})
    validate_form(form, required, where)
    if form.info.get("representation") in ("hard_call", "dosage"):
        validate_values(form.data, form.info, {"values": "allele_count"}, where)
    if expected.get("dims") is not None and form.dims != expected["dims"]:
        raise StepError(f"{where} contract: output dimension order differs from declaration")

===== src/reader/base.py =====
# src/reader/base.py
"""Reader abstract base class."""

from __future__ import annotations

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from pathlib import Path
from typing import ClassVar

from src.core import Form


class Reader(ABC):
    """Abstract reader.

    Subclasses declare which extensions they handle, which sibling
    files must or must not exist alongside the target, and whether
    they can consume compressed input directly.

    A reader receives the manifest's ``labels`` block and is
    responsible for producing a Form in which every dim has a label
    mapping. Four label specs are accepted per dim:

        builtin           use the format's built-in label source
        auto              position indices "0", "1", "2", ...
        {<dim>: <pos>}    read position <pos> along <dim>
        {name: pos, ...}  explicit mapping

    A missing dim entry defaults to ``auto``.

    Readers that encode non-numeric columns must record the encoder
    instance used in ``info["encoders"]`` as ``{dim: {label: encoder}}``.
    """

    output_contract: ClassVar[dict | None] = None
    version: ClassVar[str] = "1.0.0"
    dependencies: ClassVar[list[str] | None] = None
    dependency_imports: ClassVar[list[str] | None] = None
    parameters_schema: ClassVar[dict | None] = None

    extensions: ClassVar[list[str]] = []
    requires_siblings: ClassVar[list[str]] = []
    excludes_siblings: ClassVar[list[str]] = []

    # If True, the reader consumes compressed input directly and the
    # framework passes the original path. If False, the framework
    # decompresses to a temporary file first.
    handles_compression: ClassVar[bool] = False

    # Declarative Studio import hints. No optional parser is loaded to inspect these.
    # Compression defaults to the framework's gzip/bzip2/xz wrapper for readers
    # that do not handle compression themselves; native readers opt in explicitly.
    file_input: ClassVar[dict | None] = None

    supports_disk_read: ClassVar[bool] = False
    disk_protocol: ClassVar[str | None] = None

    def probe(self, path: Path, **args) -> dict:
        """Inspect metadata only. File bytes are never treated as RAM bytes."""
        return {"file_bytes": Path(path).stat().st_size, "dense_bytes": None}

    def read_to_disk(self, path, dims, labels, writer, chunk_bytes, **args):
        """Write numeric blocks into writer and return Form metadata, without data."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    def matches(self, path: Path) -> bool:
        ext = primary_ext(path)
        if ext not in self.extensions:
            return False
        for sib_ext in self.requires_siblings:
            if not path.with_suffix(sib_ext).exists():
                return False
        for sib_ext in self.excludes_siblings:
            if path.with_suffix(sib_ext).exists():
                return False
        return True

    @abstractmethod
    def read(
        self,
        path: Path,
        dims: list,
        labels: dict,
        **args,
    ) -> Form:
        ...


_COMPRESSION = {".gz", ".bz2", ".xz", ".zst"}


def primary_ext(path: Path) -> str:
    p = Path(path)
    if p.suffix.lower() in _COMPRESSION:
        p = p.with_suffix("")
    return p.suffix.lower()


__all__ = ["Reader", "primary_ext"]

===== src/reader/__init__.py =====
# src/reader/__init__.py
"""Reader router and unified entry point.

Callers go through ``read_source``. It selects the reader by the
original path, decompresses if the reader cannot handle compression,
and dispatches.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import bz2
import gzip
import importlib
import lzma
import os
import pkgutil
import shutil
import tempfile
from contextlib import contextmanager
from pathlib import Path

from src.core import ReaderError
from src.reader import readers as _readers_pkg
from src.reader.base import Reader, primary_ext


_COMPRESSION = {
    ".gz": gzip.open,
    ".bz2": bz2.open,
    ".xz": lzma.open,
}


def _discover() -> list[Reader]:
    instances: list[Reader] = []
    for _, mod_name, is_pkg in pkgutil.iter_modules(_readers_pkg.__path__):
        if is_pkg:
            continue
        mod = importlib.import_module(f"src.reader.readers.{mod_name}")
        for attr in vars(mod).values():
            if (
                isinstance(attr, type)
                and issubclass(attr, Reader)
                and attr is not Reader
                and getattr(attr, "extensions", None)
            ):
                from .schemas import builtin_schema
                if attr.parameters_schema is None:
                    attr.parameters_schema = builtin_schema(attr.__name__)
                instances.append(attr())
    return instances


def _from_packages():
    from biopipe.packages import bundled_entries, load_entry
    return [load_entry(descriptor)() for _, descriptor in bundled_entries("reader")]


READERS: list[Reader] = [] if os.getenv("BIOPIPE_REGISTRY_MAINTENANCE") == "1" else _from_packages()


def pick_reader(path: Path) -> Reader:
    """Return the single reader that claims this path.

    Selection is based on the original path (compression suffix
    included), so a ``.bed.gz`` still routes to the correct reader.
    """
    path = Path(path)
    candidates = [r for r in READERS if r.matches(path)]

    if not candidates:
        ext = primary_ext(path)
        known = sorted({e for r in READERS for e in r.extensions})
        raise ReaderError(
            f"No reader for extension '{ext}' at {path}. "
            f"Known extensions: {known}"
        )
    if len(candidates) > 1:
        names = [type(r).__name__ for r in candidates]
        raise ReaderError(
            f"Multiple readers claim {path}: {names}."
        )
    return candidates[0]


@contextmanager
def _decompressed(path: Path):
    """If ``path`` has a compression suffix and is not already handled
    by the reader, decompress it to a temporary file that keeps the
    original stem and primary extension.
    """
    suffix = path.suffix.lower()
    if suffix not in _COMPRESSION:
        yield path
        return

    real_suffix = path.with_suffix("").suffix
    fd, tmp_name = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=real_suffix)
    os.close(fd)
    tmp_path = Path(tmp_name)

    try:
        opener = _COMPRESSION[suffix]
        with opener(path, "rb") as src, open(tmp_path, "wb") as dst:
            shutil.copyfileobj(src, dst)
        yield tmp_path
    finally:
        tmp_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)


def read_source(
    path: Path,
    dims: list,
    labels: dict,
    **args,
):
    """Read a source file into a Form.

    Handles compression once, at the entry point, so individual
    readers only ever see plain files unless they declare
    ``handles_compression = True``.
    """
    path = Path(path)
    reader = pick_reader(path)
    if reader.parameters_schema is not None:
        from biopipe_sdk.protocol import validate_parameters
        args = validate_parameters(reader.parameters_schema, args)

    if reader.handles_compression:
        form = reader.read(path, dims, labels, **args)
    else:
        with _decompressed(path) as real_path:
            form = reader.read(real_path, dims, labels, **args)
    from src.core.contracts import validate_form, validate_output, source_schema
    validate_form(form, reader.output_contract, f"reader {type(reader).__name__} output")
    validate_output(form, source_schema(reader.output_contract, dims), f"reader {type(reader).__name__} output")
    return form


__all__ = [
    "READERS",
    "pick_reader",
    "read_source",
    "Reader",
    "primary_ext",
]

"""Declarative options for built-in readers, available without optional parsers."""
from copy import deepcopy


def builtin_schema(name):
    encoder = {
        "type": "object",
        "title": "Encoders",
        "description": "Per-field encoder configuration.",
        "additionalProperties": True,
    }
    csv = {
        "header": {
            "type": "boolean",
            "title": "Header row",
            "default": True,
        },
        "sep": {
            "type": "string",
            "title": "Separator",
            "minLength": 1,
        },
        "encoders": encoder,
        "columns": {
            "type": "array",
            "items": {"type": "string"},
            "title": "Columns",
            "description": (
                "Restrict the reader to these header names, in order. "
                "Label indices still refer to the original file."
            ),
        },
        "info_columns": {
            "type": "object",
            "title": "Info columns",
            "description": (
                "Map a header name to an info field. The column is "
                "read as strings into info[field] and declared "
                "parallel to the row dimension."
            ),
            "additionalProperties": {"type": "string"},
        },
    }
    tsv = {
        "header": {
            "type": "boolean",
            "title": "Header row",
            "default": True,
        },
        "encoders": encoder,
        "columns": {
            "type": "array",
            "items": {"type": "string"},
            "title": "Columns",
            "description": (
                "Restrict the reader to these header names, in order. "
                "Label indices still refer to the original file."
            ),
        },
        "info_columns": {
            "type": "object",
            "title": "Info columns",
            "description": (
                "Map a header name to an info field. The column is "
                "read as strings into info[field] and declared "
                "parallel to the row dimension."
            ),
            "additionalProperties": {"type": "string"},
        },
    }
    definitions = {
        "CsvReader": csv,
        "TsvReader": tsv,
        "TxtReader": {
            **csv,
            "reader": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Delegate reader",
                "default": "csv",
            },
        },
        "H5adReader": {
            "layer": {
                "type": ["string", "null"],
                "x-ui-type": "string",
                "title": "Layer",
                "description": "Leave unset to read X.",
            },
        },
        **{
            key: {"encoders": encoder}
            for key in ("BedReader", "GtfReader", "Gff3Reader", "ParquetReader")
        },
    }
    return {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": deepcopy(definitions.get(name, {})),
        "additionalProperties": True,
    }
===== src/reader/disk.py =====
"""Bounded writes into one C-order NPY file; no full-array concatenation/mmap."""
import math
import os
from collections import OrderedDict
from contextlib import contextmanager
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from src.core import ReaderError


class DenseDiskWriter:
    def __init__(self, path, shape, dtype=np.float32, *, block_bytes=4 * 1024**2,
                 budget_bytes=16 * 1024**2, pressure=None, event=None):
        self.shape, self.dtype = tuple(shape), np.dtype(dtype)
        self.path = Path(path)
        self.block_bytes = max(self.dtype.itemsize, (min(block_bytes, budget_bytes) // self.dtype.itemsize) * self.dtype.itemsize)
        self.budget_bytes = max(self.block_bytes, budget_bytes)
        self.blocks = OrderedDict()
        self.pressure, self.event = pressure, event or (lambda *a, **k: None)
        self.peak_bytes = 0
        self.file = open(path, "w+b")
        np.lib.format.write_array_header_2_0(self.file, {
            "descr": np.lib.format.dtype_to_descr(self.dtype),
            "fortran_order": False, "shape": self.shape})
        self.offset = self.file.tell()
        self.file.truncate(self.offset + math.prod(self.shape) * self.dtype.itemsize)

    def _evict(self, reason):
        key, block = self.blocks.popitem(last=False)
        self.file.seek(self.offset + key * self.block_bytes)
        remaining = math.prod(self.shape) * self.dtype.itemsize - key * self.block_bytes
        self.file.write(memoryview(block)[:min(remaining, len(block))])
        self.event("densify_block_spill", reason=reason, block=key, bytes=len(block))

    def _block(self, position):
        key = position // self.block_bytes
        if self.pressure and self.pressure():
            while self.blocks:
                self._evict("rss_pressure")
        if key not in self.blocks:
            while self.blocks and (len(self.blocks) + 1) * self.block_bytes > self.budget_bytes:
                self._evict("buffer_watermark")
            self.file.seek(self.offset + key * self.block_bytes)
            block = bytearray(self.file.read(self.block_bytes))
            block.extend(bytes(self.block_bytes - len(block)))
            self.blocks[key] = block
            self.peak_bytes = max(self.peak_bytes, len(self.blocks) * self.block_bytes)
        self.blocks.move_to_end(key)
        return self.blocks[key], position % self.block_bytes

    def write(self, row, column, values):
        values = np.asarray(values, dtype=self.dtype).reshape(-1)
        if not (0 <= row < self.shape[0] and 0 <= column <= self.shape[1]
                and column + values.size <= self.shape[1]):
            raise ReaderError("Matrix block lies outside declared shape")
        position = (row * self.shape[1] + column) * self.dtype.itemsize
        raw = memoryview(values).cast("B")
        while raw:
            block, offset = self._block(position)
            count = min(len(raw), len(block) - offset)
            block[offset:offset + count] = raw[:count]
            position += count
            raw = raw[count:]

    def add(self, row, column, value):
        if not (0 <= row < self.shape[0] and 0 <= column < self.shape[1]):
            raise ReaderError("Matrix coordinate lies outside declared shape")
        position = (row * self.shape[1] + column) * self.dtype.itemsize
        block, offset = self._block(position)
        cell = np.frombuffer(block, dtype=self.dtype, count=1, offset=offset)
        cell[0] += value

    def close(self, retain=False):
        if self.file.closed:
            return
        try:
            if retain:
                for key, block in self.blocks.items():
                    self.file.seek(self.offset + key * self.block_bytes)
                    remaining = math.prod(self.shape) * self.dtype.itemsize - key * self.block_bytes
                    self.file.write(memoryview(block)[:min(remaining, len(block))])
            else:
                while self.blocks:
                    self._evict("phase_handoff")
            self.file.flush()
            os.fsync(self.file.fileno())
        finally:
            self.file.close()


@contextmanager
def accumulator(target, dtype, chunk_bytes):
    """Sum duplicate coordinates at source precision, then cast in bounded rows."""
    path = target.path.with_name('accumulator.npy')
    work = DenseDiskWriter(path, target.shape, dtype,
                           block_bytes=target.block_bytes, budget_bytes=target.budget_bytes,
                           pressure=target.pressure, event=target.event)
    try:
        yield work
        work.close()
        with path.open('rb') as source:
            np.lib.format.read_magic(source)
            np.lib.format.read_array_header_2_0(source)
            width = max(1, chunk_bytes // max(16, np.dtype(dtype).itemsize * 2))
            for row in range(target.shape[0]):
                for column in range(0, target.shape[1], width):
                    count = min(width, target.shape[1] - column)
                    values = np.fromfile(source, dtype=dtype, count=count)
                    if len(values) != count:
                        raise ReaderError('Truncated source accumulator')
                    target.write(row, column, values)
    finally:
        if not work.file.closed:
            work.file.close()
        path.unlink(missing_ok=True)

===== src/reader/readers/_encoder.py =====
# src/reader/readers/_encoder.py
"""Shared label encoders for tabular readers.

Each encoder exposes:

    name             — short identifier ("labelencoder", ...)
    fit_transform(x) — fit on x, return float32 codes
    inverse_transform(codes) — map codes back to original values
    mapping          — {original: code}
    inverse          — {code: original}
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np

from src.core import ReaderError


class LabelEncoder:
    """Map each unique value to an integer in first-appearance order."""

    name = "labelencoder"

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.mapping: dict = {}
        self.inverse: dict = {}

    def fit_transform(self, values) -> np.ndarray:
        order = list(dict.fromkeys(values))
        self.mapping = {v: i for i, v in enumerate(order)}
        self.inverse = {i: v for v, i in self.mapping.items()}
        return np.asarray(
            [self.mapping[v] for v in values], dtype=np.float32
        )

    def inverse_transform(self, codes) -> list:
        return [self.inverse[int(c)] for c in codes]


_ENCODERS = {
    "labelencoder": LabelEncoder,
}


def get_encoder(name: str):
    if name not in _ENCODERS:
        raise ReaderError(
            f"Unknown encoder '{name}'. "
            f"Available: {sorted(_ENCODERS)}"
        )
    return _ENCODERS[name]()
===== src/reader/readers/_labels.py =====
# src/reader/readers/_labels.py
"""Shared helper for building unique label mappings.

Every reader that builds a label dict from a list of names uses this
helper. It guarantees that each axis position gets a unique key, so
downstream operators can rebuild the mapping without losing entries.
"""

from __future__ import annotations


def unique_labels(names, dim_name: str, source: str) -> dict[str, int]:
    """Return {name: position}, renaming duplicates with a ``#<position>``
    suffix on the second and later occurrences.
    """
    seen: set[str] = set()
    out: dict[str, int] = {}
    n_dup = 0
    for i, raw in enumerate(names):
        key = str(raw)
        if key in seen:
            n_dup += 1
            key = f"{key}#{i}"
        seen.add(key)
        out[key] = i
    if n_dup:
        print(
            f"  [{source}] renamed {n_dup} duplicate label(s) "
            f"in '{dim_name}'"
        )
    return out
===== src/reader/readers/gaf_reader.py =====
# src/reader/readers/gaf_reader.py
"""GO Annotation File (GAF) reader.

Tab-separated, 17 fixed columns, comment lines start with ``!``.

    db                    1  source database
    db_object_id          2  database-specific identifier
    db_object_symbol      3  gene symbol
    qualifier             4  NOT / contributes_to / ...
    go_id                 5  GO term identifier
    db_reference          6  reference
    evidence_code         7  EXP / IDA / ...
    with_from             8  supporting identifiers
    aspect                9  P / F / C
    db_object_name       10  full name
    db_object_synonym    11  pipe-separated synonyms
    db_object_type       12  protein / gene / ...
    taxon                13  NCBI taxonomy id
    date                 14  annotation date
    assigned_by          15  assigning group
    annotation_extension 16  structured extension
    gene_product_form_id 17  isoform identifier

Reference:
    https://geneontology.org/docs/go-annotation-file-gaf-format-2.2/

Layout
------
    dims = [annotation, field]

    ``annotation`` is one row per GAF annotation line.
    ``field`` holds the 16 non-synonym column names above.

``db_object_synonym`` is a pipe-separated multi-valued column. It is
read verbatim into ``info["db_object_synonym"]`` and declared parallel
to the annotation axis. The column is removed from data so it can be
exploded downstream.

Label rules accepted:

    builtin           the standard column names above
    auto              position indices
    {<field_dim>: <k>}  read column <k> as annotation labels
    {name: pos, ...}  explicit mapping

Info fields set:

    missing_code         np.nan
    db_object_synonym    list of strings
    encoders             one encoder per non-numeric column
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import gzip
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from src.core import Form, ReaderError
from src.core.contracts import reader_contract
from src.reader.base import Reader
from src.reader.readers._encoder import get_encoder
from src.reader.readers._labels import unique_labels


_GAF_COLUMNS = [
    "db",
    "db_object_id",
    "db_object_symbol",
    "qualifier",
    "go_id",
    "db_reference",
    "evidence_code",
    "with_from",
    "aspect",
    "db_object_name",
    "db_object_synonym",
    "db_object_type",
    "taxon",
    "date",
    "assigned_by",
    "annotation_extension",
    "gene_product_form_id",
]

_INFO_COLUMNS = {"db_object_synonym"}
_N_COLUMNS = len(_GAF_COLUMNS)


class GafReader(Reader):
    """Reader for GO Annotation Files (.gaf)."""

    output_contract = reader_contract()

    file_input = {
        "dims": ["annotation", "field"],
        "labels": {"annotation": "auto", "field": "builtin"},
    }

    extensions = [".gaf"]
    handles_compression = True
    parameters_schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {},
        "additionalProperties": False,
    }

    def read(self, path: Path, dims: list, labels: dict, **args) -> Form:
        if len(dims) != 2:
            raise ReaderError(
                f"GAF reader requires exactly two dims, got {dims}"
            )
        row_dim, col_dim = dims[0], dims[1]

        if args:
            raise ReaderError(
                f"GAF reader: unknown args {sorted(args)}"
            )

        rows = _load_rows(path)
        if not rows:
            raise ReaderError(f"GAF file has no data lines: {path}")

        # Extract label rules.
        extracted: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}
        cols_to_drop: set[int] = set()

        for dim_name, rule in labels.items():
            if dim_name not in dims:
                raise ReaderError(
                    f"GAF reader: labels entry '{dim_name}' is not "
                    f"one of {dims}"
                )
            if rule in (None, "auto"):
                continue

            if rule == "builtin":
                if dim_name != col_dim:
                    raise ReaderError(
                        f"GAF reader: 'builtin' is only valid for the "
                        f"field dim '{col_dim}', not '{dim_name}'"
                    )
                continue

            if isinstance(rule, dict):
                if dim_name == row_dim and set(rule.keys()) == {col_dim}:
                    k = rule[col_dim]
                    if not 0 <= k < _N_COLUMNS:
                        raise ReaderError(
                            f"GAF reader: labels.{row_dim} column "
                            f"index {k} out of range "
                            f"(GAF has {_N_COLUMNS} columns)"
                        )
                    cols_to_drop.add(k)
                    values = [r[k] for r in rows]
                    extracted[row_dim] = unique_labels(
                        values, row_dim, "gaf"
                    )
                elif dim_name == row_dim:
                    extracted[row_dim] = {
                        str(k): int(v) for k, v in rule.items()
                    }
                elif dim_name == col_dim:
                    extracted[col_dim] = {
                        str(k): int(v) for k, v in rule.items()
                    }
                else:
                    raise ReaderError(
                        f"GAF reader: unsupported label rule for "
                        f"'{dim_name}': {rule!r}"
                    )
                continue

            raise ReaderError(
                f"GAF reader: labels.{dim_name} must be 'builtin', "
                f"'auto', a mapping, or omitted; got {rule!r}"
            )

        # Extract info columns, remove them from data.
        info_extracted: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
        info_col_indices: set[int] = set()
        for idx, name in enumerate(_GAF_COLUMNS):
            if name not in _INFO_COLUMNS:
                continue
            info_extracted[name] = [r[idx] for r in rows]
            info_col_indices.add(idx)

        keep = [
            i for i in range(_N_COLUMNS)
            if i not in cols_to_drop and i not in info_col_indices
        ]
        if not keep:
            raise ReaderError(
                "GAF reader: all columns were consumed as row labels"
            )
        kept_names = [_GAF_COLUMNS[i] for i in keep]

        encoders_used: dict[str, object] = {}
        columns_data: list[np.ndarray] = []
        for idx in keep:
            name = _GAF_COLUMNS[idx]
            raw = [r[idx] for r in rows]
            encoder = get_encoder("labelencoder")
            columns_data.append(encoder.fit_transform(raw))
            encoders_used[name] = encoder

        data = np.stack(columns_data, axis=1).astype(np.float32)

        final: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}

        if row_dim in extracted:
            final[row_dim] = extracted[row_dim]
        else:
            final[row_dim] = {str(i): i for i in range(data.shape[0])}

        if col_dim in extracted:
            final[col_dim] = extracted[col_dim]
        else:
            rule = labels.get(col_dim)
            if rule == "builtin":
                final[col_dim] = unique_labels(
                    kept_names, col_dim, "gaf"
                )
            else:
                final[col_dim] = {
                    str(i): i for i in range(data.shape[1])
                }

        info: dict = {"missing_code": np.nan}
        info.update(info_extracted)
        if encoders_used:
            info["encoders"] = {col_dim: encoders_used}

        axis_info: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
        if info_extracted:
            axis_info[row_dim] = sorted(info_extracted.keys())

        return Form(
            data=data,
            dims=list(dims),
            labels=final,
            info=info,
            axis_info=axis_info,
        )


def _load_rows(path: Path) -> list[list[str]]:
    opener = gzip.open if path.suffix == ".gz" else open
    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    with opener(path, "rt", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        for line in fh:
            line = line.rstrip("\n")
            if not line or line.startswith("!"):
                continue
            parts = line.split("\t")
            if len(parts) != _N_COLUMNS:
                raise ReaderError(
                    f"GAF line has {len(parts)} columns, expected "
                    f"{_N_COLUMNS}: {line[:80]!r}"
                )
            rows.append(parts)
    return rows
===== src/reader/readers/tsv_reader.py =====
# src/reader/readers/tsv_reader.py
"""TSV reader.

Tab-separated two-dimensional tables. The first line is treated as
a header by default; set ``header: false`` in args to read every
line as data.

Compression (.gz, .bz2, .xz) is handled by pandas directly; this
reader receives the original path.

Label rules accepted:

    builtin           header row (valid only for the column dim)
    auto              position indices "0", "1", ...
    {<col_dim>: <k>}  read column <k>; its values become the row
                      labels and the column is removed from data.
                      ``k`` is a position in the original file,
                      independent of ``columns``.
    {name: pos, ...}  explicit mapping

A missing dim entry defaults to ``auto``. Duplicate labels are
disambiguated by appending ``#<position>``.

Column selection
----------------
``columns`` restricts the reader to a subset of header names, in the
order given. ``labels`` indices still refer to the original file.

Info columns
------------
``info_columns`` maps a header name to an info field name. The column
is read verbatim as strings into ``info[field]`` and declared parallel
to the row dimension in ``axis_info``. The column is removed from
data.

Non-numeric columns are encoded. The encoder instance used for each
column is recorded in ``info["encoders"]`` as
``{col_dim: {label: encoder}}``.

Info fields set:

    missing_code      np.nan
    encoders          present only when a column was encoded
    <info_columns>    one entry per declared info column
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from src.core import Form, ReaderError
from src.reader.base import Reader
from src.core.contracts import reader_contract
from src.reader.readers._encoder import get_encoder
from src.reader.readers._labels import unique_labels


class TsvReader(Reader):
    """Reader for .tsv files."""

    output_contract = reader_contract()

    file_input = {
        "dims": ["sample", "feature"],
        "labels": {"sample": "auto", "feature": "builtin"},
        "compression": [".gz", ".bz2", ".xz"],
    }

    extensions = [".tsv"]
    handles_compression = True
    parameters_schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "header": {
                "type": "boolean",
                "title": "Header row",
                "default": True,
            },
            "encoders": {
                "type": "object",
                "title": "Encoders",
                "additionalProperties": True,
            },
            "columns": {
                "type": "array",
                "items": {"type": "string"},
                "title": "Columns",
            },
            "info_columns": {
                "type": "object",
                "title": "Info columns",
                "additionalProperties": {"type": "string"},
            },
        },
        "additionalProperties": True,
    }

    def read(self, path: Path, dims: list, labels: dict, **args) -> Form:
        if len(dims) != 2:
            raise ReaderError(
                f"TSV reader requires exactly two dims, got {dims}"
            )
        row_dim, col_dim = dims[0], dims[1]

        has_header = bool(args.get("header", True))

        encoders_cfg = args.get("encoders", {})
        if not isinstance(encoders_cfg, dict):
            raise ReaderError("TSV reader: 'encoders' must be a mapping")

        columns_cfg = args.get("columns")
        if columns_cfg is not None:
            if (not isinstance(columns_cfg, list)
                    or not columns_cfg
                    or any(not isinstance(c, str) or not c
                           for c in columns_cfg)):
                raise ReaderError(
                    "TSV reader: 'columns' must be a non-empty list of "
                    "header names"
                )
            if len(set(columns_cfg)) != len(columns_cfg):
                raise ReaderError(
                    "TSV reader: 'columns' contains duplicates"
                )

        info_columns_cfg = args.get("info_columns", {})
        if not isinstance(info_columns_cfg, dict):
            raise ReaderError(
                "TSV reader: 'info_columns' must be a mapping of "
                "column name -> info field name"
            )
        for col_name, info_key in info_columns_cfg.items():
            if not isinstance(col_name, str) or not col_name:
                raise ReaderError(
                    f"TSV reader: info_columns key must be a non-empty "
                    f"string, got {col_name!r}"
                )
            if not isinstance(info_key, str) or not info_key:
                raise ReaderError(
                    f"TSV reader: info_columns['{col_name}'] must be a "
                    f"non-empty string, got {info_key!r}"
                )

        df = pd.read_csv(path, header=0 if has_header else None, sep="\t")
        if df.shape[0] == 0:
            raise ReaderError(
                f"TSV reader: file has zero data rows: {path}"
            )

        if has_header:
            col_names = [str(c) for c in df.columns]
        else:
            col_names = [str(i) for i in range(df.shape[1])]
        df.columns = col_names

        if len(set(col_names)) != len(col_names):
            raise ReaderError(
                "TSV reader: header contains duplicate column names; "
                "rename them before reading"
            )

        if columns_cfg is not None:
            missing = [c for c in columns_cfg if c not in col_names]
            if missing:
                raise ReaderError(
                    f"TSV reader: requested columns not in header: "
                    f"{missing}. Available: {col_names}"
                )
        for col_name in info_columns_cfg:
            if col_name not in col_names:
                raise ReaderError(
                    f"TSV reader: info_columns key '{col_name}' not in "
                    f"header. Available: {col_names}"
                )

        extracted: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}
        label_cols_to_drop: set[int] = set()

        for dim_name, rule in labels.items():
            if dim_name not in dims:
                raise ReaderError(
                    f"TSV reader: labels entry '{dim_name}' is not "
                    f"one of {dims}"
                )
            if rule in (None, "auto"):
                continue

            if rule == "builtin":
                if dim_name != col_dim:
                    raise ReaderError(
                        f"TSV reader: 'builtin' is only valid for the "
                        f"column dim '{col_dim}', not '{dim_name}'"
                    )
                continue

            if isinstance(rule, dict):
                if dim_name == row_dim and set(rule.keys()) == {col_dim}:
                    k = rule[col_dim]
                    if not 0 <= k < df.shape[1]:
                        raise ReaderError(
                            f"TSV reader: labels.{row_dim} column "
                            f"index {k} out of range "
                            f"(file has {df.shape[1]} columns)"
                        )
                    label_cols_to_drop.add(k)
                    values = df.iloc[:, k].astype(str).tolist()
                    extracted[row_dim] = unique_labels(
                        values, row_dim, "tsv"
                    )
                elif dim_name == row_dim:
                    extracted[row_dim] = {
                        str(k): int(v) for k, v in rule.items()
                    }
                elif dim_name == col_dim:
                    extracted[col_dim] = {
                        str(k): int(v) for k, v in rule.items()
                    }
                else:
                    raise ReaderError(
                        f"TSV reader: unsupported label rule for "
                        f"'{dim_name}': {rule!r}"
                    )
                continue

            raise ReaderError(
                f"TSV reader: labels.{dim_name} must be 'builtin', "
                f"'auto', a mapping, or omitted; got {rule!r}"
            )

        info_extracted: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
        info_cols_to_drop: set[int] = set()
        for col_name, info_key in info_columns_cfg.items():
            idx = col_names.index(col_name)
            if idx in label_cols_to_drop:
                raise ReaderError(
                    f"TSV reader: column '{col_name}' is already "
                    f"consumed as a label column; cannot also declare "
                    f"it as an info column"
                )
            if info_key in info_extracted:
                raise ReaderError(
                    f"TSV reader: info field '{info_key}' is declared "
                    f"twice in info_columns"
                )
            info_extracted[info_key] = (
                df.iloc[:, idx].fillna("").astype(str).tolist()
            )
            info_cols_to_drop.add(idx)

        if columns_cfg is not None:
            selected_indices = [col_names.index(c) for c in columns_cfg]
            df = df.iloc[:, selected_indices]
            new_col_names = [col_names[i] for i in selected_indices]
            old_to_new = {
                old: new for new, old in enumerate(selected_indices)
            }
            label_cols_to_drop = {
                old_to_new[i] for i in label_cols_to_drop
                if i in old_to_new
            }
            info_cols_to_drop = {
                old_to_new[i] for i in info_cols_to_drop
                if i in old_to_new
            }
            col_names = new_col_names

        keep = [
            i for i in range(df.shape[1])
            if i not in label_cols_to_drop
            and i not in info_cols_to_drop
        ]
        if not keep:
            raise ReaderError(
                "TSV reader: no data columns remain after label and "
                "info column extraction"
            )
        kept_names = [col_names[i] for i in keep]

        encoders_used: dict[str, object] = {}
        cols_data = []
        for old_idx in keep:
            col = df.iloc[:, old_idx]
            numeric = True
            try:
                values = col.to_numpy(dtype=np.float32)
            except (ValueError, TypeError):
                numeric = False

            if numeric:
                cols_data.append(values)
                continue

            name = col_names[old_idx]
            enc_name = (
                encoders_cfg.get(name)
                or encoders_cfg.get("all")
                or "labelencoder"
            )
            encoder = get_encoder(enc_name)
            cols_data.append(encoder.fit_transform(col.tolist()))
            encoders_used[name] = encoder

        data = np.stack(cols_data, axis=1).astype(np.float32)

        final: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}

        if row_dim in extracted:
            final[row_dim] = extracted[row_dim]
        else:
            final[row_dim] = {str(i): i for i in range(data.shape[0])}

        if col_dim in extracted:
            final[col_dim] = extracted[col_dim]
        else:
            rule = labels.get(col_dim)
            if rule == "builtin":
                final[col_dim] = unique_labels(
                    kept_names, col_dim, "tsv"
                )
            else:
                final[col_dim] = {
                    str(i): i for i in range(data.shape[1])
                }

        info: dict = {"missing_code": np.nan}
        info.update(info_extracted)
        if encoders_used:
            info["encoders"] = {col_dim: encoders_used}

        axis_info: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
        if info_extracted:
            axis_info[row_dim] = list(info_extracted.keys())

        return Form(
            data=data,
            dims=list(dims),
            labels=final,
            info=info,
            axis_info=axis_info,
        )
===== src/reader/readers/csv_reader.py =====
# src/reader/readers/csv_reader.py
"""CSV reader.

Comma-separated (or custom-separator) two-dimensional tables. The
first line is treated as a header by default; set ``header: false``
in args to read every line as data.

Compression (.gz, .bz2, .xz) is handled by pandas directly; this
reader receives the original path.

Label rules accepted:

    builtin           header row (valid only for the column dim)
    auto              position indices "0", "1", ...
    {<col_dim>: <k>}  read column <k>; its values become the row
                      labels and the column is removed from data.
                      ``k`` is a position in the original file,
                      independent of ``columns``.
    {name: pos, ...}  explicit mapping

A missing dim entry defaults to ``auto``. Duplicate labels are
disambiguated by appending ``#<position>``.

Column selection
----------------
``columns`` restricts the reader to a subset of header names, in the
order given. ``labels`` indices still refer to the original file.

Info columns
------------
``info_columns`` maps a header name to an info field name. The column
is read verbatim as strings into ``info[field]`` and declared parallel
to the row dimension in ``axis_info``. The column is removed from
data.

Non-numeric columns are encoded. The encoder instance used for each
column is recorded in ``info["encoders"]`` as
``{col_dim: {label: encoder}}``.

Info fields set:

    missing_code      np.nan
    encoders          present only when a column was encoded
    <info_columns>    one entry per declared info column
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from src.core import Form, ReaderError
from src.reader.base import Reader
from src.core.contracts import reader_contract
from src.reader.readers._encoder import get_encoder
from src.reader.readers._labels import unique_labels


class CsvReader(Reader):
    """Reader for .csv files."""

    output_contract = reader_contract()

    file_input = {
        "dims": ["sample", "feature"],
        "labels": {"sample": "auto", "feature": "builtin"},
        "compression": [".gz", ".bz2", ".xz"],
    }

    extensions = [".csv"]
    handles_compression = True
    parameters_schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "header": {
                "type": "boolean",
                "title": "Header row",
                "default": True,
            },
            "sep": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Separator",
                "minLength": 1,
            },
            "encoders": {
                "type": "object",
                "title": "Encoders",
                "additionalProperties": True,
            },
            "columns": {
                "type": "array",
                "items": {"type": "string"},
                "title": "Columns",
            },
            "info_columns": {
                "type": "object",
                "title": "Info columns",
                "additionalProperties": {"type": "string"},
            },
        },
        "additionalProperties": True,
    }

    def read(self, path: Path, dims: list, labels: dict, **args) -> Form:
        if len(dims) != 2:
            raise ReaderError(
                f"CSV reader requires exactly two dims, got {dims}"
            )
        row_dim, col_dim = dims[0], dims[1]

        has_header = bool(args.get("header", True))

        sep = args.get("sep", ",")
        if not isinstance(sep, str) or not sep:
            raise ReaderError(
                f"CSV reader: 'sep' must be a non-empty string, got {sep!r}"
            )

        encoders_cfg = args.get("encoders", {})
        if not isinstance(encoders_cfg, dict):
            raise ReaderError("CSV reader: 'encoders' must be a mapping")

        columns_cfg = args.get("columns")
        if columns_cfg is not None:
            if (not isinstance(columns_cfg, list)
                    or not columns_cfg
                    or any(not isinstance(c, str) or not c
                           for c in columns_cfg)):
                raise ReaderError(
                    "CSV reader: 'columns' must be a non-empty list of "
                    "header names"
                )
            if len(set(columns_cfg)) != len(columns_cfg):
                raise ReaderError(
                    "CSV reader: 'columns' contains duplicates"
                )

        info_columns_cfg = args.get("info_columns", {})
        if not isinstance(info_columns_cfg, dict):
            raise ReaderError(
                "CSV reader: 'info_columns' must be a mapping of "
                "column name -> info field name"
            )
        for col_name, info_key in info_columns_cfg.items():
            if not isinstance(col_name, str) or not col_name:
                raise ReaderError(
