                    f"CSV reader: info_columns key must be a non-empty "
                    f"string, got {col_name!r}"
                )
            if not isinstance(info_key, str) or not info_key:
                raise ReaderError(
                    f"CSV reader: info_columns['{col_name}'] must be a "
                    f"non-empty string, got {info_key!r}"
                )

        df = pd.read_csv(path, header=0 if has_header else None, sep=sep)
        if df.shape[0] == 0:
            raise ReaderError(
                f"CSV reader: file has zero data rows: {path}"
            )

        if has_header:
            col_names = [str(c) for c in df.columns]
        else:
            col_names = [str(i) for i in range(df.shape[1])]
        df.columns = col_names

        if len(set(col_names)) != len(col_names):
            raise ReaderError(
                "CSV reader: header contains duplicate column names; "
                "rename them before reading"
            )

        if columns_cfg is not None:
            missing = [c for c in columns_cfg if c not in col_names]
            if missing:
                raise ReaderError(
                    f"CSV reader: requested columns not in header: "
                    f"{missing}. Available: {col_names}"
                )
        for col_name in info_columns_cfg:
            if col_name not in col_names:
                raise ReaderError(
                    f"CSV reader: info_columns key '{col_name}' not in "
                    f"header. Available: {col_names}"
                )

        extracted: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}
        label_cols_to_drop: set[int] = set()

        for dim_name, rule in labels.items():
            if dim_name not in dims:
                raise ReaderError(
                    f"CSV reader: labels entry '{dim_name}' is not "
                    f"one of {dims}"
                )
            if rule in (None, "auto"):
                continue

            if rule == "builtin":
                if dim_name != col_dim:
                    raise ReaderError(
                        f"CSV reader: 'builtin' is only valid for the "
                        f"column dim '{col_dim}', not '{dim_name}'"
                    )
                continue

            if isinstance(rule, dict):
                if dim_name == row_dim and set(rule.keys()) == {col_dim}:
                    k = rule[col_dim]
                    if not 0 <= k < df.shape[1]:
                        raise ReaderError(
                            f"CSV reader: labels.{row_dim} column "
                            f"index {k} out of range "
                            f"(file has {df.shape[1]} columns)"
                        )
                    label_cols_to_drop.add(k)
                    values = df.iloc[:, k].astype(str).tolist()
                    extracted[row_dim] = unique_labels(
                        values, row_dim, "csv"
                    )
                elif dim_name == row_dim:
                    extracted[row_dim] = {
                        str(k): int(v) for k, v in rule.items()
                    }
                elif dim_name == col_dim:
                    extracted[col_dim] = {
                        str(k): int(v) for k, v in rule.items()
                    }
                else:
                    raise ReaderError(
                        f"CSV reader: unsupported label rule for "
                        f"'{dim_name}': {rule!r}"
                    )
                continue

            raise ReaderError(
                f"CSV reader: labels.{dim_name} must be 'builtin', "
                f"'auto', a mapping, or omitted; got {rule!r}"
            )

        info_extracted: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
        info_cols_to_drop: set[int] = set()
        for col_name, info_key in info_columns_cfg.items():
            idx = col_names.index(col_name)
            if idx in label_cols_to_drop:
                raise ReaderError(
                    f"CSV reader: column '{col_name}' is already "
                    f"consumed as a label column; cannot also declare "
                    f"it as an info column"
                )
            if info_key in info_extracted:
                raise ReaderError(
                    f"CSV reader: info field '{info_key}' is declared "
                    f"twice in info_columns"
                )
            info_extracted[info_key] = (
                df.iloc[:, idx].fillna("").astype(str).tolist()
            )
            info_cols_to_drop.add(idx)

        if columns_cfg is not None:
            selected_indices = [col_names.index(c) for c in columns_cfg]
            df = df.iloc[:, selected_indices]
            new_col_names = [col_names[i] for i in selected_indices]
            old_to_new = {
                old: new for new, old in enumerate(selected_indices)
            }
            label_cols_to_drop = {
                old_to_new[i] for i in label_cols_to_drop
                if i in old_to_new
            }
            info_cols_to_drop = {
                old_to_new[i] for i in info_cols_to_drop
                if i in old_to_new
            }
            col_names = new_col_names

        keep = [
            i for i in range(df.shape[1])
            if i not in label_cols_to_drop
            and i not in info_cols_to_drop
        ]
        if not keep:
            raise ReaderError(
                "CSV reader: no data columns remain after label and "
                "info column extraction"
            )
        kept_names = [col_names[i] for i in keep]

        encoders_used: dict[str, object] = {}
        cols_data = []
        for old_idx in keep:
            col = df.iloc[:, old_idx]
            numeric = True
            try:
                values = col.to_numpy(dtype=np.float32)
            except (ValueError, TypeError):
                numeric = False

            if numeric:
                cols_data.append(values)
                continue

            name = col_names[old_idx]
            enc_name = (
                encoders_cfg.get(name)
                or encoders_cfg.get("all")
                or "labelencoder"
            )
            encoder = get_encoder(enc_name)
            cols_data.append(encoder.fit_transform(col.tolist()))
            encoders_used[name] = encoder

        data = np.stack(cols_data, axis=1).astype(np.float32)

        final: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}

        if row_dim in extracted:
            final[row_dim] = extracted[row_dim]
        else:
            final[row_dim] = {str(i): i for i in range(data.shape[0])}

        if col_dim in extracted:
            final[col_dim] = extracted[col_dim]
        else:
            rule = labels.get(col_dim)
            if rule == "builtin":
                final[col_dim] = unique_labels(
                    kept_names, col_dim, "csv"
                )
            else:
                final[col_dim] = {
                    str(i): i for i in range(data.shape[1])
                }

        info: dict = {"missing_code": np.nan}
        info.update(info_extracted)
        if encoders_used:
            info["encoders"] = {col_dim: encoders_used}

        axis_info: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
        if info_extracted:
            axis_info[row_dim] = list(info_extracted.keys())

        return Form(
            data=data,
            dims=list(dims),
            labels=final,
            info=info,
            axis_info=axis_info,
        )
===== src/reader/readers/txt_reader.py =====
# src/reader/readers/txt_reader.py
"""Generic text reader that delegates to another reader.

Handles .txt and .tab extensions that have no dedicated reader.
Parsing is delegated to another registered reader, selected by name.

Params:
    reader : name of the reader to delegate to, matched against the
             class name with "Reader" stripped and lowercased.
             Examples: "csv" -> CsvReader, "bed" -> BedReader,
             "gtf" -> GtfReader. Default "csv".
    All other args are forwarded to the delegated reader.

When the delegate is CsvReader and ``sep`` is not given, a tab
separator is assumed for .txt and .tab files.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from src.core import Form, ReaderError
from src.reader.base import Reader
from src.core.contracts import reader_contract, validate_form, validate_output, source_schema


class TxtReader(Reader):
    """Read generic text files by delegating to another reader."""

    # The delegate owns dimensionality and format-specific metadata guarantees.
    output_contract = {**reader_contract(ndim=None), "info_open": True}

    file_input = {'dims': ['sample', 'feature'], 'labels': {'sample': 'auto', 'feature': 'builtin'}}

    extensions = [".txt", ".tab"]
    handles_compression = False

    def read(self, path: Path, dims: list, labels: dict, **args) -> Form:
        reader_name = args.pop("reader", "csv")
        if not isinstance(reader_name, str):
            raise ReaderError(
                f"TxtReader: 'reader' must be a string, "
                f"got {type(reader_name).__name__}"
            )

        delegate = _find_reader(reader_name, exclude=self)

        # Sensible default separator for the CSV delegate.
        if type(delegate).__name__ == "CsvReader" and "sep" not in args:
            ext = path.suffix.lower()
            if ext in (".tab", ".txt"):
                args["sep"] = "\t"

        form = delegate.read(path, dims, labels, **args)
        validate_form(form, delegate.output_contract, f"TxtReader/{type(delegate).__name__} output")
        validate_output(form, source_schema(delegate.output_contract, dims), f"TxtReader/{type(delegate).__name__} output")
        return form


def _find_reader(name: str, exclude: Reader | None = None) -> Reader:
    from src.reader import READERS

    key = name.lower().strip()
    matches: list[Reader] = []
    for r in READERS:
        if r is exclude:
            continue
        cls_name = type(r).__name__
        stem = cls_name[:-len("Reader")] if cls_name.endswith("Reader") \
            else cls_name
        if stem.lower() == key:
            matches.append(r)

    if not matches:
        available = sorted({
            (lambda c: c[:-len("Reader")] if c.endswith("Reader") else c)(
                type(r).__name__
            ).lower()
            for r in READERS
            if r is not exclude
        })
        raise ReaderError(
            f"TxtReader: no reader named '{name}'. "
            f"Available: {available}"
        )
    if len(matches) > 1:
        raise ReaderError(
            f"TxtReader: ambiguous reader name '{name}': "
            f"{[type(r).__name__ for r in matches]}"
        )
    return matches[0]
===== src/reader/readers/gff3_reader.py =====
# src/reader/readers/gff3_reader.py
"""GFF3 (Generic Feature Format version 3) reader.

A 9-column tab-delimited text file. No header. Lines starting with
``#`` are skipped (both ``##`` directives and ``#`` comments).
Column names follow the GFF3 specification:

    0  seqid       chromosome or scaffold name
    1  source      program or database
    2  type        feature type (gene, mRNA, exon, CDS, ...)
    3  start       1-based inclusive start
    4  end         1-based inclusive end
    5  score       floating point or '.'
    6  strand      '+', '-', or '.'
    7  phase       '0', '1', '2', or '.'
    8  attributes  semicolon-separated key=value pairs:
                   ``ID=gene1;Name=DDX11;biotype=protein_coding``

Compression is handled by the framework; this reader sees plain
files only.

Label rules accepted:

    builtin           the standard GFF3 column names above
    auto              position indices "0", "1", ...
    {<other_dim>: <k>}  read column <k> as the other dim's labels
                        (removed from data)
    {name: pos, ...}  explicit mapping

A missing dim entry defaults to ``auto``. Duplicate labels are
disambiguated by appending ``#<position>``.

Non-numeric columns are encoded. The encoder instance used for each
column is recorded in ``info["encoders"]``.

Reference
---------
GFF3 specification:
https://github.com/The-Sequence-Ontology/Specifications/blob/master/gff3.md

Info fields set:

    missing_code      np.nan
    encoders          present only when a column was encoded
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from src.core import Form, ReaderError
from src.reader.base import Reader
from src.core.contracts import reader_contract
from src.reader.readers._encoder import get_encoder
from src.reader.readers._labels import unique_labels


_GFF3_COLUMNS = [
    "seqid", "source", "type", "start", "end",
    "score", "strand", "phase", "attributes",
]


class Gff3Reader(Reader):
    """Reader for .gff / .gff3 files."""

    output_contract = reader_contract()

    file_input = {'dims': ['record', 'field'], 'labels': {'record': 'auto', 'field': 'builtin'}}

    extensions = [".gff", ".gff3"]
    handles_compression = False

    def read(self, path: Path, dims: list, labels: dict, **args) -> Form:
        if len(dims) != 2:
            raise ReaderError(
                f"GFF3 reader requires exactly two dims, got {dims}"
            )
        row_dim, col_dim = dims[0], dims[1]

        encoders_cfg = args.get("encoders", {})
        rows = _load_rows(path)

        if not rows:
            raise ReaderError(f"GFF3 file has no data lines: {path}")
        ncols = len(rows[0])
        if ncols != 9:
            raise ReaderError(
                f"GFF3 requires exactly 9 columns, got {ncols}"
            )
        if any(len(r) != 9 for r in rows):
            raise ReaderError(
                f"GFF3 has inconsistent column counts: {path}"
            )

        extracted: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}
        cols_to_drop: set[int] = set()

        for dim_name, rule in labels.items():
            if dim_name not in dims:
                raise ReaderError(
                    f"GFF3 reader: labels entry '{dim_name}' is not "
                    f"one of {dims}"
                )
            if rule in (None, "auto"):
                continue

            if rule == "builtin":
                if dim_name != col_dim:
                    raise ReaderError(
                        f"GFF3 reader: 'builtin' is only valid for the "
                        f"column dim '{col_dim}', not '{dim_name}'"
                    )
                continue

            if isinstance(rule, dict):
                if dim_name == row_dim and set(rule.keys()) == {col_dim}:
                    k = rule[col_dim]
                    if not 0 <= k < 9:
                        raise ReaderError(
                            f"GFF3 reader: labels.{row_dim} column "
                            f"index {k} out of range (0-8)"
                        )
                    cols_to_drop.add(k)
                    values = [r[k] for r in rows]
                    extracted[row_dim] = unique_labels(
                        values, row_dim, "gff3"
                    )
                elif dim_name == row_dim:
                    extracted[row_dim] = {
                        str(k): int(v) for k, v in rule.items()
                    }
                elif dim_name == col_dim:
                    extracted[col_dim] = {
                        str(k): int(v) for k, v in rule.items()
                    }
                else:
                    raise ReaderError(
                        f"GFF3 reader: unsupported label rule for "
                        f"'{dim_name}': {rule!r}"
                    )
                continue

            raise ReaderError(
                f"GFF3 reader: labels.{dim_name} must be 'builtin', "
                f"'auto', a mapping, or omitted; got {rule!r}"
            )

        keep = [i for i in range(9) if i not in cols_to_drop]
        kept_names = [_GFF3_COLUMNS[i] for i in keep]

        encoders_used: dict[str, object] = {}
        cols_data = []
        for old_idx in keep:
            raw = [r[old_idx] for r in rows]
            numeric = True
            try:
                values = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float32)
            except (ValueError, TypeError):
                numeric = False

            if numeric:
                cols_data.append(values)
                continue

            name = _GFF3_COLUMNS[old_idx]
            enc_name = (
                encoders_cfg.get(name)
                or encoders_cfg.get("all")
                or "labelencoder"
            )
            encoder = get_encoder(enc_name)
            cols_data.append(encoder.fit_transform(raw))
            encoders_used[name] = encoder

        data = np.stack(cols_data, axis=1).astype(np.float32)

        final: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}

        if row_dim in extracted:
            final[row_dim] = extracted[row_dim]
        else:
            final[row_dim] = {
                str(i): i for i in range(data.shape[0])
            }

        if col_dim in extracted:
            final[col_dim] = extracted[col_dim]
        else:
            rule = labels.get(col_dim)
            if rule == "builtin":
                final[col_dim] = unique_labels(
                    kept_names, col_dim, "gff3"
                )
            else:
                final[col_dim] = {
                    str(i): i for i in range(data.shape[1])
                }

        info: dict = {"missing_code": np.nan}
        if encoders_used:
            info["encoders"] = {col_dim: encoders_used}

        return Form(
            data=data,
            dims=list(dims),
            labels=final,
            info=info,
        )


def _load_rows(path: Path) -> list[list[str]]:
    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        for line in fh:
            line = line.rstrip("\n")
            if not line or line.startswith("#"):
                continue
            rows.append(line.split("\t"))
    return rows
===== src/task/step/base.py =====
# src/task/step/base.py
"""Step abstract base class."""

from __future__ import annotations

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from typing import ClassVar

from src.core import Form
from src.task.context import Context


class Step(ABC):
    """Abstract step.

    Subclasses declare:

    * ``name`` — matched against the manifest's ``name`` field.
    * ``inputs_arity`` — number of input forms, or ``"*"`` for any.
    * ``outputs`` — context keys the step registers on exit.

    And implement ``run``. They may also implement ``export`` to
    return a JSON-serializable dict of the step's full results.
    """

    name: ClassVar[str] = "unnamed"
    # Optional presentation metadata; execution kind is inferred from the base.
    category: ClassVar[str | None] = None
    icon: ClassVar[str | None] = None
    dependencies: ClassVar[list[str] | None] = None
    dependency_imports: ClassVar[list[str] | None] = None
    inputs_arity: ClassVar[int | str] = 0
    outputs: ClassVar[list[str]] = []
    input_contracts: ClassVar[list[dict] | None] = None
    output_contract: ClassVar[dict | None] = None
    output_parameter: ClassVar[str | None] = None
    supports_streaming: ClassVar[bool] = False
    stream_protocol: ClassVar[str | None] = None
    stream_buffer_multiplier: ClassVar[int] = 4
    stream_fixed_bytes: ClassVar[int] = 0
    version: ClassVar[str] = "1.0.0"
    parameters_schema: ClassVar[dict | None] = None

    @classmethod
    def estimate_memory(cls, inputs, params, profiles):
        """Return (temporary workspace bytes, output bytes); not a hard bound.

        Existing operators use their established estimates. Plugins can override
        this hook without editing the planner and without copying input arrays.
        """
        from src.task.memory.planner import full_estimate
        resolved = dict(params)
        if cls.output_parameter or cls.outputs:
            resolved.setdefault("output", cls.name)
        return full_estimate(cls.name, inputs, resolved, profiles)

    @abstractmethod
    def run(self, ctx: Context, inputs: list[Form]) -> None:
        """Execute the step."""

    def export(self, ctx: Context) -> dict:
        """Return a JSON-serializable summary of this step's results.

        Called by the runner right after run(). The default returns
        an empty dict. Subclasses return everything a user would want
        to see. Do not truncate.
        """
        return {}

    def run_stream(self, ctx: Context, stream: dict) -> None:
        """Optional equivalent chunk mode; defaults to unsupported."""
        raise NotImplementedError(f"{self.name} has no streaming contract")

===== src/task/step/__init__.py =====
# src/task/step/__init__.py
"""Step router.

Loads the bundled package index into a name -> class map. Source scanning
is retained solely for the package-index maintenance command.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib
import os
import pkgutil

from src.core import StepError
from src.task.step import steps as _steps_pkg
from src.task.step.base import Step


def _discover() -> dict[str, type[Step]]:
    registry: dict[str, type[Step]] = {}
    for _, mod_name, is_pkg in pkgutil.iter_modules(_steps_pkg.__path__):
        if is_pkg:
            continue
        mod = importlib.import_module(f"src.task.step.steps.{mod_name}")
        for attr in vars(mod).values():
            if (
                isinstance(attr, type)
                and issubclass(attr, Step)
                and attr is not Step
                and attr.name != "unnamed"
            ):
                if attr.name in registry and registry[attr.name] is not attr:
                    raise StepError(
                        f"Duplicate step name '{attr.name}' "
                        f"in module '{mod_name}'"
                    )
                registry[attr.name] = attr
    return registry


def _from_packages():
    from biopipe.packages import bundled_entries, load_entry
    return {descriptor["id"]: load_entry(descriptor)
            for _, descriptor in bundled_entries("operator")}


STEPS: dict[str, type[Step]] = {} if os.getenv("BIOPIPE_REGISTRY_MAINTENANCE") == "1" else _from_packages()


__all__ = ["STEPS", "Step"]

===== src/task/step/steps/__init__.py =====
# src/task/step/steps/__init__.py
"""Step modules.

Each module here defines one or more Step subclasses with a
non-default ``name``. The router in ``src/task/step/__init__.py``
scans this package.

Adding a new step: drop a new module with a Step subclass in this
directory. No registry to edit.
"""
===== src/task/step/steps/_axis_contracts.py =====
"""SDK declarations for the upstream axis and single-cell building blocks.

These kernels use full-memory admission. Only Readers and operators explicitly
advertising a streaming protocol may run in chunks.
"""
from src.core.contracts import param, port
from ._microbiome import DIM, enum, estimate, field, projected, schema, strings

STATISTICS = ["sum", "mean", "std", "var", "min", "max", "median", "nonzero", "count"]
SCHEMAS = {
    "bin": schema({"dim": DIM, "bins": field("integer", minimum=1, default=10),
        "method": enum(["quantile", "uniform"], "quantile"), "value_label": DIM,
        "stat": enum(["mean", "median", "min", "max", "first"], "mean")}, ["dim"]),
    "cluster_axis": schema({"dim": DIM, "method": enum(["kmeans", "hierarchical", "louvain"]),
        "n_clusters": field("integer", minimum=1), "n_neighbors": field("integer", minimum=1, default=15),
        "resolution": field("number", exclusiveMinimum=0, default=1.0), "random_state": field("integer", default=0)}, ["dim", "method"]),
    "filter_by": schema({"dim": DIM, "rules": field("array", minItems=1, items={
        **schema({"field": DIM, "min": field("number"), "max": field("number")}, ["field"]),
        "anyOf": [{"required": ["min"]}, {"required": ["max"]}]})}, ["dim", "rules"]),
    "rank_axis": {**schema({"dim": DIM, "by": enum(["variance", "std", "mean", "sum", "nonzero", "abs_mean", "max"]),
        "top_n": field("integer", minimum=1), "min_value": field("number"),
        "keep_ties": field("boolean", default=False), "descending": field("boolean", default=True)}, ["dim", "by"]),
        "anyOf": [{"required": ["top_n"]}, {"required": ["min_value"]}]},
    "reduce_axis": schema({"dim": DIM, "stats": {"oneOf": [enum(STATISTICS),
        strings(minItems=1, uniqueItems=True, items=enum(STATISTICS))]}}, ["dim", "stats"]),
    "dotplot": schema({"feature_dim": DIM, "group_dim": DIM, "features": strings(minItems=1, uniqueItems=True),
        "group_label": DIM, "groups": strings(minItems=1, uniqueItems=True),
        "statistic": enum(["mean", "median", "sum"], "mean"), "proportion_mode": enum(["nonzero", "threshold"], "nonzero"),
        "threshold": field("number", default=0), "filename": DIM, "title": field("string"), "xlabel": field("string"),
        "ylabel": field("string"), "cmap": field("string", default="viridis"), "dot_scale": field("number", exclusiveMinimum=0, default=200)},
        ["feature_dim", "group_dim", "features", "group_label", "filename"]),
    "violin": schema({"dim": field("string", default="feature"), "value_label": DIM, "group_dim": DIM, "group_label": DIM,
        "groups": strings(minItems=1, uniqueItems=True), "filename": DIM, "title": field("string"), "xlabel": field("string"),
        "ylabel": field("string"), "inner": enum(["box", "quartile", "point", "none"], "box"),
        "show_points": field("boolean", default=False), "point_size": field("number", exclusiveMinimum=0, default=4),
        "point_alpha": field("number", minimum=0, maximum=1, default=.5), "rotate_labels": field("boolean", default=False)},
        ["value_label", "group_dim", "group_label", "filename"]),
    "compare_groups": {**schema({"dim": DIM, "group_dim": DIM, "group_label": DIM,
        "mode": enum(["two_groups", "one_vs_rest", "pairwise"], "two_groups"),
        "group_a": field(["string", "number"], **{"x-ui-type": "scalar"}), "group_b": field(["string", "number"], **{"x-ui-type": "scalar"}),
        "groups": strings(minItems=2, uniqueItems=True), "min_per_group": field("integer", minimum=1, default=2),
        "method": enum(["ttest", "welch", "mannwhitney", "wilcoxon", "deseq2"], "ttest")}, ["dim", "group_dim", "group_label"]),
        "if": {"properties": {"mode": {"const": "two_groups"}}}, "then": {"required": ["group_a", "group_b"]}},
}
CONTRACTS = {
    "bin": ([port(dims=[param("dim")])], projected("bins", keep_dims=[param("dim")], append_dims=[{"name": "bin", "labels": ["bin"]}])),
    "cluster_axis": ([port(dims=[param("dim")])], projected("clusters", keep_dims=[param("dim")], append_dims=[{"name": "cluster", "labels": ["cluster"]}])),
    "reduce_axis": ([port(dims=[param("dim")])], projected("summary", drop_dims=[param("dim")], append_dims=[{"name": "statistic", "labels": param("stats")}])),
    "filter_by": ([port(dims=[param("dim")]), port(ndim=2, dims=[param("dim"), "statistic"])], {"mode": "subset"}),
    "rank_axis": ([port(dims=[param("dim")])], {"mode": "subset"}),
    "dotplot": ([port(ndim=2, dims=[param("feature_dim"), param("group_dim")]),
        {**port(ndim=2, dims=[param("group_dim")]), "labels_any": [param("group_label")]}], {"mode": "report"}),
    "violin": ([{**port(ndim=2, dims=[param("dim", "feature"), param("group_dim")]), "labels_any": [param("value_label")]},
        {**port(ndim=2, dims=[param("group_dim")]), "labels_any": [param("group_label")]}], {"mode": "report"}),
    "compare_groups": ([port(ndim=2, dims=[param("dim"), param("group_dim")]),
        {**port(ndim=2, dims=[param("group_dim")]), "labels_any": [param("group_label")]}], {"mode": "compare_groups"}),
}


def estimate_axis(name, inputs, params, profiles):
    workspace, output = estimate(name, inputs, params, {})
    if inputs:
        form = inputs[0]
        dim = params.get("dim", params.get("group_dim"))
        n = form.data.shape[form.dims.index(dim)] if dim in form.dims else max(form.data.shape)
        if name == "cluster_axis":
            workspace += n * n * 32
        elif name == "compare_groups" and params.get("mode", "two_groups") != "two_groups":
            samples = form.data.shape[form.dims.index(params["group_dim"])]
            groups = len(params.get("groups", [])) or samples
            comparisons = groups * (groups - 1) // 2 if params["mode"] == "pairwise" else groups
            output = n * comparisons * 7 * 4 + comparisons * 512
            workspace += output * 4
        elif name in {"dotplot", "violin"}:
            workspace, output = max(workspace, 16 * 1024**2), 0
    override = profiles.get(name, {})
    return int(override.get("workspace_mb", workspace / 1024**2) * 1024**2), int(override.get("output_mb", output / 1024**2) * 1024**2)

===== src/task/step/steps/_plot_helpers.py =====
# src/task/step/steps/_plot_helpers.py
"""Shared helpers for plotting steps."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use("Agg")
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt


_STYLE_APPLIED = False


def setup_style() -> None:
    global _STYLE_APPLIED
    if _STYLE_APPLIED:
        return
    matplotlib.rcParams.update({
        "font.family": "sans-serif",
        "font.sans-serif": ["Arial", "Helvetica", "DejaVu Sans"],
        "font.size": 10,
        "axes.labelsize": 11,
        "axes.titlesize": 12,
        "xtick.labelsize": 9,
        "ytick.labelsize": 9,
        "legend.fontsize": 9,
        "axes.linewidth": 0.8,
        "axes.spines.top": False,
        "axes.spines.right": False,
        "xtick.direction": "out",
        "ytick.direction": "out",
        "lines.linewidth": 1.0,
        "figure.dpi": 100,
        "savefig.dpi": 200,
        "savefig.bbox": "tight",
        "savefig.pad_inches": 0.1,
    })
    _STYLE_APPLIED = True


def save_figure(fig, ctx, filename: str) -> str:
    """Save ``fig`` in the current run's figures directory.

    Returns the absolute path as a string.
    """
    base = ctx.memory.run_dir if ctx.memory else ctx.out_dir
    fig_dir = base / "figures"
    fig_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out_path = fig_dir / filename
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(out_path)
    plt.close(fig)
    return str(out_path)

===== src/task/step/steps/_microbiome.py =====
"""Shared declarations and resource estimates for abundance-table operators.

Kernels remain full-memory operations. Estimates account for float64 temporary
arrays and quadratic outputs, and use the existing admission/spill machinery.
"""
import math

import numpy as np

from src.core.contracts import MISSING, param, port
from src.core.forms.metadata import project_metadata


def field(kind, **kwargs):
    return {"type": kind, **kwargs}


def enum(values, default=None):
    return {"type": "string", "enum": list(values), **({"default": default} if default is not None else {})}


def strings(**kwargs):
    return field("array", **{"items": field("string"), **kwargs})


def schema(properties, required):
    return {"type": "object", "properties": properties, "required": required, "additionalProperties": False}


DIM = field("string", minLength=1)
SCHEMAS = {
    "diversity": schema({"dim": DIM, "metrics": {"oneOf": [enum(["shannon", "simpson", "richness", "evenness", "gini"]),
        strings(minItems=1, uniqueItems=True, items=enum(["shannon", "simpson", "richness", "evenness", "gini"]))]}}, ["dim", "metrics"]),
    "distance_matrix": schema({"dim": DIM, "metric": enum(["euclidean", "manhattan", "cosine", "correlation", "braycurtis", "jaccard", "hamming"], "euclidean"),
        "max_positions": field(["integer", "null"], minimum=1, default=None, description="Optional position limit; null uses the memory budget without a fixed count cap.")}, ["dim"]),
    "correlation": schema({"dim": DIM, "metric": enum(["pearson", "spearman", "kendall", "cosine", "jaccard", "overlap"], "pearson"),
        "min_valid": field("integer", minimum=2, default=3), "max_positions": field(["integer", "null"], minimum=1, default=None)}, ["dim"]),
    "reduce": schema({"dim": DIM, "method": enum(["pca", "pcoa", "umap", "tsne"], "pca"),
        "n_components": field("integer", minimum=1, description="Omit for method default: PCA/PCoA 10, UMAP/t-SNE 2."),
        "standardize": field("boolean", default=True), "random_state": field("integer", default=0),
        "n_neighbors": field("integer", minimum=2), "min_dist": field("number", minimum=0), "metric": field("string"),
        "perplexity": field("number", exclusiveMinimum=0), "learning_rate": {"anyOf": [field("number", exclusiveMinimum=0), {"const": "auto"}]},
        "n_iter": field("integer", minimum=250)}, ["dim"]),
    "join": schema({"dim": DIM, "columns": {"oneOf": [{"const": "all"}, strings(minItems=1, uniqueItems=True)], "default": "all"},
        "how": enum(["inner", "left"], "inner"), "prefix": field("string", default="")}, ["dim"]),
    "aggregate": schema({"dim": DIM, "group_col": DIM, "method": enum(["sum", "mean", "median", "min", "max", "count"], "sum")}, ["dim", "group_col"]),
    "permanova": schema({"dist_dim": field("string", default="sample"), "group_dim": DIM, "group_label": DIM,
        "groups": strings(minItems=2, uniqueItems=True), "pairwise": field("boolean", default=False),
        "n_permutations": field("integer", minimum=1, default=999), "random_state": field("integer", default=0)}, ["group_label"]),
    "barplot": schema({"dim": DIM, "sample_dim": DIM, "filename": DIM, "top_n": field(["integer", "null"], minimum=0, default=20),
        "other_label": field("string", default="Other"), "normalize": field("boolean", default=True), "title": field("string"),
        "xlabel": field("string"), "ylabel": field("string"), "cmap": field("string", default="tab20"),
        "figsize": field("array", items=field("number", exclusiveMinimum=0), minItems=2, maxItems=2),
        "legend": field("boolean", default=True), "legend_ncol": field("integer", minimum=1, default=3),
        "rotate_labels": field("boolean", default=True)}, ["dim", "sample_dim", "filename"]),
}


def projected(representation, **kwargs):
    return {"mode": "project", "derived": True, "info": {"missing_code": MISSING,
            "representation": {"type": "string", "const": representation}}, **kwargs}


PAIR_AXIS = {"name": {"param": "dim", "suffix": "_b"}, "labels_from": param("dim")}
CONTRACTS = {
    "diversity": ([port(dims=[param("dim")])], projected("diversity", drop_dims=[param("dim")], append_dims=[{"name": "metric", "labels": param("metrics")} ])),
    "distance_matrix": ([port(dims=[param("dim")])], projected("distance", keep_dims=[param("dim")], append_dims=[PAIR_AXIS])),
    "correlation": ([port(dims=[param("dim")])], projected("correlation", keep_dims=[param("dim")], append_dims=[PAIR_AXIS])),
    "reduce": ([port(ndim=2, dims=[param("dim")])], {"mode": "choose", "param": "method", "default": "pca", "cases": {
        "pcoa": projected("scores", keep_dims=[param("dim")], append_dims=[{"name": "component"}])},
        "otherwise": projected("scores", drop_dims=[param("dim")], append_dims=[{"name": "component"}])}),
    "join": ([port(ndim=2, dims=[param("dim")]), port(ndim=2, dims=[param("dim")])],
             projected("joined", labels_unknown=True, axis_info_unknown=True)),
    "aggregate": ([port(ndim=2, dims=[param("dim")]), {**port(ndim=2, dims=[param("dim")]), "labels_axis": [{"axis": 1, "values": [param("group_col")]}]}],
                  projected("aggregate", drop_dims=[param("dim")], prepend_dims=[{"input_axis": [1, 1]}])),
    "permanova": ([port(ndim=2, dims=[param("dist_dim", "sample"), {"param": "dist_dim", "default": "sample", "suffix": "_b"}]),
                   {**port(ndim=2, dims=[param("group_dim", param("dist_dim", "sample"))]), "labels_axis": [{"axis": 1, "values": [param("group_label")]}]}],
                  {"mode": "declare", "schema": {"ndim": 2, "numeric": True, "dims": ["comparison", "statistic"],
                   "labels": {"comparison": None, "statistic": ["F", "R2", "P", "n_perm"]}, "axis_info": {},
                   "info": {"missing_code": MISSING, "representation": {"type": "string", "const": "permanova"}}}}),
    "barplot": ([port(ndim=2, dims=[param("dim"), param("sample_dim")])], {"mode": "report"}),
}


def metadata(form, keep, representation, selections=None):
    info, axes = project_metadata(form, selections, keep_dims=keep, derived=True)
    info.update(missing_code=np.nan, representation=representation)
    return info, axes


def estimate(name, inputs, params, profiles):
    if not inputs:
        return 0, 0
    form = inputs[0]
    size = math.prod(form.data.shape)
    dim = params.get("dim", params.get("dist_dim", "sample"))
    axis = form.dims.index(dim) if dim in form.dims else 0
    n = form.data.shape[axis]
    label_bytes = sum(sum(len(v) for v in f.labels.values()) * 192 for f in inputs)
    workspace, output = size * 96 + label_bytes, size * 4 + label_bytes
    if name in {"distance_matrix", "correlation"}:
        workspace = size * 40 + n * n * 48 + label_bytes
        output = n * n * 4 + n * 384
    elif name == "reduce":
        method = params.get("method", "pca")
        observations = n if method == "pcoa" else form.data.shape[1 - axis]
        k = min(int(params.get("n_components", 2 if method in {"umap", "tsne"} else 10)), min(form.data.shape))
        workspace = size * 128 + observations * observations * (128 if method in {"tsne", "umap"} else 16) + label_bytes
        output = observations * k * 4 + (observations + k) * 192 + label_bytes
    elif name == "diversity":
        metrics = params.get("metrics", [])
        count = 1 if isinstance(metrics, str) else len(metrics)
        output = size // max(1, n) * count * 4 + label_bytes
    elif name == "join":
        rows = form.data.shape[0]
        columns = form.data.shape[1] + inputs[1].data.shape[1]
        output = rows * columns * 4 + label_bytes
        workspace = output * 6
    elif name == "permanova":
        # Permutations run sequentially. Worst case: each row has a group.
        groups = len(params["groups"]) if params.get("groups") else n
        comparisons = 1 + groups * (groups - 1) // 2 if params.get("pairwise") else 1
        workspace = n * n * 96 + comparisons * 2048 + label_bytes
        output = comparisons * (4 * 4 + 192)
    elif name == "barplot":
        workspace, output = max(workspace, 16 * 1024**2), 0
    override = profiles.get(name, {})
    return int(override.get("workspace_mb", workspace / 1024**2) * 1024**2), int(override.get("output_mb", output / 1024**2) * 1024**2)

===== src/task/step/steps/describe.py =====
# src/task/step/steps/describe.py
"""Describe step: print and export a summary of an input form."""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np

from src.task.context import Context
from src.task.step.base import Step
from src.core.contracts import MISSING, param, port


class DescribeStep(Step):
    """Print a summary of one input form.

    Params: none.
    """

    name = "describe"
    inputs_arity = 1
    outputs = []
    input_contracts = [port()]
    output_contract = {"mode": "report"}

    supports_streaming = True
    stream_protocol = "flat-reduction-v1"

    def run_stream(self, ctx: Context, stream: dict) -> None:
        """Bounded finite-cell statistics; float64 moments may differ by rounding."""
        count, mean, m2 = 0, 0.0, 0.0
        minimum = maximum = None
        for block in stream["chunks"]:
            finite = block[np.isfinite(block)]
            if finite.size:
                values = finite.astype(np.float64, copy=False)
                n = int(values.size)
                local_mean = float(values.mean())
                local_m2 = float(values.var()) * n
                delta = local_mean - mean
                total = count + n
                m2 += local_m2 + delta * delta * count * n / total
                mean += delta * n / total
                count = total
                low, high = float(values.min()), float(values.max())
                minimum = low if minimum is None else min(minimum, low)
                maximum = high if maximum is None else max(maximum, high)
                del values
            del finite
        meta = stream["metadata"]
        self._export_data = {
            "shape": list(stream["shape"]), "dims": list(meta["dims"]),
            "info": _serialize_info(meta["info"]),
            "axis_info": meta.get("axis_info", {}),
            "labels": {dim: {str(k): int(v) for k, v in labels.items()}
                       for dim, labels in meta["labels"].items()},
            "global": {"min": minimum, "max": maximum,
                       "mean": mean if count else None,
                       "std": float(np.sqrt(max(0.0, m2 / count))) if count else None,
                       "n_valid": count,
                       "n_missing": int(np.prod(stream["shape"])) - count},
        }
        print(f"[describe] streamed shape={stream['shape']}, n_valid={count}")

    def run(self, ctx: Context, inputs) -> None:
        form = inputs[0]

        data = form.data
        finite = data[np.isfinite(data)]

        summary = {
            "shape": list(data.shape),
            "dims": list(form.dims),
            "info": _serialize_info(form.info),
            "axis_info": form.axis_info,
            "labels": {
                dim: {str(k): int(v) for k, v in mapping.items()}
                for dim, mapping in form.labels.items()
            },
            "global": {
                "min": float(np.min(finite)) if finite.size else None,
                "max": float(np.max(finite)) if finite.size else None,
                "mean": float(np.mean(finite)) if finite.size else None,
                "std": float(np.std(finite)) if finite.size else None,
                "n_valid": int(finite.size),
                "n_missing": int(data.size - finite.size),
            },
        }
        self._export_data = summary

        print("[describe] form")
        print(f"  shape:  {tuple(data.shape)}")
        print(f"  dims:   {form.dims}")
        print(f"  info:   {form.info}")
        # if form.labels:
        #     print(f"  labels: {form.labels}")
        if finite.size > 0:
            print(
                f"  global: min={float(np.min(finite)):.4f} "
                f"max={float(np.max(finite)):.4f} "
                f"mean={float(np.mean(finite)):.4f} "
                f"std={float(np.std(finite)):.4f} "
                f"n_valid={finite.size} "
                f"n_missing={data.size - finite.size}"
            )
        else:
            print("  global: all cells missing")

    def export(self, ctx: Context) -> dict:
        return self._export_data


def _serialize_info(info: dict) -> dict:
    def value(item):
        if isinstance(item, np.generic):
            return value(item.item())
        if isinstance(item, np.ndarray):
            return value(item.tolist())
        if isinstance(item, (list, tuple)):
            return [value(v) for v in item]
        if isinstance(item, dict):
            return {str(k): value(v) for k, v in item.items()}
        if isinstance(item, float) and not np.isfinite(item):
            return str(item)
        if isinstance(item, (str, int, float, bool)) or item is None:
            return item
        return str(item)
    out: dict = {}
    for k, v in info.items():
        if k == "encoders":
            out[k] = {
                dim: {
                    label: {
                        "name": enc.name,
                        "mapping": {
                            str(kk): int(vv)
                            for kk, vv in enc.mapping.items()
                        },
                    }
                    for label, enc in cols.items()
                }
                for dim, cols in v.items()
            }
        else:
            out[k] = value(v)
    return out

===== src/task/step/steps/select.py =====
# src/task/step/steps/select.py
"""Select step: keep a subset of labels along one dimension."""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np

from src.core import Form, StepError
from src.task.context import Context
from src.task.step.base import Step
from src.core.contracts import MISSING, param, port


class SelectStep(Step):
    """Keep only the listed labels along one dimension.

    Params:
        dim    : dimension to select along
        labels : list of label names to keep, in output order
        output : context key for the result
    """

    name = "select"
    inputs_arity = 1
    outputs = []
    input_contracts = [port(dims=[param("dim")], labels=[{"dim": param("dim"), "values": param("labels")}])]
    output_contract = {"mode": "select"}
    output_parameter = "output"


    def run(self, ctx: Context, inputs) -> None:
        form = inputs[0]
        dim = ctx.step_params["dim"]
        labels = ctx.step_params["labels"]
        out_name = ctx.step_params.get("output", self.name)

        if dim not in form.dims:
            raise StepError(f"select: dim '{dim}' not in {form.dims}")
        if not isinstance(labels, list) or not labels:
            raise StepError("select: 'labels' must be a non-empty list")

        dim_labels = form.labels.get(dim, {})
        if not dim_labels:
            raise StepError(
                f"select: dim '{dim}' has no labels to select from"
            )

        missing = [l for l in labels if l not in dim_labels]
        if missing:
            raise StepError(
                f"select: labels not found on dim '{dim}': {missing}. "
                f"Available: {sorted(dim_labels)}"
            )

        axis = form.dims.index(dim)
        positions = [dim_labels[l] for l in labels]

        new_form = form.subset_axis(dim, positions)
        ctx[out_name] = new_form

        self._export_data = {
            "dim": dim,
            "n_before": int(form.data.shape[axis]),
            "n_after": len(labels),
            "selected_labels": list(labels),
        }

        print(
            f"[select] dim '{dim}': {len(labels)} labels kept "
            f"-> {out_name}, shape={new_form.data.shape}"
        )

    def export(self, ctx: Context) -> dict:
        return self._export_data
===== src/task/step/steps/filter_min.py =====
# src/task/step/steps/filter_min.py
"""Filter positions along one dimension by a minimum value.

Keeps positions whose value reaches ``min_value`` in at least
``min_samples`` samples. Domain-agnostic: works on any 2D form.

    pass[i, j] = valid[i, j] and data[i, j] >= min_value
    keep[i]    = sum_j(pass[i, j]) >= min_samples

Missing values never count as a passing sample.

Params:
    dim         : dimension to filter along
    min_value   : per-sample threshold (default 1)
    min_samples : minimum number of samples that must pass
                  (default 1)
    output      : context key for the result (default: step name)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np

from src.core import Form, StepError
from src.task.context import Context
from src.task.step.base import Step
from src.core.contracts import port, param
from src.core.forms.metadata import project_metadata


class FilterMinStep(Step):
    """Drop positions that do not reach a minimum value."""

    name = "filter_min"
    inputs_arity = 1
    outputs = []
    input_contracts = [port(dims=[param("dim")])]
    output_contract = {"mode": "subset"}
    output_parameter = "output"

    def run(self, ctx: Context, inputs) -> None:
        form = inputs[0]
        dim = ctx.step_params.get("dim")
        if dim is None:
            raise StepError("filter_min: 'dim' is required")
        min_value = float(ctx.step_params.get("min_value", 1))
        min_samples = int(ctx.step_params.get("min_samples", 1))
        out_name = ctx.step_params.get("output", self.name)

        if dim not in form.dims:
            raise StepError(
                f"filter_min: dim '{dim}' is not present. "
                f"Available: {form.dims}"
            )
        if min_samples < 1:
            raise StepError(
                f"filter_min: min_samples must be >= 1, "
                f"got {min_samples}"
            )

        axis = form.dims.index(dim)
        moved = np.moveaxis(form.data, axis, 0)
        n_positions = moved.shape[0]
        flat = moved.reshape(n_positions, -1)

        missing_code = form.info.get("missing_code", np.nan)
        if isinstance(missing_code, float) and np.isnan(missing_code):
            valid = ~np.isnan(flat)
        else:
            valid = flat != missing_code

        passing = valid & (flat >= min_value)
        n_pass = passing.sum(axis=1)
        keep = n_pass >= min_samples

        n_before = int(n_positions)
        n_after = int(keep.sum())
        if n_after == 0:
            raise StepError(
                f"filter_min: threshold removed every position "
                f"along '{dim}'."
            )

        kept = np.where(keep)[0]
        new_form = form.subset_axis(dim, kept)
        ctx[out_name] = new_form

        pos_to_label = {v: k for k, v in form.labels[dim].items()}
        kept_labels = [pos_to_label[int(i)] for i in kept]
        kept_set = set(kept.tolist())
        dropped_labels = [
            pos_to_label[i] for i in range(n_before)
            if i not in kept_set
        ]

        self._export_data = {
            "dim": dim,
            "min_value": min_value,
            "min_samples": min_samples,
            "n_before": n_before,
            "n_after": n_after,
            "n_removed": n_before - n_after,
            "kept_labels": kept_labels,
            "dropped_labels": dropped_labels,
        }

        ctx.metadata.setdefault(self.name, {})[out_name] = {
            "dim": dim,
            "min_value": min_value,
            "min_samples": min_samples,
            "n_before": n_before,
            "n_after": n_after,
            "n_removed": n_before - n_after,
        }

        print(
            f"[filter_min] dim='{dim}' {n_before} -> {n_after} "
            f"(min_value={min_value}, min_samples={min_samples})"
        )

    def export(self, ctx: Context) -> dict:
        return self._export_data
===== src/task/step/steps/annotate.py =====
# src/task/step/steps/annotate.py
"""Attach reference fields onto a target by key label.

Aligns two forms on a shared key dimension by label, then copies the
requested reference fields into the target's ``info``, declaring them
parallel to the target key axis.

A reference field may come from either:

* a data column: its label on ``field_dim``; encoded columns carry
  their encoder across so codes stay comparable.
* an info field: declared parallel to ``source_dim`` in
  ``axis_info``; typically read verbatim by ``info_columns``.

This is the standard way to enrich a table with an external lookup
table (variant annotations, gene descriptions, taxon metadata,
sample covariates) without merging data dimensions.

Layout
------
    target     dims: [key_dim, ...]
    reference  dims: [key_dim_ref, field_dim]
    output     dims: same as target

Alignment
---------
Matching is by label, not by position. For every target label on
``target_dim``, the reference row whose ``source_dim`` label equals
it supplies the values. Target labels without a reference match get
NaN.

The alignment is computed once as a position lookup array and applied
with numpy fancy indexing, so the cost is O(n) in C, not in Python.

Params:
    target_dim : key dimension on the target (required)
    source_dim : key dimension on the reference (required)
    fields     : reference field names to attach (required)
    field_dim  : reference data dimension (default: the other 2D dim)
    output     : context key for the result (default: step name)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from src.core import Form, StepError
from src.core.contracts import param, port
from src.task.context import Context
from src.task.step.base import Step


class AnnotateStep(Step):
    """Attach reference fields onto a target by key label."""

    name = "annotate"
    category = "data"
    icon = "Tag"
    inputs_arity = 2
    outputs = []
    input_contracts = [
        port(dims=[param("target_dim")]),
        port(ndim=2, dims=[param("source_dim")]),
    ]
    output_contract = {"mode": "unknown"}
    output_parameter = "output"
    parameters_schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "target_dim": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Target key dimension",
                "description": "Dimension on the target form to align on.",
            },
            "source_dim": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Reference key dimension",
                "description": "Dimension on the reference form to align on.",
            },
            "fields": {
                "type": "array",
                "items": {"type": "string"},
                "title": "Fields to attach",
                "minItems": 1,
                "description": (
                    "Names of reference data columns or reference info "
                    "fields. Data columns are looked up by their label "
                    "on the field dimension; info fields must be "
                    "declared parallel to the source key dimension."
                ),
            },
            "field_dim": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Reference field dimension",
            },
            "output": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Output key",
            },
        },
        "required": ["target_dim", "source_dim", "fields"],
    }

    def run(self, ctx: Context, inputs) -> None:
        target, reference = inputs
        target_dim = ctx.step_params.get("target_dim")
        source_dim = ctx.step_params.get("source_dim")
        fields = ctx.step_params.get("fields")
        out_name = ctx.step_params.get("output", self.name)

        if not target_dim:
            raise StepError("annotate: 'target_dim' is required")
        if not source_dim:
            raise StepError("annotate: 'source_dim' is required")
        if not fields:
            raise StepError("annotate: 'fields' is required")

        if target_dim not in target.dims:
            raise StepError(
                f"annotate: target_dim '{target_dim}' not present. "
                f"Target dims: {target.dims}"
            )
        if source_dim not in reference.dims:
            raise StepError(
                f"annotate: source_dim '{source_dim}' not present. "
                f"Reference dims: {reference.dims}"
            )
        if reference.data.ndim != 2:
            raise StepError(
                f"annotate: reference must be 2D, "
                f"got ndim={reference.data.ndim}"
            )

        field_dim = ctx.step_params.get("field_dim")
        if field_dim is None:
            other = [d for d in reference.dims if d != source_dim]
            if len(other) != 1:
                raise StepError(
                    f"annotate: cannot infer field_dim from "
                    f"{reference.dims}; specify 'field_dim'"
                )
            field_dim = other[0]
        if field_dim not in reference.dims:
            raise StepError(
                f"annotate: field_dim '{field_dim}' not present. "
                f"Reference dims: {reference.dims}"
            )
        if field_dim == source_dim:
            raise StepError(
                "annotate: field_dim and source_dim must differ"
            )

        ref_field_labels = reference.labels.get(field_dim, {})
        ref_parallel = reference.axis_info.get(source_dim, [])

        field_sources: dict[str, tuple[str, object]] = {}
        for field in fields:
            if field in ref_field_labels:
                field_sources[field] = ("data", ref_field_labels[field])
            elif field in ref_parallel:
                field_sources[field] = ("info", field)
            else:
                raise StepError(
                    f"annotate: field '{field}' is neither a column "
                    f"label on '{field_dim}' "
                    f"({sorted(ref_field_labels)}) nor an info field "
                    f"parallel to '{source_dim}' ({ref_parallel})"
                )

        target_labels = target.labels[target_dim]
        source_labels = reference.labels[source_dim]

        target_axis = target.dims.index(target_dim)
        source_axis = reference.dims.index(source_dim)
        field_axis = reference.dims.index(field_dim)

        target_size = target.data.shape[target_axis]
        source_size = reference.data.shape[source_axis]

        # --- Build a position lookup: target position -> source position. ---
        lookup = np.full(target_size, -1, dtype=np.int64)
        if source_size <= target_size:
            for label, src_pos in source_labels.items():
                tgt_pos = target_labels.get(label)
                if tgt_pos is not None:
                    lookup[tgt_pos] = src_pos
        else:
            for label, tgt_pos in target_labels.items():
                src_pos = source_labels.get(label)
                if src_pos is not None:
                    lookup[tgt_pos] = src_pos

        valid = lookup >= 0
        n_matched = int(valid.sum())
        n_unmatched = target_size - n_matched
        matched_lookup = lookup[valid]

        # Reorient reference so axis 0 is source_dim and axis 1 is field_dim.
        ref_data = np.moveaxis(
            reference.data, (source_axis, field_axis), (0, 1)
        )

        ref_encoders = reference.info.get("encoders", {}).get(
            field_dim, {}
        )

        new_info = dict(target.info)
        new_axis_info = {
            k: list(v) for k, v in target.axis_info.items()
        }
        parallel = set(new_axis_info.get(target_dim, []))
        encoders_out = {
            d: dict(cols)
            for d, cols in new_info.get("encoders", {}).items()
        }

        encoded_fields: list[str] = []

        for field in fields:
            source_kind, source_key = field_sources[field]

            if source_kind == "data":
                column = np.asarray(
                    ref_data[:, source_key], dtype=np.float64
                )
                encoder = ref_encoders.get(field)
            else:
                raw = reference.info.get(field)
                if raw is None:
                    raise StepError(
                        f"annotate: info['{field}'] missing from "
                        f"reference"
                    )
                if isinstance(raw, np.ndarray):
                    column = raw.astype(np.float64, copy=False)
                else:
                    column = pd.to_numeric(
                        pd.Series(raw), errors="coerce"
                    ).to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)
                encoder = None

            values = np.full(target_size, np.nan, dtype=np.float64)
            if n_matched:
                values[valid] = column[matched_lookup]

            new_info[field] = values
            if encoder is not None:
                encoders_out.setdefault(target_dim, {})[field] = encoder
                encoded_fields.append(field)
            parallel.add(field)

        new_axis_info[target_dim] = sorted(parallel)

        if encoders_out:
            new_info["encoders"] = {
                d: c for d, c in encoders_out.items() if c
            }
        else:
            new_info.pop("encoders", None)

        out_form = Form(
            data=target.data.copy(),
            dims=list(target.dims),
            labels={k: dict(v) for k, v in target.labels.items()},
            info=new_info,
            axis_info=new_axis_info,
        )
        out_form.validate()
        ctx[out_name] = out_form

        unmatched_indices = np.where(~valid)[0]
        pos_to_label = {v: k for k, v in target_labels.items()}
        unmatched_labels = [
            pos_to_label[int(i)]
            for i in unmatched_indices
            if int(i) in pos_to_label
        ]

        self._export_data = {
            "target_dim": target_dim,
            "source_dim": source_dim,
            "field_dim": field_dim,
            "fields": list(fields),
            "field_sources": {
                f: field_sources[f][0] for f in fields
            },
            "n_target": int(target_size),
            "n_matched": n_matched,
            "n_unmatched": n_unmatched,
            "encoded_fields": encoded_fields,
            "unmatched_labels": unmatched_labels,
        }

        ctx.metadata.setdefault(self.name, {})[out_name] = {
            "target_dim": target_dim,
            "fields": list(fields),
            "n_matched": n_matched,
            "n_unmatched": n_unmatched,
        }

        print(
            f"[annotate] '{target_dim}' <- '{source_dim}' "
            f"{n_matched}/{target_size} matched "
            f"({n_unmatched} missing)"
        )

    def export(self, ctx: Context) -> dict:
        return self._export_data

===== src/task/step/steps/explode.py =====
# src/task/step/steps/explode.py
"""Expand a multi-valued info field into separate positions.

When an info field declared parallel to ``dim`` contains a list per
position (for example semicolon-separated GO terms), this step
creates one output position per element. Other axes are replicated.

This is the standard way to turn a one-to-many annotation into a
long table that downstream grouping operators can consume.

Layout
------
    input   dims: [..., dim, ...]
    output  dims: same, with ``dim`` expanded

Params:
    dim        : dimension whose info field is exploded (required)
    field      : info field name declared parallel to ``dim``
                 (required)
    sep        : separator inside each cell (default ";")
    keep_empty : keep positions whose field is empty (default False)
    output     : context key for the result (default: step name)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np

from src.core import Form, StepError
from src.core.contracts import param, port
from src.task.context import Context
from src.task.step.base import Step


class ExplodeStep(Step):
    """Expand a multi-valued info field into separate positions."""

    name = "explode"
    category = "data"
    icon = "ArrowsDownUp"
    inputs_arity = 1
    outputs = []
    input_contracts = [
        port(
            dims=[param("dim")],
            parallel=[{"fields": param("field"), "dim": param("dim")}],
        ),
    ]
    output_contract = {
        "mode": "declare",
        "schema": {
            "numeric": True,
            "info_open": True,
            "axis_info_open": True,
        },
    }
    output_parameter = "output"
    parameters_schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "dim": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Dimension to expand",
            },
            "field": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Multi-valued field",
                "description": (
                    "Info field declared parallel to 'dim'. Each "
                    "cell is split by 'sep'."
                ),
            },
            "sep": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Separator",
                "default": ";",
                "minLength": 1,
            },
            "keep_empty": {
                "type": "boolean",
                "title": "Keep empty",
                "default": False,
            },
            "output": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Output key",
            },
        },
        "required": ["dim", "field"],
    }

    def run(self, ctx: Context, inputs) -> None:
        form = inputs[0]
        dim = ctx.step_params.get("dim")
        field = ctx.step_params.get("field")
        sep = ctx.step_params.get("sep", ";")
        keep_empty = bool(ctx.step_params.get("keep_empty", False))
        out_name = ctx.step_params.get("output", self.name)

        if not dim:
            raise StepError("explode: 'dim' is required")
        if not field:
            raise StepError("explode: 'field' is required")
        if dim not in form.dims:
            raise StepError(
                f"explode: dim '{dim}' not present. "
                f"Available: {form.dims}"
            )

        parallel = form.axis_info.get(dim, [])
        if field not in parallel:
            raise StepError(
                f"explode: field '{field}' is not declared parallel "
                f"to '{dim}'. Declared: {parallel}"
            )

        raw = form.info.get(field)
        if raw is None:
            raise StepError(f"explode: info['{field}'] is missing")

        axis = form.dims.index(dim)
        size = form.data.shape[axis]
        if len(raw) != size:
            raise StepError(
                f"explode: info['{field}'] has length {len(raw)}, "
                f"expected {size}"
            )

        cells: list[list[str]] = []
        for value in raw:
            if value is None:
                parts = []
            elif isinstance(value, str):
                parts = [p.strip() for p in value.split(sep) if p.strip()]
            elif isinstance(value, (list, tuple, np.ndarray)):
                parts = [str(p).strip() for p in value if str(p).strip()]
            else:
                parts = [str(value)]
            cells.append(parts)

        source_indices: list[int] = []
        expanded_labels: list[str] = []
        for src_pos, parts in enumerate(cells):
            if not parts and keep_empty:
                source_indices.append(src_pos)
                expanded_labels.append(f"{src_pos}#empty")
                continue
            for part in parts:
                source_indices.append(src_pos)
                expanded_labels.append(part)

        if not source_indices:
            raise StepError(
                f"explode: field '{field}' produced zero output positions"
            )

        new_data = np.take(form.data, source_indices, axis=axis).copy()

        from src.reader.readers._labels import unique_labels
        dim_labels = unique_labels(expanded_labels, dim, "explode")

        new_info = {k: v for k, v in form.info.items()}
        new_axis_info = {k: list(v) for k, v in form.axis_info.items()}

        for other_field in form.axis_info.get(dim, []):
            if other_field == field:
                continue
            other_raw = form.info.get(other_field)
            if other_raw is None:
                continue
            if isinstance(other_raw, np.ndarray):
                new_info[other_field] = other_raw[source_indices].copy()
            else:
                new_info[other_field] = [
                    other_raw[i] for i in source_indices
                ]

        new_info.pop(field, None)
        if field in new_axis_info.get(dim, []):
            new_axis_info[dim] = [
                f for f in new_axis_info[dim] if f != field
            ]
            if not new_axis_info[dim]:
                del new_axis_info[dim]

        encoders = new_info.get("encoders", {})
        if dim in encoders and field in encoders[dim]:
            encoders[dim] = {
                k: v for k, v in encoders[dim].items() if k != field
            }
            if not encoders[dim]:
                del encoders[dim]
            if encoders:
                new_info["encoders"] = encoders
            else:
                new_info.pop("encoders", None)

        new_labels = {k: dict(v) for k, v in form.labels.items()}
        new_labels[dim] = dim_labels

        out_form = Form(
            data=new_data,
            dims=list(form.dims),
            labels=new_labels,
            info=new_info,
            axis_info=new_axis_info,
        )
        out_form.validate()
        ctx[out_name] = out_form

        self._export_data = {
            "dim": dim,
            "field": field,
            "sep": sep,
            "keep_empty": keep_empty,
            "n_before": int(size),
            "n_after": len(source_indices),
            "expansion": [
                {"source": int(src), "label": lab}
                for src, lab in zip(source_indices, expanded_labels)
            ],
        }

        ctx.metadata.setdefault(self.name, {})[out_name] = {
            "dim": dim,
            "field": field,
            "n_before": int(size),
            "n_after": len(source_indices),
        }

        print(
            f"[explode] '{field}' on '{dim}': "
            f"{size} -> {len(source_indices)} positions"
        )

    def export(self, ctx: Context) -> dict:
        return self._export_data

===== src/task/step/steps/pivot.py =====
# src/task/step/steps/pivot.py
"""Turn a long table into a wide matrix by two categorical columns.

The input is a 2D form whose ``field_dim`` holds a set of named
columns. Two of those columns are chosen: one becomes the row axis
of the output, the other becomes the column axis. A third column may
supply the cell value; without it, every observed pair contributes 1.

This is the standard way to build a gene x GO-term membership matrix
from a GAF file, a sample x feature matrix from a long CSV, or any
pair-count table that downstream matrix operators can consume.

Layout
------
    input   dims: [pair, field]
    output  dims: [<index_name>, <columns_name>]

Duplicate (index, column) pairs are combined by ``agg``.

A hard cell-count cap protects the runtime from accidentally
materialising a huge dense matrix. When the cap is exceeded, filter
the input to a smaller subset before pivoting.

Params:
    index_label   : column label on the input field dim, becomes the
                    row axis (required)
    columns_label : column label on the input field dim, becomes the
                    column axis (required)
    values_label  : column label supplying cell values; without it
                    every observed pair contributes 1
    index_name    : name of the output row dim (default: index_label)
    columns_name  : name of the output column dim (default: columns_label)
    agg           : sum | first | max | min | count (default sum)
    output        : context key for the result (default: step name)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from src.core import Form, StepError
from src.core.contracts import param, port
from src.task.context import Context
from src.task.step.base import Step


_AGGS = ("sum", "first", "max", "min", "count")
# Hard cap on dense output cells (~800 MB at float32).
_MAX_CELLS = 200_000_000_0


class PivotStep(Step):
    """Pivot a long 2D table into a wide matrix by two categorical columns."""

    name = "pivot"
    category = "data"
    icon = "Columns"
    inputs_arity = 1
    outputs = []
    input_contracts = [port(ndim=2)]
    output_contract = {
        "mode": "declare",
        "schema": {
            "ndim": 2,
            "numeric": True,
            "info_open": True,
            "axis_info_open": True,
        },
    }
    output_parameter = "output"
    parameters_schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "index_label": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Index column",
                "description": "Column label that becomes the row axis.",
            },
            "columns_label": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Columns column",
                "description": "Column label that becomes the column axis.",
            },
            "values_label": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Values column",
                "description": (
                    "Optional column supplying cell values. Without it "
                    "every observed pair contributes 1."
                ),
            },
            "index_name": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Row axis name",
            },
            "columns_name": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Column axis name",
            },
            "agg": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Aggregation",
                "enum": list(_AGGS),
                "default": "sum",
            },
            "output": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Output key",
            },
        },
        "required": ["index_label", "columns_label"],
    }

    def run(self, ctx: Context, inputs) -> None:
        form = inputs[0]
        if form.data.ndim != 2:
            raise StepError(
                f"pivot: input must be 2D, got ndim={form.data.ndim}"
            )

        index_label = ctx.step_params.get("index_label")
        columns_label = ctx.step_params.get("columns_label")
        values_label = ctx.step_params.get("values_label")
        index_name = ctx.step_params.get("index_name") or index_label
        columns_name = ctx.step_params.get("columns_name") or columns_label
        agg = ctx.step_params.get("agg", "sum")
        out_name = ctx.step_params.get("output", self.name)

        if not index_label:
            raise StepError("pivot: 'index_label' is required")
        if not columns_label:
            raise StepError("pivot: 'columns_label' is required")
        if agg not in _AGGS:
            raise StepError(
                f"pivot: agg must be one of {list(_AGGS)}, got {agg!r}"
            )
        if index_name == columns_name:
            raise StepError(
                f"pivot: index_name and columns_name must differ "
                f"(both '{index_name}')"
            )

        row_dim, field_dim = form.dims
        field_labels = form.labels.get(field_dim, {})

        for label in (index_label, columns_label):
            if label not in field_labels:
                raise StepError(
                    f"pivot: label '{label}' not in field labels "
                    f"{sorted(field_labels)}"
                )
        if values_label and values_label not in field_labels:
            raise StepError(
                f"pivot: values_label '{values_label}' not in field "
                f"labels {sorted(field_labels)}"
            )

        index_codes = form.data[:, field_labels[index_label]]
        columns_codes = form.data[:, field_labels[columns_label]]

        if values_label:
            values = form.data[
                :, field_labels[values_label]
            ].astype(np.float64)
        else:
            values = np.ones(form.data.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)

        encoders_for_field = form.info.get("encoders", {}).get(
            field_dim, {}
        )

        index_strings = _decode(
            index_codes, encoders_for_field.get(index_label)
        )
        columns_strings = _decode(
            columns_codes, encoders_for_field.get(columns_label)
        )

        frame = pd.DataFrame({
            "index": index_strings,
            "column": columns_strings,
            "value": values,
        })

        agg_func = {
            "sum": "sum",
            "first": "first",
            "max": "max",
            "min": "min",
            "count": "size",
        }[agg]

        try:
            grouped = frame.groupby(
                ["index", "column"], sort=False, dropna=False
            )["value"]
            if agg == "count":
                wide = grouped.size().unstack(fill_value=0)
            else:
                wide = grouped.agg(agg_func).unstack(fill_value=0)
        except (ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
            raise StepError(f"pivot: aggregation failed: {exc}") from exc

        n_index = int(wide.shape[0])
        n_columns = int(wide.shape[1])

        if n_index * n_columns > _MAX_CELLS:
            raise StepError(
                f"pivot: output would be {n_index} x {n_columns} "
                f"= {n_index * n_columns:,} cells, exceeding the "
                f"{_MAX_CELLS:,} cap. Filter the input to a smaller "
                f"subset before pivoting."
            )

        out_data = wide.to_numpy(dtype=np.float32)

        index_labels = {
            str(label): i for i, label in enumerate(wide.index)
        }
        column_labels = {
            str(label): i for i, label in enumerate(wide.columns)
        }

        out_form = Form(
            data=out_data,
            dims=[index_name, columns_name],
            labels={
                index_name: index_labels,
                columns_name: column_labels,
            },
            info={"missing_code": np.nan},
        )
        out_form.validate()
        ctx[out_name] = out_form

        nonzero = int(np.count_nonzero(out_data))

        self._export_data = {
            "index_label": index_label,
            "columns_label": columns_label,
            "values_label": values_label,
            "index_name": index_name,
            "columns_name": columns_name,
            "agg": agg,
            "n_input_rows": int(form.data.shape[0]),
            "n_index": n_index,
            "n_columns": n_columns,
            "n_cells": n_index * n_columns,
            "n_nonzero": nonzero,
            "sparsity": (
                1.0 - nonzero / (n_index * n_columns)
                if n_index * n_columns else 0.0
            ),
        }

        ctx.metadata.setdefault(self.name, {})[out_name] = {
            "index_name": index_name,
            "columns_name": columns_name,
            "shape": [n_index, n_columns],
        }

        print(
            f"[pivot] {form.data.shape} -> ({n_index}, {n_columns}) "
            f"[{index_name} x {columns_name}], agg={agg}, "
            f"nonzero={nonzero}"
        )

    def export(self, ctx: Context) -> dict:
        return self._export_data


def _decode(codes, encoder) -> list[str]:
    if encoder is None:
        return [str(int(round(float(c)))) for c in codes]
    inverse = getattr(encoder, "inverse", {})
    out = []
    for c in codes:
        key = int(round(float(c)))
        out.append(str(inverse.get(key, key)))
    return out

===== src/task/step/steps/normalize.py =====
# src/task/step/steps/normalize.py
"""Normalize a Form along one dimension.

Applies a sample-level normalization. Standard second step after
filtering, for any count or intensity matrix (RNA-seq, proteomics,
metabolomics, single-cell).

Supported methods
-----------------
    cpm                counts per million: divide each sample by its
                       total, then scale to ``target_sum``
    total              divide each sample by its total (sum = 1)
    median_of_ratios   DESeq2 size factors: per-gene geometric mean,
                       per-sample median of ratios
    quantile           quantile normalization: for each rank position,
                       replace the value with the mean of that rank
                       across all samples
    zscore             z-score each sample (mean 0, std 1)

Layout
------
Input is any Form. The normalization runs along ``dim`` (typically
the sample axis); every other axis is flattened.

Output has the same dims, labels, info and axis_info as the input.
Only ``data`` is replaced.

Params:
    method     : "cpm" | "total" | "median_of_ratios" | "quantile"
                 | "zscore"
    dim        : sample dimension
    target_sum : target total for ``cpm`` (default 1e6)
    output     : context key for the result (default: step name)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np

from src.core import Form, StepError
from src.task.context import Context
from src.task.step.base import Step
from src.core.contracts import port, param
