from src.core.forms.metadata import project_metadata


_METHODS = (
    "cpm", "total", "median_of_ratios", "quantile", "zscore",
)
_SIZE_FACTOR_METHODS = ("cpm", "total", "median_of_ratios")


class NormalizeStep(Step):
    """Normalize a Form along one dimension."""

    name = "normalize"
    inputs_arity = 1
    outputs = []
    input_contracts = [port(dims=[param("dim")])]
    output_contract = {"mode": "transform", "representation": "normalize"}
    output_parameter = "output"

    def run(self, ctx: Context, inputs) -> None:
        form = inputs[0]
        method = ctx.step_params.get("method")
        dim = ctx.step_params.get("dim")
        target_sum = float(ctx.step_params.get("target_sum", 1e6))
        out_name = ctx.step_params.get("output", self.name)

        if method not in _METHODS:
            raise StepError(
                f"normalize: method must be one of {_METHODS}, "
                f"got {method!r}"
            )
        if dim is None:
            raise StepError("normalize: 'dim' is required")
        if dim not in form.dims:
            raise StepError(
                f"normalize: dim '{dim}' is not present. "
                f"Available: {form.dims}"
            )
        if method == "cpm" and target_sum <= 0:
            raise StepError(
                f"normalize: target_sum must be > 0, got {target_sum}"
            )

        axis = form.dims.index(dim)
        moved = np.moveaxis(form.data, axis, 0).astype(np.float64)
        n_samples = moved.shape[0]
        flat = moved.reshape(n_samples, -1)

        missing_code = form.info.get("missing_code", np.nan)
        if isinstance(missing_code, float) and np.isnan(missing_code):
            valid = ~np.isnan(flat)
        else:
            valid = flat != missing_code

        size_factors = None
        if method == "cpm":
            size_factors = _cpm(flat, valid, target_sum)
        elif method == "total":
            size_factors = _total(flat, valid)
        elif method == "median_of_ratios":
            size_factors = _median_of_ratios(flat, valid)

        if method in _SIZE_FACTOR_METHODS:
            with np.errstate(invalid="ignore", divide="ignore"):
                normalized = np.where(
                    valid, flat / size_factors[:, None], np.nan,
                )
        elif method == "quantile":
            normalized = _quantile(flat, valid)
        else:  # zscore
            normalized = _zscore(flat, valid)

        pos_to_label = {v: k for k, v in form.labels[dim].items()}
        masked_before = np.where(valid, flat, np.nan)
        masked_after = np.where(valid, normalized, np.nan)

        per_sample = {}
        for i in range(n_samples):
            label = pos_to_label[i]
            before_row = masked_before[i]
            after_row = masked_after[i]
            finite_before = before_row[np.isfinite(before_row)]
            finite_after = after_row[np.isfinite(after_row)]

            entry = {
                "sum_before": float(np.nansum(before_row)),
                "sum_after": float(np.nansum(after_row)),
                "min_before": (
                    float(finite_before.min())
                    if finite_before.size else None
                ),
                "min_after": (
                    float(finite_after.min())
                    if finite_after.size else None
                ),
                "max_before": (
                    float(finite_before.max())
                    if finite_before.size else None
                ),
                "max_after": (
                    float(finite_after.max())
                    if finite_after.size else None
                ),
                "mean_before": (
                    float(finite_before.mean())
                    if finite_before.size else None
                ),
                "mean_after": (
                    float(finite_after.mean())
                    if finite_after.size else None
                ),
                "std_before": (
                    float(finite_before.std())
                    if finite_before.size else None
                ),
                "std_after": (
                    float(finite_after.std())
                    if finite_after.size else None
                ),
            }
            if size_factors is not None:
                entry["size_factor"] = float(size_factors[i])
            per_sample[label] = entry

        normalized = normalized.reshape(moved.shape)
        normalized = np.moveaxis(normalized, 0, axis).astype(np.float32)

        info, axis_info = project_metadata(form, derived=True)
        info.update(missing_code=np.nan, representation="normalize")
        ctx[out_name] = Form(
            data=normalized,
            dims=list(form.dims),
            labels={k: dict(v) for k, v in form.labels.items()},
            info=info,
            axis_info=axis_info,
        )

        self._export_data = {
            "method": method,
            "dim": dim,
            "target_sum": target_sum if method == "cpm" else None,
            "n_samples": int(n_samples),
            "per_sample": per_sample,
        }

        ctx.metadata.setdefault(self.name, {})[out_name] = {
            "method": method,
            "dim": dim,
            "n_samples": int(n_samples),
        }

        if size_factors is not None:
            print(
                f"[normalize] method='{method}' dim='{dim}' "
                f"n_samples={n_samples} "
                f"size factors [{size_factors.min():.4g}, "
                f"{size_factors.max():.4g}]"
            )
        else:
            print(
                f"[normalize] method='{method}' dim='{dim}' "
                f"n_samples={n_samples}"
            )

    def export(self, ctx: Context) -> dict:
        return self._export_data


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# Size-factor methods
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

def _cpm(flat, valid, target_sum):
    masked = np.where(valid, flat, 0.0)
    totals = masked.sum(axis=1)
    bad = np.where(totals <= 0)[0]
    if bad.size:
        raise StepError(
            f"normalize/cpm: {bad.size} sample(s) have zero total "
            f"count and cannot be normalized "
            f"(positions {bad.tolist()[:5]})."
        )
    return totals / target_sum


def _total(flat, valid):
    masked = np.where(valid, flat, 0.0)
    totals = masked.sum(axis=1)
    bad = np.where(totals <= 0)[0]
    if bad.size:
        raise StepError(
            f"normalize/total: {bad.size} sample(s) have zero total "
            f"count and cannot be normalized "
            f"(positions {bad.tolist()[:5]})."
        )
    return totals


def _median_of_ratios(flat, valid):
    """DESeq2-style size factors.

    For each gene, compute the geometric mean of its valid positive
    counts. A gene is eligible only if all of its valid counts are
    strictly positive. For each sample, the size factor is the median
    over eligible genes of (count / geometric mean).
    """
    positive = valid & (flat > 0)
    valid_zero = valid & (flat == 0)
    has_any_valid = valid.any(axis=0)
    has_valid_zero = valid_zero.any(axis=0)
    eligible = has_any_valid & ~has_valid_zero

    if not eligible.any():
        raise StepError(
            "normalize/median_of_ratios: no gene has all-positive "
            "counts; cannot compute size factors."
        )

    eligible_flat = flat[:, eligible]
    eligible_valid = valid[:, eligible]
    masked = np.where(eligible_valid, eligible_flat, np.nan)

    with np.errstate(invalid="ignore", divide="ignore"):
        log_vals = np.log(masked)
        log_mean = np.nanmean(log_vals, axis=0)
        geo_means = np.exp(log_mean)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(geo_means)):
        raise StepError(
            "normalize/median_of_ratios: geometric means are not "
            "finite for some eligible genes."
        )

    with np.errstate(invalid="ignore", divide="ignore"):
        ratios = masked / geo_means

    with np.errstate(invalid="ignore"):
        size_factors = np.nanmedian(ratios, axis=1)

    bad = ~np.isfinite(size_factors) | (size_factors <= 0)
    if bad.any():
        raise StepError(
            f"normalize/median_of_ratios: {bad.sum()} sample(s) have "
            f"invalid size factors (positions "
            f"{np.where(bad)[0].tolist()[:5]})."
        )

    return size_factors


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# Quantile normalization
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

def _quantile(flat, valid):
    """Quantile normalization.

    Rows of ``flat`` are samples, columns are features. For each rank
    position k (0-indexed), the target value is the mean of the k-th
    smallest valid value across all samples. Each sample's sorted
    values are then replaced by these target values.

    When samples have different numbers of valid values, the ranking
    depth is the minimum valid count across samples. The remaining
    values in a sample keep the last target value.
    """
    n_samples, n_features = flat.shape
    result = np.full_like(flat, np.nan, dtype=np.float64)

    n_valid = valid.sum(axis=1)
    if n_valid.min() == 0:
        raise StepError(
            "normalize/quantile: a sample has no valid values."
        )

    n_q = int(n_valid.min())

    sorted_trimmed = np.full((n_samples, n_q), np.nan, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(n_samples):
        vals = flat[i][valid[i]]
        sorted_trimmed[i] = np.sort(vals)[:n_q]

    target = sorted_trimmed.mean(axis=0)

    for i in range(n_samples):
        vals = flat[i]
        vmask = valid[i]
        n = int(vmask.sum())
        order = np.argsort(vals[vmask])
        if n <= n_q:
            new_vals = target[:n]
        else:
            tail = np.full(n - n_q, target[-1])
            new_vals = np.concatenate([target, tail])
        filled = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        filled[order] = new_vals
        result[i, vmask] = filled

    return result


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# Z-score
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

def _zscore(flat, valid):
    masked = np.where(valid, flat, np.nan)
    with np.errstate(invalid="ignore"):
        mu = np.nanmean(masked, axis=1, keepdims=True)
        sd = np.nanstd(masked, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    sd = np.where(sd > 1e-12, sd, 1.0)
    with np.errstate(invalid="ignore", divide="ignore"):
        normalized = (flat - mu) / sd
    return np.where(valid, normalized, np.nan)
===== src/task/step/steps/aggregate.py =====
# src/task/step/steps/aggregate.py
"""Aggregate positions along one axis by group.

Two inputs share an axis (the "row" axis). The first input carries
values; the second carries a group label for each position. For
each group, the operator collapses all positions in that group
into a single row by applying a reduction (sum, mean, median, ...).

This is the natural companion of ``join``: where ``join`` attaches
metadata columns to a form, ``aggregate`` reduces a form by a
metadata column. Typical uses:

    - collapse species to genus before plotting composition
    - collapse cells to clusters before profiling
    - collapse genes to pathways before scoring
    - collapse any [row, other] matrix by a [row, group] table

Layout
------
    data    dims: [row_dim, other_dim]   (any two axes)
    groups  dims: [row_dim, group_dim]   (same row_dim first)
    output  dims: [group_dim, other_dim]

Both inputs must be 2D with ``dim`` as their first axis. To use a
form whose shared axis is second, transpose it first.

Reductions
----------
    sum       sum of values in the group
    mean      mean of values in the group
    median    median of values in the group
    min       minimum
    max       maximum
    count     number of non-missing values in the group

Missing cells are excluded before the reduction, except for
``count`` where they are dropped from the count.

Group labels
------------
The group column can be a numeric column (each value is used as a
group key) or an encoded string column (the encoder's inverse map
is used to recover group names). The output group labels are the
recovered names, sorted by first appearance in the input order.

Params:
    dim        : shared dim name (required)
    group_col  : column label on the groups form (required)
    method     : reduction name (default "sum")
    output     : context key for the result (default: step name)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np

from src.core import Form, StepError
from src.task.context import Context
from src.task.step.base import Step
from src.task.step.steps._microbiome import CONTRACTS, SCHEMAS, estimate, metadata


_METHODS = ("sum", "mean", "median", "min", "max", "count")


class AggregateStep(Step):
    """Aggregate positions along one axis by group."""

    name = "aggregate"
    inputs_arity = 2
    outputs = []
    title = 'Aggregate'
    category = "Microbiome"
    builtin_package = "biopipe-microbiome"
    legacy_parameters = False
    parameters_schema = SCHEMAS['aggregate']
    input_contracts, output_contract = CONTRACTS['aggregate']
    output_parameter = "output"

    @classmethod
    def estimate_memory(cls, inputs, params, profiles):
        return estimate(cls.name, inputs, params, profiles)

    def run(self, ctx: Context, inputs) -> None:
        data_form, groups_form = inputs
        dim = ctx.step_params.get("dim")
        group_col = ctx.step_params.get("group_col")
        method = ctx.step_params.get("method", "sum")
        out_name = ctx.step_params.get("output", self.name)

        if not dim:
            raise StepError("aggregate: 'dim' is required")
        if not group_col:
            raise StepError("aggregate: 'group_col' is required")
        if method not in _METHODS:
            raise StepError(
                f"aggregate: method must be one of {list(_METHODS)}, "
                f"got {method!r}"
            )
        if data_form.data.ndim != 2:
            raise StepError(
                f"aggregate: data form must be 2D, "
                f"got ndim={data_form.data.ndim}"
            )
        if groups_form.data.ndim != 2:
            raise StepError(
                f"aggregate: groups form must be 2D, "
                f"got ndim={groups_form.data.ndim}"
            )
        if data_form.dims[0] != dim:
            raise StepError(
                f"aggregate: data's first dim must be '{dim}', "
                f"got '{data_form.dims[0]}'. Use transpose if needed."
            )
        if groups_form.dims[0] != dim:
            raise StepError(
                f"aggregate: groups' first dim must be '{dim}', "
                f"got '{groups_form.dims[0]}'. Use transpose if needed."
            )

        other_dim = data_form.dims[1]
        group_dim = groups_form.dims[1]

        if group_col not in groups_form.labels[group_dim]:
            raise StepError(
                f"aggregate: group_col '{group_col}' not in dim "
                f"'{group_dim}'. Available: "
                f"{sorted(groups_form.labels[group_dim])}"
            )

        data_row_labels = data_form.labels[dim]
        groups_row_labels = groups_form.labels[dim]
        common = [
            l for l in data_row_labels if l in groups_row_labels
        ]
        if not common:
            raise StepError(
                f"aggregate: no common row labels on '{dim}'"
            )

        data_idx = [data_row_labels[l] for l in common]
        group_idx = [groups_row_labels[l] for l in common]

        # Extract the group code for each shared row.
        col_pos = groups_form.labels[group_dim][group_col]
        group_codes_raw = groups_form.data[group_idx, col_pos]
        group_codes_raw = np.where(group_codes_raw == groups_form.info.get("missing_code", np.nan), np.nan, group_codes_raw)

        # Recover the human-readable group label.
        encoders = groups_form.info.get("encoders", {})
        encoder = None
        if group_dim in encoders and group_col in encoders[group_dim]:
            encoder = encoders[group_dim][group_col]

        group_names = [
            _decode(c, encoder) for c in group_codes_raw
        ]
        valid = np.array([g is not None for g in group_names])
        if not valid.any():
            raise StepError(
                "aggregate: no rows with a valid group value"
            )

        # Group order: first appearance in the input order.
        seen: dict[str, int] = {}
        ordered_groups: list[str] = []
        for g, v in zip(group_names, valid):
            if not v:
                continue
            if g not in seen:
                seen[g] = len(ordered_groups)
                ordered_groups.append(g)

        n_groups = len(ordered_groups)
        n_other = data_form.data.shape[1]
        out_data = np.full((n_groups, n_other), np.nan, dtype=np.float64)

        missing_code = data_form.info.get("missing_code", np.nan)
        if isinstance(missing_code, float) and np.isnan(missing_code):
            missing = np.isnan(data_form.data)
        else:
            missing = data_form.data == missing_code

        # Aggregate row by row.
        for gi, g in enumerate(ordered_groups):
            rows = [
                di for di, gn, v in
                zip(data_idx, group_names, valid)
                if v and gn == g
            ]
            if not rows:
                continue
            block = data_form.data[rows, :].astype(np.float64)
            block_missing = missing[rows, :]
            masked = np.where(block_missing, np.nan, block)

            with np.errstate(invalid="ignore", divide="ignore"):
                if method == "sum":
                    out_data[gi] = np.nansum(masked, axis=0)
                elif method == "mean":
                    out_data[gi] = np.nanmean(masked, axis=0)
                elif method == "median":
                    out_data[gi] = np.nanmedian(masked, axis=0)
                elif method == "min":
                    out_data[gi] = np.nanmin(masked, axis=0)
                elif method == "max":
                    out_data[gi] = np.nanmax(masked, axis=0)
                elif method == "count":
                    out_data[gi] = (~block_missing).sum(axis=0)

        out_labels = {
            group_dim: {g: i for i, g in enumerate(ordered_groups)},
            other_dim: dict(data_form.labels[other_dim]),
        }

        out_info, out_axes = metadata(data_form, [other_dim], "aggregate")
        ctx[out_name] = Form(
            data=out_data.astype(np.float32),
            dims=[group_dim, other_dim],
            labels=out_labels,
            info=out_info,
            axis_info=out_axes,
        )

        group_sizes = {}
        for g, v, gn in zip(ordered_groups, [True] * n_groups,
                            ordered_groups):
            group_sizes[g] = 0
        for g, v in zip(group_names, valid):
            if v:
                group_sizes[g] += 1

        self._export_data = {
            "dim": dim,
            "group_dim": group_dim,
            "other_dim": other_dim,
            "method": method,
            "group_col": group_col,
            "n_input_rows": len(common),
            "n_groups": n_groups,
            "n_other_features": n_other,
            "groups": ordered_groups,
            "group_sizes": group_sizes,
        }

        ctx.metadata.setdefault(self.name, {})[out_name] = {
            "dim": dim,
            "method": method,
            "n_groups": n_groups,
            "n_other_features": n_other,
        }

        print(
            f"[aggregate] dim='{dim}' group_col='{group_col}' "
            f"method='{method}' {len(common)} rows -> "
            f"{n_groups} groups x {n_other} features"
        )

    def export(self, ctx: Context) -> dict:
        return self._export_data


def _decode(code, encoder):
    if encoder is None:
        try:
            f = float(code)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return str(code)
        if not np.isfinite(f):
            return None
        return str(int(f)) if float(f).is_integer() else str(f)
    try:
        return str(encoder.inverse[int(code)])
    except (KeyError, ValueError, TypeError):
        return None

===== src/task/step/steps/order_axis.py =====
# src/task/step/steps/order_axis.py
"""Reorder every position along an axis by an info field.

Unlike ``prioritize``, which keeps only the top N positions,
``order_axis`` keeps every position and only changes their order.
The set of labels is unchanged; their positions along ``dim`` are
reassigned according to the ranking.

Typical uses: sort variants by genomic position before plotting,
sort samples by a covariate, sort features by variance before a
heatmap.

Layout
------
    input   dims: [..., dim, ...]
    output  dims: same as input, ``dim`` reordered

Params:
    dim        : dimension to reorder (required)
    by         : numeric info field declared parallel to ``dim``
                 (required)
    descending : True ranks largest first; False ranks smallest
                 first (default)
    output     : context key for the result (default: step name)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np

from src.core import Form, StepError
from src.core.contracts import param, port
from src.task.context import Context
from src.task.step.base import Step


class OrderAxisStep(Step):
    """Reorder an axis by a numeric info field, keeping all positions."""

    name = "order_axis"
    category = "data"
    icon = "SortAscending"
    inputs_arity = 1
    outputs = []
    input_contracts = [
        port(
            dims=[param("dim")],
            parallel=[{"fields": param("by"), "dim": param("dim")}],
        ),
    ]
    output_contract = {"mode": "unknown"}
    output_parameter = "output"
    parameters_schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "dim": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Dimension to reorder",
            },
            "by": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Ordering field",
                "description": (
                    "Numeric info field declared parallel to 'dim'."
                ),
            },
            "descending": {
                "type": "boolean",
                "title": "Descending",
                "default": False,
            },
            "output": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Output key",
            },
        },
        "required": ["dim", "by"],
    }

    def run(self, ctx: Context, inputs) -> None:
        form = inputs[0]
        dim = ctx.step_params.get("dim")
        by = ctx.step_params.get("by")
        descending = bool(ctx.step_params.get("descending", False))
        out_name = ctx.step_params.get("output", self.name)

        if not dim:
            raise StepError("order_axis: 'dim' is required")
        if not by:
            raise StepError("order_axis: 'by' is required")
        if dim not in form.dims:
            raise StepError(
                f"order_axis: dim '{dim}' not present. "
                f"Available: {form.dims}"
            )

        parallel = form.axis_info.get(dim, [])
        if by not in parallel:
            raise StepError(
                f"order_axis: field '{by}' is not declared parallel "
                f"to '{dim}'. Declared: {parallel}"
            )

        raw = form.info.get(by)
        if raw is None:
            raise StepError(f"order_axis: info['{by}'] is missing")

        values = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64)
        axis = form.dims.index(dim)
        size = form.data.shape[axis]
        if values.shape != (size,):
            raise StepError(
                f"order_axis: info['{by}'] has shape {values.shape}, "
                f"expected ({size},)"
            )

        finite = np.isfinite(values)
        if not finite.any():
            raise StepError(
                f"order_axis: info['{by}'] has no finite values"
            )

        missing_count = int((~finite).sum())

        sorted_vals = np.where(finite, values, np.nan)
        order = np.argsort(sorted_vals, kind="stable")
        if descending:
            order = order[::-1]
        finite_order = order[finite[order]]
        missing_order = order[~finite[order]]
        order = np.concatenate([finite_order, missing_order])

        new_form = form.subset_axis(dim, order)
        ctx[out_name] = new_form

        pos_to_label = {v: k for k, v in form.labels[dim].items()}
        self._export_data = {
            "dim": dim,
            "by": by,
            "descending": descending,
            "n_positions": int(size),
            "n_missing": missing_count,
            "order": [
                {
                    "position": i,
                    "label": pos_to_label[int(pos)],
                    "value": (
                        float(values[int(pos)])
                        if np.isfinite(values[int(pos)])
                        else None
                    ),
                }
                for i, pos in enumerate(order)
            ],
        }

        ctx.metadata.setdefault(self.name, {})[out_name] = {
            "dim": dim,
            "by": by,
            "descending": descending,
        }

        print(
            f"[order_axis] '{dim}' ordered by '{by}' "
            f"({'descending' if descending else 'ascending'}), "
            f"{size} positions"
        )

    def export(self, ctx: Context) -> dict:
        return self._export_data

===== src/task/step/steps/prioritize.py =====
# src/task/step/steps/prioritize.py
"""Keep the top positions along an axis, ranked by an info field.

Positions are sorted by a numeric info field declared parallel to
``dim``. The top ``top_n`` are retained. Their order in the output
matches their order in the source, so downstream label alignment
stays stable.

This pairs with ``annotate``: annotate writes reference columns into
``info``; prioritize selects the positions whose annotation matters
most.

Layout
------
    input   dims: [..., dim, ...]
    output  dims: same as input, with ``dim`` shortened to ``top_n``

Ranking
-------
Missing values (NaN) are ranked last regardless of direction.
Ties keep their source order (stable sort).

Params:
    dim        : dimension to sort along (required)
    by         : info field to rank by (required)
    top_n      : number of positions to keep (required)
    ascending  : False ranks largest first (default); True ranks
                 smallest first
    output     : context key for the result (default: step name)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np

from src.core import Form, StepError
from src.core.contracts import param, port
from src.task.context import Context
from src.task.step.base import Step


class PrioritizeStep(Step):
    """Keep the top positions along a dimension, ranked by an info field."""

    name = "prioritize"
    category = "data"
    icon = "Funnel"
    inputs_arity = 1
    outputs = []
    input_contracts = [
        port(
            dims=[param("dim")],
            parallel=[{"fields": param("by"), "dim": param("dim")}],
        ),
    ]
    output_contract = {"mode": "subset"}
    output_parameter = "output"
    parameters_schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "dim": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Dimension to sort",
                "description": "Axis along which positions are ranked.",
            },
            "by": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Ranking field",
                "description": (
                    "Numeric info field declared parallel to 'dim'. "
                    "Cannot be an encoded field."
                ),
            },
            "top_n": {
                "type": "integer",
                "title": "Top N",
                "minimum": 1,
            },
            "ascending": {
                "type": "boolean",
                "title": "Ascending",
                "default": False,
                "description": (
                    "False ranks largest first; True ranks smallest first."
                ),
            },
            "output": {
                "type": "string",
                "title": "Output key",
            },
        },
        "required": ["dim", "by", "top_n"],
    }

    def run(self, ctx: Context, inputs) -> None:
        form = inputs[0]
        dim = ctx.step_params.get("dim")
        by = ctx.step_params.get("by")
        top_n = ctx.step_params.get("top_n")
        ascending = bool(ctx.step_params.get("ascending", False))
        out_name = ctx.step_params.get("output", self.name)

        if not dim:
            raise StepError("prioritize: 'dim' is required")
        if not by:
            raise StepError("prioritize: 'by' is required")
        if top_n is None:
            raise StepError("prioritize: 'top_n' is required")

        if dim not in form.dims:
            raise StepError(
                f"prioritize: dim '{dim}' not present. "
                f"Available: {form.dims}"
            )

        parallel = form.axis_info.get(dim, [])
        if by not in parallel:
            raise StepError(
                f"prioritize: field '{by}' is not declared parallel "
                f"to '{dim}'. Declared: {parallel}"
            )

        encoders_for_dim = form.info.get("encoders", {}).get(dim, {})
        if by in encoders_for_dim:
            raise StepError(
                f"prioritize: field '{by}' is encoded; rank a "
                f"non-encoded numeric field"
            )

        raw = form.info.get(by)
        if raw is None:
            raise StepError(f"prioritize: info['{by}'] is missing")

        values = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64)
        axis = form.dims.index(dim)
        size = form.data.shape[axis]
        if values.shape != (size,):
            raise StepError(
                f"prioritize: info['{by}'] has shape {values.shape}, "
                f"expected ({size},)"
            )

        finite = np.isfinite(values)
        if not finite.any():
            raise StepError(
                f"prioritize: info['{by}'] has no finite values"
            )

        if top_n < 1:
            raise StepError(
                f"prioritize: top_n must be >= 1, got {top_n}"
            )
        if top_n > size:
            raise StepError(
                f"prioritize: top_n={top_n} exceeds dim size {size}"
            )

        sorted_vals = np.where(finite, values, np.nan)
        order = np.argsort(sorted_vals, kind="stable")
        if not ascending:
            order = order[::-1]
        finite_order = order[finite[order]]
        missing_order = order[~finite[order]]
        order = np.concatenate([finite_order, missing_order])

        kept = order[:top_n]
        kept_sorted = np.sort(kept)

        new_form = form.subset_axis(dim, kept_sorted)
        ctx[out_name] = new_form

        pos_to_label = {v: k for k, v in form.labels[dim].items()}
        kept_set = set(kept.tolist())
        rank_position = {
            int(pos): rank + 1 for rank, pos in enumerate(order)
        }

        self._export_data = {
            "dim": dim,
            "by": by,
            "ascending": ascending,
            "top_n": top_n,
            "n_before": int(size),
            "n_after": int(top_n),
            "kept_labels": [
                pos_to_label[int(i)] for i in kept_sorted
            ],
            "ranking": [
                {
                    "rank": rank_position[int(pos)],
                    "label": pos_to_label[int(pos)],
                    "value": (
                        float(values[int(pos)])
                        if np.isfinite(values[int(pos)])
                        else None
                    ),
                }
                for pos in order
                if int(pos) in kept_set
            ],
            "n_excluded": int(size - top_n),
        }

        ctx.metadata.setdefault(self.name, {})[out_name] = {
            "dim": dim,
            "by": by,
            "top_n": top_n,
        }

        print(
            f"[prioritize] '{dim}' ranked by '{by}' "
            f"({'ascending' if ascending else 'descending'}), "
            f"kept {top_n}/{size}"
        )

    def export(self, ctx: Context) -> dict:
        return self._export_data

===== biopipe_sdk/__init__.py =====
"""BioPipe SDK v1: stable imports for operators and readers.

Implementations remain in the engine for compatibility with existing projects.
Lazy exports keep plugin discovery independent of engine import order.
"""
from importlib import import_module

__version__ = "1.2.0"
PROTOCOL_VERSION = 1
_EXPORTS = {
    "Form": ("src.core", "Form"),
    "Step": ("src.task.step.base", "Step"),
    "Reader": ("src.reader.base", "Reader"),
    "DenseDiskWriter": ("src.reader.disk", "DenseDiskWriter"),
    "StreamInput": ("biopipe_sdk.resources", "StreamInput"),
    "SourceProbe": ("biopipe_sdk.resources", "SourceProbe"),
    "Operator": ("biopipe_sdk.operator", "Operator"),
    "Context": ("src.task.context.base", "Context"),
    "StepError": ("src.core", "StepError"),
    "ReaderError": ("src.core", "ReaderError"),
    "port": ("src.core.contracts", "port"),
    "param": ("src.core.contracts", "param"),
    "MISSING": ("src.core.contracts", "MISSING"),
    "reader_contract": ("src.core.contracts", "reader_contract"),
    "project_metadata": ("src.core.forms.metadata", "project_metadata"),
    "concat_metadata": ("src.core.forms.metadata", "concat_metadata"),
}
__all__ = [*_EXPORTS, "PROTOCOL_VERSION", "__version__"]


def __getattr__(name):
    if name not in _EXPORTS:
        raise AttributeError(name)
    module, attribute = _EXPORTS[name]
    value = getattr(import_module(module), attribute)
    globals()[name] = value
    return value

===== biopipe_sdk/protocol.py =====
"""JSON-compatible public descriptors and parameter validation."""
from copy import deepcopy
from typing import Any, Literal

from jsonschema import Draft202012Validator
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator

PROTOCOL_VERSION = 1


class Descriptor(BaseModel):
    # Legacy UI fields are retained as extension fields in catalog v1.
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="allow")
    protocol_version: Literal[1] = 1
    id: str = Field(pattern=r"^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,79}$")
    version: str = Field(pattern=r"^\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?:[-+][A-Za-z0-9.-]+)?$")
    kind: Literal["operator", "reader"]
    entry_point: str
    title: str
    category: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Operator library category, independent of package origin. Custom labels are allowed.")
    icon: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Optional module-relative image path or Studio icon name. Missing or unknown names use the category default.")
    icon_image: str | None = Field(default=None, pattern=r"^data:image/(png|jpeg|webp|gif|svg\+xml);base64,[A-Za-z0-9+/]+={0,2}$", description="SDK-generated portable image data; authors declare a relative path in icon.")
    parameters_schema: dict[str, Any]
    contracts: dict[str, Any]
    resources: dict[str, Any]
    dependencies: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="PEP 508 dependencies; compatible versions share the host runtime, conflicting versions use a private dependency directory and subprocess with the same Python.")
    dependency_imports: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Optional import smoke checks, run outside the UI process.")

    @field_validator('dependencies')
    @classmethod
    def validate_dependencies(cls, value):
        from .dependencies import requirements
        return requirements(value)

    @field_validator('dependency_imports')
    @classmethod
    def validate_imports(cls, value):
        from .dependencies import imports
        return imports(value)


class PackageDescriptor(BaseModel):
    """One installation unit; operator identities remain independent of origin."""
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="allow")
    protocol_version: Literal[1] = 1
    name: str = Field(pattern=r"^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9.-]*$")
    version: str
    title: str | None = None
    origin: Literal["bundled", "wheel"] = "wheel"
    descriptors: list[Descriptor]


def validate_schema(schema):
    Draft202012Validator.check_schema(schema)
    if schema.get("type") != "object":
        raise ValueError("Parameter schema must describe an object")
    # Schemas are self-contained, so validation never fetches remote documents.
    def check(value):
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            for reference in ("$ref", "$dynamicRef"):
                if reference in value and not value[reference].startswith("#"):
                    raise ValueError("Only local JSON Schema references are supported")
            for child in value.values():
                check(child)
        elif isinstance(value, list):
            for child in value:
                check(child)
    check(schema)


def validate_parameters(schema, params):
    validate_schema(schema)
    result = deepcopy(params)
    for name, field in schema.get("properties", {}).items():
        if name not in result and "default" in field:
            result[name] = deepcopy(field["default"])
    errors = sorted(Draft202012Validator(schema).iter_errors(result), key=lambda error: str(error.path))
    if errors:
        raise ValueError("; ".join(f"{'.'.join(map(str, e.path)) or 'params'}: {e.message}" for e in errors))
    return result


def schema_to_fields(schema):
    result = []
    for key, value in schema.get("properties", {}).items():
        kind = value.get("type", "object")
        if isinstance(kind, list):
            concrete = [item for item in kind if item != "null"]
            kind = value.get("x-ui-type", concrete[0] if len(concrete) == 1 else "object")
        field = {"key": key, "label": value.get("title", key),
                 "type": "choice" if "enum" in value else {"array": "json", "object": "json"}.get(kind, kind),
                 "required": key in schema.get("required", [])}
        if value.get("description"):
            field["description"] = value["description"]
        if value.get("type") == "array" and value.get("items", {}).get("type") == "string":
            field["type"] = "list"
        if not isinstance(field["type"], str):
            field["type"] = "json"
        for source, target in (("default", "default"), ("minimum", "min"), ("maximum", "max"), ("enum", "options")):
            if source in value:
                field[target] = deepcopy(value[source])
        result.append(field)
    return result


def legacy_schema(fields, defaults):
    properties = {}
    for field in fields:
        kind = field["type"]
        value = {"title": field.get("label", field["key"])}
        types = {"list": "array", "scalar": ["string", "number"], "json": ["object", "array"]}
        if kind == "choice":
            value["enum"] = field.get("options", [])
        else:
            value["type"] = types.get(kind, kind)
        for source, target in (("min", "minimum"), ("max", "maximum")):
            if source in field:
                value[target] = field[source]
        if field["key"] in defaults:
            value["default"] = defaults[field["key"]]
        properties[field["key"]] = value
    # Existing manifests can contain additional engine parameters. The adapter
    # advertises them without tightening historical validation behavior.
    return {"$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema", "type": "object",
            "properties": properties, "additionalProperties": True,
            "required": [f["key"] for f in fields if f.get("required") and f["key"] not in defaults]}


def describe_operator(cls, legacy=None):
    packaged = getattr(cls, "_package_descriptor", None)
    value = deepcopy(packaged or legacy or {})
    schema = getattr(cls, "parameters_schema", None)
    if schema is not None:
        validate_schema(schema)
        if not packaged:
            value["parameters"] = schema_to_fields(schema)
            value["runtime_defaults"] = {key: field["default"] for key, field in schema.get("properties", {}).items() if "default" in field}
    else:
        schema = legacy_schema(value.get("parameters", []), value.get("runtime_defaults", {}))
    output_parameter = getattr(cls, "output_parameter", None)
    value.update(protocol_version=1, id=cls.name, version=getattr(cls, "version", "1.0.0"), kind="operator",
                 entry_point=f"{cls.__module__}:{cls.__name__}", module=cls.__module__,
                 title=getattr(cls, "title", None) or value.get("title", cls.name),
                 parameters_schema=schema, name=cls.name, inputs_arity=cls.inputs_arity,
                 outputs=list(cls.outputs), output_parameter=output_parameter,
                 legacy_parameters=bool(getattr(cls, "legacy_parameters", False)),
                 contracts={"version": 1, "inputs": cls.input_contracts, "output": cls.output_contract},
                 resources={"memory_modes": ["full", "stream"] if cls.supports_streaming else ["full"],
                            "supports_cuda": bool(getattr(cls, "supports_cuda", False)),
                            "estimate": "estimate_memory",
                            **({"stream_protocol": cls.stream_protocol,
                                "stream_buffer_multiplier": cls.stream_buffer_multiplier,
                                "stream_fixed_bytes": cls.stream_fixed_bytes} if cls.supports_streaming else {})})
    # Declarations travel through source scans and wheel manifests alike. A base
    # class's None defaults must not erase older packaged presentation metadata.
    for field in ("category", "icon"):
        declared = getattr(cls, field, None)
        if declared is not None:
            value[field] = declared
    if value.get("category") is None:
        value["category"] = "Plugins"
    from biopipe_sdk.icons import resolve_icon
    resolve_icon(cls, value)
    from biopipe_sdk.dependencies import declarations
    value.update(declarations(cls, value))
    value.setdefault("description", (cls.__doc__ or "").strip())
    value.setdefault("input_type", "Form")
    value.setdefault("output_type", "Form" if cls.outputs or output_parameter else "Report")
    value.update(memory_modes=value["resources"]["memory_modes"], supports_cuda=value["resources"]["supports_cuda"])
    # Preserve the shape of previously built wheel manifests: optional display
    # fields that were never declared must not suddenly appear as null.
    return Descriptor.model_validate(value).model_dump(exclude_unset=True)


def describe_reader(reader):
    cls = type(reader)
    from biopipe_sdk.dependencies import declarations
    schema = getattr(cls, "parameters_schema", None) or {"type": "object", "additionalProperties": True}
    validate_schema(schema)
    return Descriptor(protocol_version=1, id=getattr(cls, "name", None) or cls.__name__,
                      version=getattr(cls, "version", "1.0.0"), kind="reader",
                      entry_point=f"{cls.__module__}:{cls.__name__}", title=getattr(cls, "title", None) or cls.__name__,
                      parameters_schema=schema, contracts={"version": 1, "inputs": [], "output": reader.output_contract},
                      parameters=schema_to_fields(schema),
                      resources={"memory_modes": ["full", "disk"] if reader.supports_disk_read else ["full"], "supports_cuda": False,
                                 **({"disk_protocol": reader.disk_protocol} if reader.supports_disk_read else {})},
                      name=cls.__name__, module=cls.__module__, extensions=list(reader.extensions),
                      requires_siblings=list(reader.requires_siblings), excludes_siblings=list(reader.excludes_siblings),
                      handles_compression=reader.handles_compression,
                      **({"file_input": deepcopy(reader.file_input)} if reader.file_input is not None else {}),
                      output_contract=reader.output_contract, **declarations(cls, getattr(cls, '_package_descriptor', None))).model_dump(exclude_unset=True)


def validate_output_rule(rule):
    modes = {"unknown", "declare", "project", "choose", "passthrough", "transform", "subset", "select", "drop", "rename", "transpose", "concat", "attach", "report", "none", "pca", "regress", "correction", "compare_groups", "cox_regression", "logrank_test"}
    if not isinstance(rule, dict) or rule.get("mode") not in modes:
        raise ValueError("Use a documented output contract mode")
    if rule["mode"] == "choose":
        if not isinstance(rule.get("param"), str) or not rule.get("cases"):
            raise ValueError("choose requires a parameter and non-empty cases")
        for case in rule["cases"].values():
            validate_output_rule(case)
        if "otherwise" in rule:
            validate_output_rule(rule["otherwise"])
    if rule["mode"] == "project":
        for axis in rule.get("prepend_dims", []) + rule.get("append_dims", []):
            if not isinstance(axis, dict) or not ("name" in axis or "input_axis" in axis):
                raise ValueError("Added dimensions require name or input_axis")
            ref = axis.get("input_axis")
            if ref is not None and (not isinstance(ref, list) or len(ref) != 2 or any(type(n) is not int or n < 0 for n in ref)):
                raise ValueError("input_axis must be [input_index, axis_index]")


def validate_port_rule(rule):
    if not isinstance(rule, dict):
        raise ValueError("Port contracts must be objects")
    if "numeric" in rule and not isinstance(rule["numeric"], bool):
        raise ValueError("Port numeric must be boolean")
    if rule.get("ndim") is not None and (type(rule["ndim"]) is not int or rule["ndim"] < 0):
        raise ValueError("Port ndim must be a nonnegative integer")
    if not isinstance(rule.get("info", {}), dict):
        raise ValueError("Port info must be an object")
    for key in ("dims", "labels", "parallel", "labels_any", "labels_axis"):
        value = rule.get(key, [])
        if not isinstance(value, list) and not (isinstance(value, dict) and isinstance(value.get("param"), str)):
            raise ValueError(f"Port {key} must be a list or parameter reference")


def validate_extension(cls, kind):
    """Strict authoring requirements for new extensions; legacy adapters stay permissive."""
    from src.task.step.base import Step
    from src.reader.base import Reader
    base = Step if kind == "operator" else Reader
    if not isinstance(cls, type) or not issubclass(cls, base):
        raise ValueError(f"Plugin entry must subclass {base.__name__}")
    if cls.parameters_schema is None:
        raise ValueError("Plugins must declare parameters_schema, including readers with no options")
    validate_schema(cls.parameters_schema)
    if not cls.output_contract:
        raise ValueError("Plugins must declare output_contract")
    if kind == "operator":
        if cls.output_parameter not in (None, "output"):
            raise ValueError("Plugin v1 supports only the dynamic output parameter 'output'")
        arity = cls.inputs_arity
        if arity != "*" and (type(arity) is not int or arity < 0 or arity > 20):
            raise ValueError("inputs_arity must be an integer from 0 to 20 or '*'")
        if cls.input_contracts is None or len(cls.input_contracts) != (1 if arity == "*" else arity):
            raise ValueError("Declare one input contract per port (one repeat contract for '*')")
        for contract in cls.input_contracts:
            validate_port_rule(contract)
        validate_output_rule(cls.output_contract)
        if cls.output_contract["mode"] not in {"report", "none", "unknown"} and not (cls.outputs or cls.output_parameter):
            raise ValueError("Form-producing operators must declare outputs or output_parameter")
        if cls.supports_streaming:
            if (cls.stream_protocol != "flat-reduction-v1" or cls.inputs_arity != 1
                    or cls.output_contract.get("mode") not in {"report", "none"}
                    or cls.outputs or cls.output_parameter or cls.run_stream is Step.run_stream):
                raise ValueError("Streaming requires flat-reduction-v1, one input, report-only output and run_stream")
            if (type(cls.stream_buffer_multiplier) is not int or cls.stream_buffer_multiplier < 1
                    or type(cls.stream_fixed_bytes) is not int or cls.stream_fixed_bytes < 0):
                raise ValueError("Stream buffer multiplier and fixed workspace must be valid integer estimates")
    else:
        validate_port_rule(cls.output_contract)
        if cls.supports_disk_read:
            if (cls.disk_protocol != "dense-writer-v1" or cls.probe is Reader.probe
                    or cls.read_to_disk is Reader.read_to_disk):
                raise ValueError("Disk readers require dense-writer-v1, probe and read_to_disk")
        if not cls.extensions or any(not isinstance(ext, str) or not ext.startswith(".") for ext in cls.extensions):
            raise ValueError("Readers must declare file extensions such as '.npy'")

===== biopipe_sdk/operator.py =====
"""Small function-oriented operator API layered over the compatible Step API."""
from abc import abstractmethod
from src.task.step.base import Step
from src.core import Form, StepError


class Operator(Step):
    """Implement compute(inputs, params, context) and return one Form or a report.

    Set output_parameter=None and output_contract={"mode": "report"} for a
    report-only operator. Use Step directly for legacy multi-output behavior.
    """
    inputs_arity = 1
    output_parameter = "output"
    parameters_schema = {"type": "object", "properties": {}, "additionalProperties": False}

    @abstractmethod
    def compute(self, inputs, params, context):
        """Return a new Form, or a JSON-compatible report for report operators."""

    def run(self, ctx, inputs):
        params = {key: value for key, value in ctx.step_params.items() if key not in ("output", "_input_names")}
        result = self.compute(inputs, params, ctx)
        if self.output_parameter:
            if not isinstance(result, Form):
                raise StepError(f"{self.name}: compute() must return a Form")
            ctx[str(ctx.step_params.get(self.output_parameter, self.name))] = result
            self._report = {"shape": list(result.data.shape), "dims": result.dims}
        else:
            self._report = result or {}

    def export(self, ctx):
        return self._report

===== biopipe_sdk/resources.py =====
"""Resource protocol 1: bounded numeric streams and two-dimensional disk input."""
from typing import Iterator, TypedDict
import math
import numpy as np


class StreamInput(TypedDict):
    shape: tuple[int, ...]
    metadata: dict
    chunks: Iterator[np.ndarray]


class SourceProbe(TypedDict):
    file_bytes: int
    shape: list[int]
    dtype: str
    dense_bytes: int
    preparation_bytes: int


def validate_probe(probe):
    """Validate an allocation promise before the writer allocates disk space."""
    shape = probe.get("shape")
    if not isinstance(shape, (tuple, list)) or len(shape) != 2 or any(type(n) is not int or n < 0 for n in shape):
        raise ValueError("dense-writer-v1 probe.shape must contain two non-negative integer dimensions")
    dtype = np.dtype(probe.get("dtype"))
    if dtype.kind not in "biuf":
        raise ValueError("dense-writer-v1 requires a fixed-width real numeric dtype")
    size = math.prod(shape) * dtype.itemsize
    if type(probe.get("dense_bytes")) is not int or probe["dense_bytes"] != size:
        raise ValueError("probe.dense_bytes must equal product(shape) * dtype.itemsize")
    if type(probe.get("preparation_bytes", size)) is not int or probe.get("preparation_bytes", size) < size:
        raise ValueError("probe.preparation_bytes must include the output and temporary disk storage")
    return probe

===== biopipe_sdk/dependencies.py =====
"""Portable dependency declarations shared by scanning, packaging and startup."""
import ast
import re
from packaging.requirements import Requirement


def requirements(values):
    if not isinstance(values, list) or any(not isinstance(v, str) or '\n' in v or '\r' in v for v in values):
        raise ValueError('dependencies must be a list of PEP 508 requirement strings')
    result = []
    for value in values:
        requirement = Requirement(value)
        if requirement.url:
            raise ValueError('Dependency URLs and local paths are not supported; use package names and version ranges')
        result.append(str(requirement))
    return sorted(set(result))


def imports(values):
    if not isinstance(values, list) or any(not isinstance(v, str) or not re.fullmatch(r'[A-Za-z_]\w*(?:\.[A-Za-z_]\w*)*', v) for v in values):
        raise ValueError('dependency_imports must contain Python module names')
    return sorted(set(values))


def declarations(cls, previous=None):
    result = {}
    for key, validate in (('dependencies', requirements), ('dependency_imports', imports)):
        value = getattr(cls, key, None)
        if value is None:
            value = (previous or {}).get(key)
        if value is not None:
            result[key] = validate(value)
    return result


def source_requirements(source):
    """Read literal metadata before importing code that needs the dependency."""
    tree = ast.parse(source)
    values = []
    for parent in [tree, *[n for n in tree.body if isinstance(n, ast.ClassDef)]]:
        for node in parent.body:
            targets = node.targets if isinstance(node, ast.Assign) else [node.target] if isinstance(node, ast.AnnAssign) else []
            for target in targets:
                if not isinstance(target, ast.Name):
                    continue
                if isinstance(parent, ast.ClassDef) and target.id == 'dependencies':
                    value = ast.literal_eval(node.value)
                    if value is not None:
                        values.extend(requirements(value))
                elif parent is tree and target.id == 'BIOPIPE_PLUGIN':
                    values.extend(requirements(ast.literal_eval(node.value).get('dependencies', [])))
    return sorted(set(values))


def source_entries(source, module):
    """Literal per-class dependencies for direct SDK subclasses in a .py drop-in.

    Complex/dynamic modules retain the module entry-point fallback. Discovery
    still imports and validates the real classes in a disposable process.
    """
    tree = ast.parse(source)
    aliases = {'Step': 'operator', 'Operator': 'operator', 'Reader': 'reader'}
    for node in tree.body:
        if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom) and node.module in ('biopipe_sdk', 'src.task.step.base', 'src.reader.base'):
            for alias in node.names:
                if alias.name in aliases:
                    aliases[alias.asname or alias.name] = aliases[alias.name]
    result = []
    for node in tree.body:
        if not isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef):
            continue
        kinds = [aliases[b.id] for b in node.bases if isinstance(b, ast.Name) and b.id in aliases]
        if not kinds:
            return []  # Inheritance may hide another extension; use discovery.
        dependencies = []
        for field in node.body:
            targets = field.targets if isinstance(field, ast.Assign) else [field.target] if isinstance(field, ast.AnnAssign) else []
            if any(isinstance(t, ast.Name) and t.id == 'dependencies' for t in targets):
                value = ast.literal_eval(field.value)
                dependencies = requirements(value) if value is not None else []
        result.append({'entry': module + ':' + node.name, 'kind': kinds[0], 'dependencies': dependencies})
    return result

===== biopipe_sdk/testing.py =====
"""Contract-aware helpers for plugin authors' small, deterministic fixtures."""
from pathlib import Path
from tempfile import TemporaryDirectory


def run_operator(cls, inputs, params=None):
    """Run a plugin through real engine contracts, returning its Form/report.

    This helper uses small in-memory fixtures. It does not replace resource,
    performance or scientific correctness testing on representative data.
    """
    from src.task import Context, STEPS, run
    with TemporaryDirectory(prefix="biopipe-sdk-") as temporary:
        names = [f"input_{i}" for i in range(len(inputs))]
        spec = {"name": cls.name, "inputs": names, "params": dict(params or {})}
        ctx = Context(Path(temporary), {"sources": {}, "steps": [spec]})
        previous = STEPS.get(cls.name)
        STEPS[cls.name] = cls
        try:
            for name, form in zip(names, inputs):
                ctx[name] = form
            run(ctx, [spec])
            if cls.output_parameter:
                return ctx[spec["params"].get(cls.output_parameter, cls.name)]
            import json
            return json.loads((ctx.out_dir / ctx.artifacts["exports"][0]["export_path"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        finally:
            if previous is None:
                STEPS.pop(cls.name, None)
            else:
                STEPS[cls.name] = previous
            ctx.finalize()

===== biopipe_sdk/icons.py =====
"""Portable display assets, resolved during discovery rather than by the GUI."""
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import inspect
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
import re
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET

IMAGE_TYPES = {".png": "image/png", ".jpg": "image/jpeg", ".jpeg": "image/jpeg",
               ".webp": "image/webp", ".gif": "image/gif", ".svg": "image/svg+xml"}


class _StaticSVGBuilder(ET.TreeBuilder):
    def doctype(self, name, pubid, system):
        raise ValueError("icon SVG must not contain a DTD or entities")


def is_image_icon(value):
    return isinstance(value, str) and ("/" in value or "\\" in value or
                                      Path(value).suffix.lower() in IMAGE_TYPES)


def icon_path(directory, value):
    """Only module-relative, contained files; never a developer-machine path."""
    relative = PurePosixPath(value.replace("\\", "/"))
    if relative.is_absolute() or ".." in relative.parts or ":" in value:
        raise ValueError("icon must be a relative image path inside the operator module directory")
    if relative.suffix.lower() not in IMAGE_TYPES:
        raise ValueError("icon image must be PNG, JPEG, WebP, GIF or SVG")
    path = (Path(directory) / relative).resolve()
    if not path.is_relative_to(Path(directory).resolve()):
        raise ValueError("icon image must stay inside the operator module directory")
    if not path.is_file():
        raise ValueError(f"icon image not found: {value}")
    return path


def image_data(path):
    data = path.read_bytes()
    suffix = path.suffix.lower()
    if suffix == ".svg":
        # SVG is used as an image, never injected as HTML. Also reject active or
        # external content so author assets are self-contained and work offline.
        if re.search(br"<!\s*(?:DOCTYPE|ENTITY)", data, re.I):
            raise ValueError("icon SVG must not contain a DTD or entities")
        try:
            root = ET.fromstring(data, parser=ET.XMLParser(target=_StaticSVGBuilder()))
        except ET.ParseError as exc:
            raise ValueError("icon SVG is not valid XML") from exc
        allowed = {"svg", "g", "defs", "path", "circle", "ellipse", "rect", "line", "polyline",
                   "polygon", "title", "desc", "text", "tspan", "use", "symbol", "linearGradient",
                   "radialGradient", "stop", "clipPath", "mask", "pattern"}
        if root.tag not in {"svg", "{http://www.w3.org/2000/svg}svg"}:
            raise ValueError("icon SVG requires an svg root")
        for element in root.iter():
            tag = element.tag.removeprefix("{http://www.w3.org/2000/svg}")
            if tag not in allowed:
                raise ValueError(f"icon SVG element is not supported: {tag}")
            for name, value in element.attrib.items():
                name = name.rsplit("}", 1)[-1].lower()
                urls = re.findall(r"url\s*\((.*?)\)", value, re.I)
                if (name.startswith("on") or name == "base" or
                    name == "href" and not value.startswith("#") or
                    any(not url.strip(" \t\r\n\"'").startswith("#") for url in urls) or
                    re.search(r"@import|javascript:|\\", value, re.I)):
                    raise ValueError("icon SVG must contain only static, local artwork")
    else:
        valid = ((suffix == ".png" and data.startswith(b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n")) or
                 (suffix in {".jpg", ".jpeg"} and data.startswith(b"\xff\xd8\xff")) or
                 (suffix == ".gif" and data[:6] in {b"GIF87a", b"GIF89a"}) or
                 (suffix == ".webp" and data[:4] == b"RIFF" and data[8:12] == b"WEBP"))
        if not valid:
            raise ValueError(f"icon image content does not match {suffix}")
    return f"data:{IMAGE_TYPES[suffix]};base64," + base64.b64encode(data).decode("ascii")


def resolve_icon(cls, descriptor):
    """Freeze bytes into the descriptor; saved generations need no source paths."""
    value = descriptor.get("icon")
    descriptor.pop("icon_image", None)
    if is_image_icon(value):
        path = icon_path(Path(inspect.getfile(cls)).parent, value)
        descriptor["icon_image"] = image_data(path)

===== biopipe/registry.py =====
"""Explicit, reproducible registry maintenance; never run during GUI startup.

Local source discovery imports trusted repository code. Upstream discovery only
reads Git blobs; it neither executes nor merges upstream implementations.
"""
from copy import deepcopy
from hashlib import sha256
import argparse
import ast
import importlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path, PureWindowsPath
import subprocess
import sys

import yaml

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PACKAGE_INDEX = Path("biopipe/builtin_packages.json")
ROUTE_INDEX = Path("src/web/builtin_routes.json")


def read_json(path, default):
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if path.is_file() else deepcopy(default)


def scan_sources(root=ROOT, sources=None):
    """Import fresh declarations, collect every error, and reject duplicate IDs."""
    from biopipe_sdk.protocol import validate_extension, describe_reader
    from src.task.step.base import Step
    from src.reader.base import Reader
    from src.task.operator_catalog import build_catalog
    from src.reader.schemas import builtin_schema
    definitions = read_json(root / "src/task/operator_ui.json", {})
    previous = read_json(root / PACKAGE_INDEX, {"packages": []})
    old_readers = {d["id"] for p in previous["packages"] for d in p["descriptors"] if d["kind"] == "reader"}
    classes, errors, seen = [], [], set()
    for folder, base, kind in (("src/task/step/steps", Step, "operator"), ("src/reader/readers", Reader, "reader")):
        for path in sorted((root / folder).glob("*.py")):
            if path.name.startswith("_"):
                continue
            if sources is not None and path.relative_to(root).as_posix() not in sources:
                continue
            module_name = path.relative_to(root).with_suffix("").as_posix().replace("/", ".")
            try:
                module = importlib.reload(sys.modules[module_name]) if module_name in sys.modules else importlib.import_module(module_name)
                for cls in vars(module).values():
                    if not isinstance(cls, type) or not issubclass(cls, base) or cls is base or cls.__module__ != module_name:
                        continue
                    identifier = cls.name if kind == "operator" else getattr(cls, "name", None) or cls.__name__
                    if identifier in seen:
                        raise ValueError(f"Duplicate extension ID: {identifier}")
                    seen.add(identifier)
                    # Only established builtins have compatibility adapters.
                    if cls.parameters_schema is None:
                        if kind == "operator" and identifier in definitions:
                            cls.legacy_parameters = True
                        elif kind == "reader" and identifier in old_readers:
                            cls.parameters_schema = builtin_schema(cls.__name__)
                        else:
                            raise ValueError(f"{identifier}: missing parameters_schema")
                    classes.append((kind, cls, path.relative_to(root).as_posix()))
            except Exception as exc:
                errors.append({"source": path.relative_to(root).as_posix(), "error": str(exc)})
    operators = {cls.name: cls for kind, cls, _ in classes if kind == "operator"}
    descriptors = []
    for kind, cls, source in classes:
        try:
            if kind == "operator":
                descriptor = build_catalog({cls.name: cls})["operators"][0]
                if cls.parameters_schema is None:
                    cls.parameters_schema = deepcopy(descriptor["parameters_schema"])
                validate_extension(cls, kind)
            else:
                validate_extension(cls, kind)
                descriptor = describe_reader(cls())
            descriptors.append((descriptor, getattr(cls, "builtin_package", None), source))
        except Exception as exc:
            errors.append({"source": source, "error": str(exc)})
    return descriptors, errors


def package_manifest(descriptors, previous):
    owners = {(d["kind"], d["id"]): p["name"] for p in previous["packages"] for d in p["descriptors"]}
    packages = {p["name"]: {**deepcopy(p), "descriptors": []} for p in previous["packages"]}
    for descriptor, declared_owner, source in descriptors:
        key = (descriptor["kind"], descriptor["id"])
        name = declared_owner or owners.get(key) or ("biopipe-readers" if descriptor["kind"] == "reader" else "biopipe-core")
        if key in owners and name != owners[key]:
            raise ValueError(f"{source}: ownership of {descriptor['id']} cannot move from {owners[key]} to {name}")
        package = packages.setdefault(name, {"protocol_version": 1, "name": name, "title": name,
                                            "version": "1.0.0", "origin": "bundled", "descriptors": []})
        package["descriptors"].append(descriptor)
    from biopipe_sdk.protocol import PackageDescriptor
    for package in packages.values():
        package["descriptors"].sort(key=lambda d: (d["kind"], d["id"]))
        PackageDescriptor.model_validate(package)
    return {"protocol_version": 1, "packages": list(packages.values())}


def route_sources(root, route_roots=None):
    roots = [Path(p) for p in route_roots] if route_roots else [root / "routes"]
    paths = {}
    for directory in roots:
        directory = directory if directory.is_absolute() else root / directory
        if not directory.is_dir():
            raise ValueError(f"Route directory does not exist: {directory}")
        for path in sorted(set(directory.rglob("task.yaml")) | set(directory.rglob("task.yml"))):
            paths[path.resolve()] = path.parent.relative_to(directory).as_posix()
    return sorted(paths.items(), key=lambda pair: str(pair[0]))


def normalize_numbers(raw, catalog):
    """Normalize YAML 1.1 scientific notation only for declared numeric fields."""
    specs = {item["id"]: item for item in catalog["operators"]}
    for step in raw.get("steps", []):
        spec = specs.get(step.get("name"), {})
        for key, field in spec.get("parameters_schema", {}).get("properties", {}).items():
            params = step.get("params", {})
            value = params.get(key)
            if field.get("type") in ("number", "integer") and isinstance(value, str):
                number = float(value)
                params[key] = int(number) if field["type"] == "integer" and number.is_integer() else number


def build_routes(root, catalog, previous, route_roots=None):
    from src.web.graph import import_manifest
    from src.web.models import RouteDefinition
    from src.web.routes import prepare_interface, check_interface
    old = {r["id"]: r for r in previous.get("routes", [])}
    routes, sources, errors, ids, names = [], {}, [], set(), set()
    for path, relative in route_sources(root, route_roots):
        try:
            settings = read_json(path.with_name("route.json"), {})
            if settings.get("enabled", True) is False:
                continue
            key = settings.get("key", relative if relative != "." else path.parent.name)
            identifier = settings.get("id", "route_" + sha256(("biopipe/builtin/" + key).encode()).hexdigest()[:12])
            if identifier in ids:
                raise ValueError(f"Duplicate route ID: {identifier}")
            ids.add(identifier)
            raw = yaml.safe_load(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            if not isinstance(raw, dict):
                raise ValueError("Route YAML must be an object")
            normalize_numbers(raw, catalog)
            paths = [settings.get("base_dir", "."), *[s.get("path", "") for s in raw.get("sources", {}).values()]]
            if any(Path(p).is_absolute() or PureWindowsPath(p).is_absolute() for p in paths):
                raise ValueError("Bundled route paths must be relative so templates remain portable")
            name = settings.get("name", raw.get("name", key))
            if name in names:
                raise ValueError(f"Duplicate route name: {name}")
            names.add(name)
            graph = import_manifest(yaml.safe_dump(raw, allow_unicode=True), settings.get("base_dir", f".studio/builtin-inputs/{key}"), catalog, root)
            graph.name = name
            levels, rows = {}, {}
            for node in graph.nodes:
                parents = [edge.source for edge in graph.edges if edge.target == node.id]
                level = max((levels[parent] + 1 for parent in parents), default=0)
                levels[node.id] = level
                row = rows.get(level, 0)
                rows[level] = row + 1
                node.position.x, node.position.y = 70 + 290 * level, 100 + 210 * row
            graph = prepare_interface(graph, catalog, root)
            check_interface(graph, catalog)
            body = graph.model_dump(mode="json")
            before = old.get(identifier)
            version = (before["version"] + int(before["graph"] != body)) if before else 1
            record = {"id": identifier, "version": version, "name": name, "graph": body}
            RouteDefinition.model_validate(record)
            sources[identifier] = {"path": path.relative_to(root).as_posix() if path.is_relative_to(root) else path.name,
                                   "sha256": sha256(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()}
            if settings.get("source_commit"):
                sources[identifier]["commit"] = settings["source_commit"]
            routes.append(record)
        except Exception as exc:
            errors.append({"source": str(path), "error": str(exc)})
    return {"schema_version": 1, "sources": sources, "routes": sorted(routes, key=lambda r: r["id"])}, errors


def inventory(root=ROOT, source_ref=None, route_roots=None):
    """Static discovery works even if a newly added module cannot be imported."""
    known = read_json(root / PACKAGE_INDEX, {"packages": []})
    ids = {d["id"] for p in known["packages"] for d in p["descriptors"]}
    known_routes = {r["id"] for r in read_json(root / ROUTE_INDEX, {"routes": []})["routes"]}
    result = {"source_ref": source_ref, "extensions": [], "routes": [], "errors": []}
    route_keys = {}
    if source_ref:
        commit = subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", "--verify", "--end-of-options", source_ref + "^{commit}"], cwd=root, text=True).strip()
        paths = subprocess.check_output(["git", "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", commit], cwd=root, text=True).splitlines()
        result["source_ref"] = commit
        selected = [p for p in paths if (p.startswith(("src/task/step/steps/", "src/reader/readers/")) and p.endswith(".py") and not Path(p).name.startswith("_"))
                    or (p.startswith(("routes/", "tasks/")) and Path(p).name in {"task.yaml", "task.yml"})]
        sources = [(p, subprocess.check_output(["git", "show", f"{commit}:{p}"], cwd=root).decode("utf-8")) for p in selected]
    else:
        paths = [*sorted((root / "src/task/step/steps").glob("[!_]*.py")), *sorted((root / "src/reader/readers").glob("[!_]*.py"))]
        found_routes = route_sources(root, route_roots)
        paths += [p for p, _ in found_routes]
        route_keys = {str(p): relative if relative != "." else p.parent.name for p, relative in found_routes}
        sources = [(p.relative_to(root).as_posix() if p.is_relative_to(root) else str(p), p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) for p in paths]
    for path, text in sources:
        try:
            if path.endswith((".yaml", ".yml")):
                raw = yaml.safe_load(text)
                required = sorted({s["name"] for s in raw.get("steps", [])})
                settings = read_json((root / path).with_name("route.json"), {}) if not source_ref else {}
                default_key = Path(path).parent.name.removesuffix("_demo")
                if source_ref:
                    sidecar = Path(path).with_name("route.json").as_posix()
                    if sidecar in paths:
                        settings = json.loads(subprocess.check_output(["git", "show", f"{commit}:{sidecar}"], cwd=root).decode("utf-8"))
                    if path.startswith("routes/"):
                        default_key = Path(path).parent.relative_to("routes").as_posix()
                else:
                    default_key = route_keys.get(str((root / path).resolve()), default_key)
                key = settings.get("key", default_key)
                identifier = settings.get("id", "route_" + sha256(("biopipe/builtin/" + key).encode()).hexdigest()[:12])
                result["routes"].append({"source": path, "name": settings.get("name", raw.get("name", Path(path).parent.name)),
                                         "indexed": identifier in known_routes,
                                         "operators": required, "missing_operators": sorted(set(required) - ids)})
                continue
            for cls in [n for n in ast.parse(text).body if isinstance(n, ast.ClassDef)]:
                bases = {ast.unparse(base).split(".")[-1] for base in cls.bases}
                if not bases & {"Step", "Operator", "Reader"}:
                    continue
                attributes = {}
                for assignment in cls.body:
                    if isinstance(assignment, (ast.Assign, ast.AnnAssign)):
                        for target in assignment.targets if isinstance(assignment, ast.Assign) else [assignment.target]:
                            if isinstance(target, ast.Name):
                                try:
                                    attributes[target.id] = ast.literal_eval(assignment.value)
                                except (ValueError, TypeError):
                                    pass
                identifier = attributes.get("name", cls.name)
                result["extensions"].append({"id": identifier, "source": path, "kind": "reader" if "Reader" in bases else "operator",
                                             "indexed": identifier in ids, "local_source": (root / path).is_file(),
                                             "source_differs": source_ref is not None and (root / path).is_file()
                                             and (root / path).read_text(encoding="utf-8") != text.replace("\r\n", "\n")})
        except Exception as exc:
            result["errors"].append({"source": path, "error": str(exc)})
    return result


def maintain(action="check", *, root=ROOT, route_roots=None, output_dir=None, scope="all"):
    root = Path(root).resolve()
    previous = read_json(root / PACKAGE_INDEX, {"protocol_version": 1, "packages": []})
    descriptors, errors = scan_sources(root)
    candidate = package_manifest(descriptors, previous)
    catalog = {"schema_version": 1, "protocol_version": 1, "contract_version": 1,
               "operators": [d for d, _, _ in descriptors if d["kind"] == "operator"],
               "readers": [d for d, _, _ in descriptors if d["kind"] == "reader"]}
    outputs = {}
    if scope != "routes":
        outputs[PACKAGE_INDEX] = candidate
    if scope != "packages":
        routes, route_errors = build_routes(root, catalog, read_json(root / ROUTE_INDEX, {}), route_roots)
        errors += route_errors
        outputs[ROUTE_INDEX] = routes
    destination = Path(output_dir).resolve() if output_dir else root
    changed = [path.as_posix() for path, data in outputs.items() if read_json(destination / path, None) != data]
    report = {"action": action, "operators": len(catalog["operators"]), "readers": len(catalog["readers"]),
              "packages": len(candidate["packages"]), "routes": len(outputs.get(ROUTE_INDEX, {}).get("routes", [])),
              "changed": changed, "errors": errors, "written": []}
    if errors:
        return report
    if action == "build":
        from src.web.storage import FileLock, atomic_json
        with FileLock(destination / ".registry-maintenance.lock"):
            for path, data in outputs.items():
                if path.as_posix() in changed:
                    atomic_json(destination / path, data)
                    report["written"].append(str(destination / path))
    return report


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("action", choices=["discover", "check", "build"])
    parser.add_argument("--source-ref", help="Static discovery against a locally fetched Git ref (discover only)")
    parser.add_argument("--route-root", action="append", help="Repeatable directory containing task.yaml/task.yml; defaults to routes/")
    parser.add_argument("--output-dir", type=Path, help="Write/check generated indexes under an alternate directory")
    parser.add_argument("--scope", choices=["all", "packages", "routes"], default="all")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.source_ref and args.action != "discover":
        parser.error("--source-ref is for discovery only; adapt reviewed code locally before building")
    os.environ["BIOPIPE_REGISTRY_MAINTENANCE"] = "1"
    try:
        result = inventory(source_ref=args.source_ref, route_roots=args.route_root) if args.action == "discover" else maintain(
            args.action, route_roots=args.route_root, output_dir=args.output_dir, scope=args.scope)
        print(json.dumps(result, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
        return int(bool(result["errors"]) or (args.action == "check" and bool(result["changed"])))
    except Exception as exc:
        print(json.dumps({"errors": [{"error": str(exc)}]}, ensure_ascii=False))
        return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

===== scripts/build_builtin_packages.py =====
"""Refresh the builtin index, or build an independently installable builtin wheel.

python scripts/build_builtin_packages.py --refresh
python scripts/build_builtin_packages.py --package biopipe-core --output .tmp/packages
"""
import argparse
from copy import deepcopy
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
