sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))


def refresh():
    from biopipe.registry import maintain
    previous = os.environ.get("BIOPIPE_REGISTRY_MAINTENANCE")
    os.environ["BIOPIPE_REGISTRY_MAINTENANCE"] = "1"
    try:
        report = maintain("build", scope="packages")
    finally:
        if previous is None:
            os.environ.pop("BIOPIPE_REGISTRY_MAINTENANCE", None)
        else:
            os.environ["BIOPIPE_REGISTRY_MAINTENANCE"] = previous
    if report["errors"]:
        raise ValueError(json.dumps(report["errors"], ensure_ascii=False))
    from biopipe.packages import bundled_manifest, bundled_state
    bundled_manifest.cache_clear()
    bundled_state.cache_clear()
    return json.loads((ROOT / "biopipe/builtin_packages.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def build_package(name, output):
    from biopipe import __version__
    from biopipe.packages import bundled_manifest
    from biopipe.developer import build
    package = next((p for p in bundled_manifest()["packages"] if p["name"] == name), None)
    if package is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown bundled package: {name}")
    directory = Path(output).resolve() / name
    if directory.exists():
        raise ValueError(f"Choose an unused output directory: {directory}")
    module = name.replace("-", "_") + "_provider"
    target = directory / module
    target.mkdir(parents=True)
    entries, init = {"operator": [], "reader": []}, ["import json", "from pathlib import Path", "from importlib import import_module",
        "_definitions = json.loads(Path(__file__).with_name('definitions.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))"]
    definitions = []
    for i, original in enumerate(package["descriptors"]):
        descriptor = deepcopy(original)
        source_module, clsname = descriptor["entry_point"].split(":")
        source = ROOT / (source_module.replace(".", "/") + ".py")
        # A private namespace lets old and new generations coexist; src remains
        # the stable engine ABI, never overwritten by wheel installation.
        content = source.read_text(encoding="utf-8").replace("src.task.step.steps", module).replace("src.reader.readers", module)
        (target / source.name).write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
        alias = f"Entry{i}"
        descriptor["entry_point"] = f"{module}.{source.stem}:{clsname}"
        definitions.append(descriptor)
        entries[descriptor["kind"]].append(f'{alias} = "{module}:{alias}"')
    # Shared helpers and delegated readers are implementation dependencies.
    helper_sources = list((ROOT / "src/task/step/steps").glob("_*.py")) + list((ROOT / "src/reader/readers").glob("_*.py"))
    if name == "biopipe-readers":
        helper_sources += list((ROOT / "src/reader/readers").glob("*.py"))
    for source in helper_sources:
        if source.name != "__init__.py" and not (target / source.name).exists():
            (target / source.name).write_text(source.read_text(encoding="utf-8").replace("src.task.step.steps", module).replace("src.reader.readers", module), encoding="utf-8")
    init.extend(["def __getattr__(name):", "    index = int(name[5:]) if name.startswith('Entry') and name[5:].isdigit() else -1",
                 "    if index < 0 or index >= len(_definitions): raise AttributeError(name)",
                 "    d = _definitions[index]", "    module, clsname = d['entry_point'].split(':')",
                 "    cls = getattr(import_module(module), clsname)",
                 "    cls.parameters_schema = d['parameters_schema']", "    cls.legacy_parameters = d.get('legacy_parameters', True)",
                 "    cls._package_descriptor = d", "    return cls"])
    (target / "__init__.py").write_text("\n".join(init) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (target / "definitions.json").write_text(json.dumps(definitions, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
    project = ['[build-system]', 'requires = ["setuptools>=69"]', 'build-backend = "setuptools.build_meta"',
               '[project]', f'name = "{name}"', f'version = "{package["version"]}"', 'requires-python = ">=3.11"',
               f'dependencies = ["biopipe-studio=={__version__}"]', '[tool.setuptools.package-data]', f'{module} = ["definitions.json"]']
    for kind, group in (("operator", "operators"), ("reader", "readers")):
        if entries[kind]:
            project += [f'[project.entry-points."biopipe.{group}"]', *entries[kind]]
    (directory / "pyproject.toml").write_text("\n".join(project) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return build(directory)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--refresh", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--package")
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=Path("build/packages"))
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.refresh:
        refresh()
        print("Builtin package index refreshed")
    if args.package:
        print(json.dumps(build_package(args.package, args.output), indent=2))

===== tests/test_upstream_operators.py =====
"""Synthetic expression/survival routes, numeric results and metadata contracts."""
from pathlib import Path
import gzip
import numpy as np
import pytest

from src.core import Form
from src.reader import read_source
from src.task import Context, STEPS, run
from src.task.operator_catalog import build_catalog
from src.task.memory.planner import full_estimate


def expression():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(26)
    data = rng.uniform(2, 30, (8, 12)).astype(np.float32)
    data[:3, 6:] *= 2
    data[0, 0] = -9
    matrix = Form(data, ['gene','sample'], {'gene':{f'g{i}':i for i in range(8)}, 'sample':{f's{i}':i for i in range(12)}},
                  {'missing_code':-9, 'chrom':['1']*8, 'pos':np.arange(8), 'cohort':['a']*6+['b']*6, 'study':'synthetic'}, {'gene':['chrom','pos'], 'sample':['cohort']})
    meta = Form(np.array([[0.]]*6+[[1.]]*6), ['sample','feature'], {'sample':matrix.labels['sample'], 'feature':{'group':0}}, {'missing_code':np.nan})
    return matrix, meta


def step(name, inputs, **params):
    return {'name':name, 'inputs':inputs, 'params':params}


def test_registry_thirteen_adapted_operators():
    new = set('filter_min normalize log_transform impute compare_groups kaplan_meier logrank_test cox_regression volcano ma_plot heatmap boxplot forest_plot'.split())
    catalog = build_catalog(STEPS)
    assert new <= set(STEPS)
    for item in catalog['operators']:
        if item['name'] not in new: continue
        assert item['parameters'] and item['contracts']['inputs'] and item['contracts']['output']
        assert item['memory_modes'] == ['full'] and not item['supports_cuda']


@pytest.mark.parametrize('method',['cpm','total','median_of_ratios','quantile','zscore'])
def test_normalization_missing_sentinel_and_metadata(tmp_path, method):
    form, _=expression(); ctx=Context(tmp_path,{})
    ctx['x']=form
    run(ctx,[step('normalize',['x'],method=method,dim='sample',output='out')])
    out=ctx['out']
    assert np.isnan(out.data[0,0]) and np.isnan(out.info['missing_code'])
    assert out.axis_info == form.axis_info and out.info['study']=='synthetic'
    assert out.info['representation']=='normalize' and form.info['missing_code']==-9
    if method=='cpm': np.testing.assert_allclose(np.nansum(out.data,axis=0), 1e6,rtol=1e-6)
    if method=='total': np.testing.assert_allclose(np.nansum(out.data,axis=0), 1,rtol=1e-6)
    if method=='zscore': np.testing.assert_allclose(np.nanmean(out.data,axis=0),0,atol=1e-6)


def test_expression_route_branches_reports_and_aligned_annotations(tmp_path):
    matrix,meta=expression()
    ctx=Context(tmp_path,{'runtime':{'memory':{'enabled':True,'budget_mb':128,'keep':['diff','logged']}}})
    ctx['x']=matrix; ctx['meta']=meta
    steps=[step('filter_min',['x'],dim='gene',min_value=1,min_samples=3,output='filtered'),
           step('impute',['filtered'],method='median',dim='gene',output='filled'),
           step('normalize',['filled'],method='quantile',dim='sample',output='normalized'),
           step('log_transform',['normalized'],base=2,pseudocount=1,output='logged'),
           step('compare_groups',['normalized','meta'],dim='gene',group_dim='sample',group_label='group',group_a=0,group_b=1,method='welch',output='diff'),
           step('volcano',['diff'],dim='gene',filename='volcano.png'),
           step('ma_plot',['diff'],dim='gene',filename='ma.png'),
           step('heatmap',['logged'],dim='gene',other_dim='sample',top_n=5,filename='heatmap.png'),
           step('boxplot',['logged','meta'],dim='gene',value_label='g1',group_dim='sample',group_label='group',filename='boxplot.png')]
    try:
        run(ctx,steps)
        diff=ctx['diff']; logged=ctx['logged']
        assert diff.axis_info=={'gene':['chrom','pos']}
        assert 'cohort' not in diff.info and diff.info['study']=='synthetic'
        assert diff.info['representation']=='comparison'
        assert np.isfinite(logged.data).all()
        assert len(list((ctx.memory.run_dir/'figures').glob('*.png')))==4
        assert all(item['export_path'] for item in ctx.artifacts['exports'])
    finally: ctx.memory.close('complete')


def test_survival_route(tmp_path):
    pytest.importorskip('lifelines')
    rng=np.random.default_rng(91); n=100
    age=rng.normal(60,9,n); group=rng.integers(0,2,n)
    times=rng.exponential(50*np.exp(-.03*(age-60)-.4*group))+1
    events=rng.binomial(1,.8,n)
    data=np.column_stack([times,events,group,age]).astype(np.float32)
    form=Form(data,['sample','feature'],{'sample':{str(i):i for i in range(n)},'feature':{'time':0,'event':1,'group':2,'age':3}},
              {'missing_code':np.nan,'study':'synthetic','patient_note':['keep private']*n},{'sample':['patient_note']})
    ctx=Context(tmp_path,{});ctx['clinical']=form
    run(ctx,[step('kaplan_meier',['clinical'],time_label='time',event_label='event',group_label='group',filename='km.png'),
             step('logrank_test',['clinical'],time_label='time',event_label='event',group_label='group',pairwise=True,output='logrank'),
             step('cox_regression',['clinical'],time_label='time',event_label='event',covariates=['age','group'],output='cox'),
             step('forest_plot',['cox'],filename='forest.png')])
    assert ctx['logrank'].dims==['comparison','statistic']
    assert ctx['cox'].dims==['covariate','statistic']
    assert ctx['cox'].info['study']=='synthetic' and 'patient_note' not in ctx['cox'].info
    assert np.isfinite(ctx['cox'].data).all()
    assert len(list((ctx.out_dir/'figures').glob('*.png')))==2


def test_txt_reader_csv_separator_and_compression(tmp_path):
    for filename in ['data.tsv','data.txt.gz']:
        path=tmp_path/filename; content='id\ta\tb\ns0\t1\t2\ns1\t3\t4\n'
        if filename.endswith('.gz'):
            with gzip.open(path,'wt') as stream: stream.write(content)
        else: path.write_text(content)
        form=read_source(path,['sample','feature'],{'sample':{'feature':0},'feature':'builtin'},reader='csv')
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(form.data,[[1,2],[3,4]])
        assert form.labels['sample']=={'s0':0,'s1':1}
    path=tmp_path/'semi.csv';path.write_text('a;b\n1;2\n')
    assert read_source(path,['sample','feature'],{'feature':'builtin'},sep=';').data.shape==(1,2)


def test_full_kernels_reserve_float64_workspaces():
    form,_=expression()
    workspace,output=full_estimate('normalize',[form],{'method':'quantile','dim':'sample'}, {})
    assert workspace >= form.data.size*64 and output >= form.data.nbytes


def test_deseq2_selects_only_two_groups_and_rejects_normalized_counts(tmp_path):
    pytest.importorskip('pydeseq2')
    from src.task.step.steps.compare_groups import _deseq2_test
    from src.core import StepError
    rng=np.random.default_rng(2026)
    counts=rng.negative_binomial(5,.25,(32,15)).astype(float)
    mask_a=np.arange(15)<6; mask_b=(np.arange(15)>=6)&(np.arange(15)<12)
    counts[:,12:]=np.nan  # Unselected samples must not enter group B.
    result,labels=_deseq2_test(counts,mask_a,mask_b)
    assert result.shape==(32,7) and labels=={'Q_VALUE':6}
    assert np.all(result[:,5]==12) and np.isfinite(result[:,4]).any()
    with pytest.raises(StepError,match='raw integer counts'):
        _deseq2_test(counts+.25,mask_a,mask_b)

===== tests/test_registry_maintenance.py =====
"""Maintenance discovers filesystem additions and publishes only valid indexes."""
from copy import deepcopy
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys

import pytest

from biopipe.packages import bundled_state
from biopipe.plugins import PluginManager, active_descriptors

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


@pytest.fixture
def checkout(tmp_path):
    root = tmp_path / "checkout"
    root.mkdir()
    for name in ("src", "biopipe", "biopipe_sdk", "routes", "scripts"):
        shutil.copytree(ROOT / name, root / name, ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns("__pycache__", "*.pyc"))
    return root


def cli(root, *args):
    process = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "biopipe", "registry", *args], cwd=root,
                             capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", timeout=60)
    assert process.stdout, process.stderr
    return process.returncode, json.loads(process.stdout)


NEW_OPERATOR = '''from biopipe_sdk import Step, port
class Probe(Step):
    name = "example.probe"
    category = "analysis"
    icon = "Sigma"
    inputs_arity = 1
    parameters_schema = {"type": "object", "properties": {}, "additionalProperties": False}
    input_contracts = [port(ndim=2)]
    output_contract = {"mode": "report"}
    def run(self, ctx, inputs):
        pass
'''


def add_extension(root, source=NEW_OPERATOR):
    path = root / "src/task/step/steps/example_probe.py"
    path.write_text(source, encoding="utf-8")
    return path


def indexes(root):
    return [(root / path).read_bytes() for path in ("biopipe/builtin_packages.json", "src/web/builtin_routes.json")]


def test_discover_build_check_new_operator_and_unlisted_route(checkout):
    add_extension(checkout)
    path = checkout / "routes/custom/nested/task.yml"
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
    path.write_text('''name: Custom report
sources:
  counts:
    path: input/counts.csv
    dims: [sample, feature]
steps:
  - name: example.probe
    inputs: [counts]
''', encoding="utf-8")
    code, discovery = cli(checkout, "discover")
    assert code == 0
    assert any(d["id"] == "example.probe" and not d["indexed"] for d in discovery["extensions"])
    assert any(r["name"] == "Custom report" for r in discovery["routes"])
    before = indexes(checkout)
    code, check = cli(checkout, "check")
    assert code == 1 and check["changed"] and not check["errors"] and indexes(checkout) == before
    code, built = cli(checkout, "build")
    operator_count = sum(d["kind"] == "operator" for d in bundled_state()["descriptors"])
    assert code == 0 and built["operators"] == operator_count + 1 and built["routes"] == 6
    manifest = json.loads((checkout / "biopipe/builtin_packages.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    descriptor = next(d for p in manifest["packages"] for d in p["descriptors"] if d["id"] == "example.probe")
    assert descriptor["category"] == "analysis" and descriptor["icon"] == "Sigma"
    code, check = cli(checkout, "check")
    assert code == 0 and not check["changed"]
    assert next(r for r in cli(checkout, "discover")[1]["routes"] if r["name"] == "Custom report")["indexed"]
    after = indexes(checkout)
    assert cli(checkout, "build")[1]["written"] == [] and indexes(checkout) == after


def test_sdk_operator_is_discovered_and_missing_startup_index_can_be_rebuilt(checkout):
    add_extension(checkout, NEW_OPERATOR.replace('Step, port', 'Operator, port').replace('class Probe(Step)', 'class Probe(Operator)')
                  .replace('    def run(self, ctx, inputs):', '    output_parameter = None\n    def compute(self, inputs, params, context):'))
    code, result = cli(checkout, "discover")
    assert code == 0 and any(e['id'] == 'example.probe' and e['kind'] == 'operator' for e in result['extensions'])
    index = checkout / 'biopipe/builtin_packages.json'
    value = json.loads(index.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    # A removed module referenced by an old startup index must not block repair.
    entry = deepcopy(next(d for p in value['packages'] for d in p['descriptors'] if d['id'] == 'diversity'))
    entry.update(id='removed.operator', entry_point='src.task.step.steps.deleted:Deleted')
    value['packages'][0]['descriptors'].append(entry)
    index.write_text(json.dumps(value), encoding='utf-8')
    code, report = cli(checkout, 'build')
    assert code == 0 and not report['errors']
    assert 'removed.operator' not in index.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    runtime = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-c', "from src.task import STEPS; assert not STEPS['example.probe'].legacy_parameters"],
                             cwd=checkout, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
    assert runtime.returncode == 0, runtime.stderr


@pytest.mark.parametrize("source,expected", [
    (NEW_OPERATOR.replace('parameters_schema = {"type": "object", "properties": {}, "additionalProperties": False}', 'parameters_schema = None'), "parameters_schema"),
    (NEW_OPERATOR.replace('name = "example.probe"', 'name = "diversity"'), "Duplicate"),
    (NEW_OPERATOR.replace('output_contract = {"mode": "report"}', 'output_contract = {"mode": "invented"}'), "contract mode"),
    (NEW_OPERATOR + '\nraise RuntimeError("import failure")\n', "import failure"),
])
def test_invalid_extension_does_not_publish_partial_indexes(checkout, source, expected):
    add_extension(checkout, source)
    before = indexes(checkout)
    code, report = cli(checkout, "build")
    assert code == 1 and report["written"] == []
    assert any(expected in error["error"] for error in report["errors"])
    assert indexes(checkout) == before


def test_missing_route_operator_does_not_publish_and_custom_root_works(checkout):
    custom = checkout / "extra_routes/new"
    custom.mkdir(parents=True)
    source = (checkout / "routes/microbiome/task.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    (custom / "task.yaml").write_text(source.replace("name: diversity", "name: missing.operator"), encoding="utf-8")
    before = indexes(checkout)
    code, report = cli(checkout, "build", "--route-root", "extra_routes")
    assert code == 1 and any("missing.operator" in e["error"] for e in report["errors"])
    assert indexes(checkout) == before
    (custom / "task.yaml").write_text(source, encoding="utf-8")
    code, report = cli(checkout, "build", "--route-root", "extra_routes", "--output-dir", str(checkout / "generated"))
    assert code == 0 and report["routes"] == 1 and indexes(checkout) == before


def test_new_bundled_package_migrates_without_restoring_removals(tmp_path):
    manager = PluginManager(tmp_path)
    manager.directory.mkdir(parents=True)
    state = deepcopy(bundled_state())
    state["packages"] = [p for p in state["packages"] if p["name"] != "biopipe-microbiome"]
    state["descriptors"] = [d for d in state["descriptors"] if d["distribution"] != "biopipe-microbiome"]
    state.update(protocol_version=1, package_protocol_version=1, generation="before16s")
    history = manager.directory / "snapshots"
    history.mkdir()
    (history / "old.json").write_text(json.dumps(state), encoding="utf-8")
    state["packages"] = [p for p in state["packages"] if p["name"] != "biopipe-genetics"]
    state["descriptors"] = [d for d in state["descriptors"] if d["distribution"] != "biopipe-genetics"]
    next(p for p in state["packages"] if p["name"] == "biopipe-core")["enabled"] = False
    manager.registry.write_text(json.dumps(state), encoding="utf-8")
    updated = manager.state()
    names = {p["name"] for p in updated["packages"]}
    assert "biopipe-microbiome" in names and "biopipe-genetics" not in names
    assert not next(p for p in updated["packages"] if p["name"] == "biopipe-core")["enabled"]
    assert "diversity" in {d["id"] for d in active_descriptors(updated)}
    assert manager.state()["generation"] == updated["generation"]
    manager.manage("biopipe-microbiome", "remove")
    assert "biopipe-microbiome" not in {p["name"] for p in manager.state()["packages"]}
    manager.manage("biopipe-microbiome", "restore")
    assert "diversity" in {d["id"] for d in active_descriptors(manager.state())}

===== tests/test_contracts.py =====
"""Contracts, real transformations and cache round-trips, including false promises."""
from copy import deepcopy
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from src.core import Form, StepError
from src.core.contracts import compatible, describe_form, port, resolve, validate_form
from src.reader import READERS, read_source
from src.task import Context, STEPS, run
from src.task.operator_catalog import build_catalog
from src.task.step.base import Step
from src.web.api import create_app
from src.web.service import Settings


def annotated():
    return Form(np.array([[0, 1, 2, 1], [2, 1, 0, 1], [0, 0, 1, 2]], dtype=np.float32),
                ["variant", "sample"],
                {"variant": {"v0": 0, "v1": 1, "v2": 2}, "sample": {f"s{i}": i for i in range(4)}},
                {"missing_code": np.nan, "ploidy": 2, "representation": "hard_call",
                 "chrom": ["1", "2", "3"], "pos": np.array([10, 20, 30]), "group": ["a", "b", "a", "b"]},
                {"variant": ["chrom", "pos"], "sample": ["group"]})


def execute(tmp_path, inputs, steps, managed=False):
    cfg = {"runtime": {"memory": {"enabled": True, "budget_mb": 16, "keep": ["out"]}}} if managed else {}
    ctx = Context(tmp_path, cfg)
    for name, form in inputs.items():
        ctx[name] = form
    try:
        run(ctx, steps)
    except BaseException:
        if ctx.memory:
            ctx.memory.close("failed")
        raise
    return ctx


def step(name, inputs=None, **params):
    return {"name": name, "inputs": inputs or ["x"], "params": {"output": "out", **params}}


def test_registry_all_ports_and_readers_json():
    import json
    catalog = build_catalog(STEPS)
    assert catalog["contract_version"] == 1
    assert len(catalog["readers"]) == len(READERS)
    assert all(reader["output_contract"] for reader in catalog["readers"])
    assert all(op["contracts"]["inputs"] and op["contracts"]["output"] for op in catalog["operators"])
    json.dumps(catalog, allow_nan=False)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("representation,value", [("dosage", 1.25), ("hard_call", .5), ("hard_call", 3), ("hard_call", np.inf)])
def test_hwe_rejects_wrong_semantics_and_lying_info(tmp_path, representation, value):
    form = annotated()
    form.info["representation"] = representation
    form.data[0, 0] = value
    with pytest.raises(StepError, match="contract"):
        execute(tmp_path, {"x": form}, [step("hwe", dim="variant")])


def test_legacy_missing_semantic_tag_is_not_inferred_from_numbers(tmp_path):
    form = annotated()
    del form.info["representation"]
    with pytest.raises(StepError, match="representation"):
        execute(tmp_path, {"x": form}, [step("hwe", dim="variant")])


@pytest.mark.parametrize("managed", [False, True])
def test_subset_rename_transpose_branch_and_spill(tmp_path, managed):
    original = annotated()
    ctx = execute(tmp_path, {"x": original}, [
        step("select", dim="variant", labels=["v2", "v0"], output="selected"),
        step("rename_dim", ["selected"], **{"from": "variant", "to": "locus", "output": "renamed"}),
        step("transpose", ["renamed"], order=["sample", "locus"]),
        step("drop", dim="variant", labels=["v0"], output="branch"),
    ], managed)
    result = ctx["out"]
    assert result.dims == ["sample", "locus"]
    assert result.info["chrom"] == ["3", "1"]
    assert result.axis_info == {"locus": ["chrom", "pos"], "sample": ["group"]}
    assert original.info["chrom"] == ["1", "2", "3"]
    assert ctx["branch"].info["chrom"] == ["2", "3"]
    if managed:
        ctx.forms.spill(ctx.forms.entry("out"), "test")
        restored = ctx["out"]
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(restored.info["pos"], [30, 10])
        assert restored.axis_info == result.axis_info
        ctx.memory.close("complete")


def test_pca_preserves_only_retained_axis_and_replaces_semantics(tmp_path):
    ctx = execute(tmp_path, {"x": annotated()}, [step("pca", dim="variant", n_components=2)])
    result = ctx["out"]
    assert result.info["representation"] == "scores"
    assert result.axis_info == {"sample": ["group"]}
    assert not {"chrom", "pos", "ploidy"} & result.info.keys()
    assert result.info["group"] == ["a", "b", "a", "b"]


def test_concat_reorders_annotations_and_refuses_conflicts(tmp_path):
    a = annotated().subset_axis("variant", [0])
    b = annotated().subset_axis("variant", [1, 2]).subset_axis("sample", [2, 0, 3])
    ctx = execute(tmp_path / "valid", {"a": a, "b": b}, [step("concat", ["a", "b"], axis="variant")])
    result = ctx["out"]
    assert result.labels["sample"] == {"s0": 0, "s2": 1, "s3": 2}
    assert result.info["chrom"] == ["1", "2", "3"]
    assert result.info["group"] == ["a", "a", "b"]
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result.data, annotated().data[:, [0, 2, 3]])
    b.info["group"][0] = "wrong"
    with pytest.raises(StepError, match="conflicting info.group"):
        execute(tmp_path / "conflict", {"a": a, "b": b}, [step("concat", ["a", "b"], axis="variant")])


def test_regression_fdr_metadata_alignment(tmp_path):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(8)
    data = rng.normal(size=(4, 24)).astype(np.float32)
    form = Form(data, ["variant", "sample"],
                {"variant": {"a": 0, "response": 1, "b": 2, "covar": 3}, "sample": {str(i): i for i in range(24)}},
                {"missing_code": np.nan, "chrom": ["1", "2", "3", "4"], "pos": [10, 20, 30, 40]},
                {"variant": ["chrom", "pos"]})
    ctx = execute(tmp_path, {"x": form}, [step("regress", dim="variant", response="response", covariates=["covar"], output="r"),
                                         step("correction", ["r"], method="fdr")])
    result = ctx["out"]
    assert result.info["chrom"] == ["1", "3"]
    assert result.info["pos"] == [10, 30]
    assert result.axis_info == {"variant": ["chrom", "pos"]}
    assert result.info["representation"] == "regression"
    assert result.info["correction_method"] == "fdr"
    assert "Q_VALUE" in result.labels["statistic"]


def test_attach_uses_label_positions_not_insertion_order(tmp_path):
    target = annotated().subset_axis("variant", [2, 0])
    target.labels["variant"] = {"v0": 1, "v2": 0}
    target.info.pop("chrom")
    target.axis_info["variant"].remove("chrom")
    ctx = execute(tmp_path, {"x": target, "source": annotated()},
                  [step("attach_info", ["x", "source"], fields=["chrom"], target_dim="variant")])
    assert ctx["out"].info["chrom"] == ["3", "1"]
    assert "chrom" not in target.info


def test_runtime_checks_output_before_cache_and_requires_new_write(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    class Broken(Step):
        name = "broken"
        inputs_arity = 1
        input_contracts = [port()]
        output_contract = {"mode": "preserve"}
        output_parameter = "output"
        def run(self, ctx, inputs):
            value = deepcopy(inputs[0])
            value.info.pop("ploidy")
            ctx["out"] = value
    monkeypatch.setitem(STEPS, "broken", Broken)
    with pytest.raises(StepError, match="output out contract"):
        execute(tmp_path / "bad", {"x": annotated()}, [step("broken")], True)
    Broken.run = lambda self, ctx, inputs: None
    with pytest.raises(StepError, match="did not register"):
        execute(tmp_path / "stale", {"x": annotated(), "out": annotated()}, [step("broken")])


def test_reader_output_checked_even_without_operator(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    file = tmp_path / "data.csv"
    file.write_text("a,b\n1,2\n3,4\n")
    result = read_source(file, ["sample", "feature"], {"sample": "auto", "feature": "builtin"})
    assert "missing_code" in result.info
    reader = next(r for r in READERS if type(r).__name__ == "CsvReader")
    monkeypatch.setattr(reader, "read", lambda *a, **k: Form(np.ones((1, 1)), ["sample", "feature"], {"sample": {"s": 0}, "feature": {"f": 0}}, {}))
    with pytest.raises(StepError, match="reader CsvReader output contract"):
        read_source(file, ["sample", "feature"], {})


def test_partial_canvas_and_backend_reject_dosage_to_hwe(tmp_path):
    app = create_app(Settings(tmp_path, tmp_path / "studio", tmp_path / "frontend", testing=True))
    with TestClient(app) as client:
        client.headers["X-Studio-Token"] = client.get("/api/bootstrap").json()["token"]
        graph = {"nodes": [{"id": "data", "kind": "source", "position": {"x": 0, "y": 0},
                            "source": {"path": "example.bgen", "dims": ["variant", "sample"]}},
                           {"id": "hwe", "kind": "operator", "operator": "hwe", "position": {"x": 300, "y": 0},
                            "params": {"dim": "variant", "output": "out"}}],
                 "edges": [{"id": "edge", "source": "data", "target": "hwe"}]}
        response = client.post("/api/contracts", json=graph)
        assert response.status_code == 200
        assert response.json()["edges"][0]["status"] == "incompatible"
        assert "hard_call" in str(response.json()["edges"][0]["reasons"])
        assert client.post("/api/export", json=graph).status_code == 422
        graph["nodes"][0]["source"]["path"] = "example.vcf"
        assert client.post("/api/contracts", json=graph).json()["edges"][0]["status"] == "unknown"  # ploidy is file-dependent
        graph["edges"] = []
        assert client.post("/api/contracts", json=graph).status_code == 200


def test_stream_contract_checks_cached_values_without_loading_whole_tensor(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    from src.task.step.steps.describe import DescribeStep
    class StrictStream(DescribeStep):
        name = "strict_stream"
        input_contracts = STEPS["hwe"].input_contracts
    monkeypatch.setitem(STEPS, "strict_stream", StrictStream)
    ctx = Context(tmp_path, {"runtime": {"memory": {"budget_mb": 8}}})
    value = annotated()
    value.data[-1, -1] = .5
    ctx["x"] = value
    ctx.forms.spill(ctx.forms.entry("x"), "test")
    monkeypatch.setattr(ctx.forms, "_load", lambda entry: pytest.fail("stream validator loaded a full tensor"))
    with pytest.raises(StepError, match="invalid hard_call"):
        run(ctx, [step("strict_stream", dim="variant")])
    assert ctx.forms.entry("x").form is None
    ctx.memory.close("failed")


def test_encoder_maps_follow_select_and_rename(tmp_path):
    file = tmp_path / "encoded.csv"
    file.write_text("color,value\nred,1\nblue,2\nred,3\n")
    form = read_source(file, ["sample", "feature"], {"sample": "auto", "feature": "builtin"})
    ctx = execute(tmp_path / "rename", {"x": form}, [step("rename_dim", **{"from": "feature", "to": "variable"})])
    assert "color" in ctx["out"].info["encoders"]["variable"]
    assert "feature" in form.info["encoders"]
    ctx = execute(tmp_path / "select", {"x": form}, [step("select", dim="feature", labels=["value"])])
    assert "encoders" not in ctx["out"].info


def test_three_dimensional_concat_and_key_alignment(tmp_path):
    a = Form(np.arange(24).reshape(2, 3, 4), ["x", "y", "z"],
             {"x": {"a": 0, "b": 1}, "y": {str(i): i for i in range(3)}, "z": {str(i): i for i in range(4)}},
             {"missing_code": np.nan, "row": [1, 2]}, {"x": ["row"]})
    b = a.subset_axis("x", [1]).subset_axis("z", [3, 1])
    b.labels["y"] = {f"other{i}": i for i in range(3)}
    ctx = execute(tmp_path / "nd", {"a": a, "b": b}, [step("concat", ["a", "b"], axis="y")])
    assert ctx["out"].data.shape == (1, 6, 2)
    assert ctx["out"].info["row"] == [2]
    a = Form(np.array([[10, 1], [20, 2], [30, 3]], dtype=np.float32), ["row", "field"],
             {"row": {"r1": 0, "r2": 1, "r3": 2}, "field": {"id": 0, "a": 1}},
             {"missing_code": np.nan, "group": ["a", "b", "c"], "units": ["id", "a-unit"]},
             {"row": ["group"], "field": ["units"]})
    b = Form(np.array([[30, 9], [10, 8]], dtype=np.float32), ["row", "field"],
             {"row": {"other3": 0, "other1": 1}, "field": {"key": 0, "b": 1}},
             {"missing_code": np.nan, "group": ["c", "a"], "units": ["id", "b-unit"]},
             {"row": ["group"], "field": ["units"]})
    ctx = execute(tmp_path / "key", {"a": a, "b": b}, [step("concat", ["a", "b"], axis="field", align={"dim": "row", "key": {"a": "id", "b": "key"}}, label="ID")])
    result = ctx["out"]
    assert result.info["group"] == ["a", "c"]
    assert result.info["units"] == ["id", "a-unit", "b-unit"]
    assert result.labels["row"] == {"r1": 0, "r3": 1}
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result.data, [[10, 1, 8], [30, 3, 9]])


def test_fdr_rejects_out_of_range_probability_and_extends_axis_annotation(tmp_path):
    form = Form(np.array([[.2], [.7]], dtype=np.float32), ["feature", "statistic"],
                {"feature": {"a": 0, "b": 1}, "statistic": {"P": 0}},
                {"column_note": ["raw"]}, {"statistic": ["column_note"]})
    ctx = execute(tmp_path / "good", {"x": form}, [step("correction")])
    assert ctx["out"].info["column_note"] == ["raw", None, None]
    form.data[0, 0] = 1.2
    with pytest.raises(StepError, match="invalid pvalue"):
        execute(tmp_path / "bad", {"x": form}, [step("correction")])

===== src/task/runner.py =====
# src/task/runner.py
"""Runner — resolves inputs, executes steps in order."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import time

from src.core import ContextError, StepError
from src.task.context import Context
from src.task.step import STEPS
from src.core.contracts import (compatible, describe_form, describe_metadata, infer_output,
                                resolve, validate_form, validate_output, validate_values)


def run(ctx: Context, steps: list[dict]) -> None:
    exports = ctx.artifacts["exports"] = []
    ctx._run_status = "running"
    global_params = ctx.params
    try:
        if ctx.memory:
            ctx.memory.configure(steps, STEPS)
        for i, spec in enumerate(steps, start=1):
            if (ctx.task_dir / 'cancel.requested').exists():
                raise StepError('Run cancelled by user')
            ctx.params = {**global_params, **spec.get("context_params", {})}
            name = spec["name"]
            ctx.event("step_started", name=name, index=i)
            cls = STEPS.get(name)
            if cls is None:
                raise StepError(f"Unknown step '{name}'. Available: {sorted(STEPS)}")
            step = cls()
            names = spec.get("inputs", [])
            _check_arity(name, step.inputs_arity, names)
            ctx.step_params = dict(spec.get("params", {}))
            if cls.parameters_schema is not None and not getattr(cls, "legacy_parameters", False):
                from biopipe_sdk.protocol import validate_parameters
                output = ctx.step_params.pop(cls.output_parameter, None) if cls.output_parameter else None
                ctx.step_params = validate_parameters(cls.parameters_schema, ctx.step_params)
                if output is not None:
                    ctx.step_params[cls.output_parameter] = output
            ctx.step_params["_input_names"] = list(names)
            if getattr(cls, '_isolated', False):
                cls.bind_context(ctx)
            forms, stream = [], None
            schemas, expected = [], None
            successful = False
            try:
                if ctx.memory:
                    forms, stream = ctx.memory.prepare(i - 1, spec, cls)
                else:
                    forms = [_resolve(ctx, n, name) for n in names]
                requirements = [resolve(rule, ctx.step_params) for rule in (cls.input_contracts or [])]
                schemas = []
                if stream is not None:
                    schema = describe_metadata(stream["metadata"], stream["shape"])
                    required = requirements[0] if requirements else None
                    result = compatible(schema, required)
                    if required is not None and result["status"] != "compatible":
                        raise StepError(f"{name} input 1 contract: " + "; ".join(result["reasons"]))
                    original_chunks = stream["chunks"]
                    def checked_chunks(chunks=original_chunks, requirement=required, metadata=stream["metadata"]):
                        try:
                            for chunk in chunks:
                                validate_values(chunk, metadata["info"], requirement, f"{name} input 1")
                                yield chunk
                        finally:
                            chunks.close()
                    stream["chunks"] = checked_chunks()
                    schemas.append(schema)
                else:
                    for port, form in enumerate(forms):
                        required = requirements[min(port, len(requirements) - 1)] if requirements else None
                        validate_form(form, required, f"{name} input {port + 1} ({names[port]})")
                        schemas.append(describe_form(form))
                expected = infer_output(cls.output_contract, schemas, ctx.step_params)
                declared_outputs = list(step.outputs)
                if cls.output_parameter:
                    declared_outputs.append(str(ctx.step_params.get(cls.output_parameter, name)))
                ctx.written_outputs.clear()
                def check_output(key, form):
                    if cls.output_contract and cls.output_contract.get("mode") in {"report", "none"}:
                        raise StepError(f"{name} contract: report operator cannot register a Form")
                    if declared_outputs and key not in declared_outputs:
                        raise StepError(f"{name} contract: undeclared output {key}")
                    if cls.output_contract:
                        validate_output(form, expected, f"{name} output {key}")
                ctx.output_validator = check_output
                start = time.perf_counter()
                if stream is not None:
                    step.run_stream(ctx, stream)
                else:
                    step.run(ctx, forms)
                elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start
                for output in declared_outputs:
                    if output not in ctx.written_outputs:
                        raise StepError(f"Step '{name}' declared output '{output}' but did not register it")
                content = step.export(ctx)
                prefix = f"runs/{ctx.memory.run_id}/" if ctx.memory else ""
                relative = f"{prefix}steps/{i:02d}_{name}.json"
                path = ctx.out_dir / relative
                path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as file:
                    json.dump(content, file, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
                exports.append({"index": i, "name": name, "elapsed": round(elapsed, 4),
                                "inputs": list(names), "params": spec.get("params", {}),
                                "export_path": relative})
                ctx.metadata.setdefault("steps", []).append({"name": name, "elapsed": elapsed})
                ctx.event("contract_checked", name=name, inputs=len(names), outputs=declared_outputs, contract_version=1)
                successful = True
                print(f"[{i}/{len(steps)}] {name} done in {elapsed:.3f}s")
            finally:
                ctx.output_validator = None
                schemas.clear()
                expected = None
                if stream is not None:
                    stream["chunks"].close()
                forms.clear()
                stream = None
                del step
                if getattr(cls, '_isolated', False):
                    cls._context = None
                if "content" in locals():
                    del content
                if ctx.memory:
                    if successful:
                        ctx.memory.finish(spec, elapsed)
                    else:
                        ctx.memory.abort_step()
                elif successful:
                    ctx.event("step_finished", name=name, index=i, seconds=elapsed)
        ctx._run_status = "complete"
    except BaseException as exc:
        ctx._run_status, ctx._run_error = "failed", exc
        raise
    finally:
        ctx.params = global_params


def _check_arity(name: str, arity, form_names: list[str]) -> None:
    if arity == "*":
        if not form_names:
            raise StepError(
                f"Step '{name}' accepts any number of inputs but got none"
            )
        return
    if len(form_names) != arity:
        raise StepError(
            f"Step '{name}' expects {arity} inputs, got {len(form_names)}"
        )


def _resolve(ctx: Context, name: str, step_name: str):
    if not ctx.has(name):
        raise ContextError(
            f"Step '{step_name}' requires form '{name}', which is not "
            f"in context. Available: {sorted(ctx.forms)}"
        )
    return ctx[name]

===== src/task/context/base.py =====
# src/task/context/base.py
"""Context — the runtime image of a manifest."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from biopipe.run_log import RunLog

from src.core import ContextError, Form
from src.task.memory.config import MemoryConfig
from src.task.memory.runtime import MemoryRuntime


class Context:
    """Holds forms, params and task-scoped state."""

    def __init__(self, task_dir: Path, manifest: dict, *, run_log=None):
        self.task_dir = Path(task_dir).resolve()
        self.manifest = manifest
        self.params = manifest.get("params", {})
        self.forms: dict[str, Form] = {}
        self.metadata: dict = {}
        self.step_params: dict = {}
        self.artifacts: dict = {}
        self.output_validator = None
        self.written_outputs = set()

        self.out_dir = self.task_dir / "output"
        self.out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._run_status = "created"
        self._run_error = None
        self.memory = None
        self.run_log = run_log or RunLog(self.out_dir)
        try:
            config = MemoryConfig.parse(manifest)
            if config.enabled:
                self.memory = MemoryRuntime(config, self.out_dir, manifest, self.run_log)
                self.forms = self.memory.store
            else:
                self.event("run_started", run_id=self.run_log.run_id, memory_enabled=False)
        except BaseException as exc:
            if run_log is None:
                try:
                    self.run_log.bootstrap_failure(self.task_dir, exc)
                finally:
                    self.run_log.close()
            raise

    def register_source(self, name: str, spec: dict) -> None:
        """Managed sources are read on first use; legacy mode stays eager."""
        from src.reader import read_source, pick_reader
        path = self.task_dir / spec["path"]
        selected_reader = pick_reader(path)
        if hasattr(selected_reader, '_context'):
            selected_reader._context = self
        def loader():
            return read_source(path, dims=spec["dims"], labels=spec.get("labels", {}),
                               **spec.get("args", {}))
        if self.memory:
            reader = pick_reader(path)
            args = dict(spec.get("args", {}))
            if reader.parameters_schema is not None:
                from biopipe_sdk.protocol import validate_parameters
                args = validate_parameters(reader.parameters_schema, args)
            from contextlib import nullcontext
            from src.reader import _decompressed
            def reader_path():
                return nullcontext(path) if reader.handles_compression else _decompressed(path)
            if reader.supports_disk_read:
                with reader_path() as actual_path:
                    probe = reader.probe(actual_path, **args)
            else:
                probe = reader.probe(path, **args)
            probe["file_bytes"] = path.stat().st_size
            disk_loader = None
            if reader.supports_disk_read:
                from biopipe_sdk.resources import validate_probe
                validate_probe(probe)
                def disk_loader(writer, chunk):
                    import numpy as np
                    from src.core.contracts import validate_form, validate_output, source_schema
                    with reader_path() as actual_path:
                        metadata = reader.read_to_disk(actual_path, spec["dims"], spec.get("labels", {}), writer, chunk, **args)
                    placeholder = Form(np.broadcast_to(np.zeros((), dtype=writer.dtype), writer.shape), **metadata)
                    requirement = {k: v for k, v in reader.output_contract.items() if k != "values"}
                    validate_form(placeholder, requirement, f"reader {type(reader).__name__} metadata")
                    validate_output(placeholder, source_schema(reader.output_contract, spec["dims"]),
                                    f"reader {type(reader).__name__} metadata")
                    # Validate real numeric blocks after flushing, never a fake
                    # zero array. A disk reader's values contract covers all cells.
                    if reader.output_contract.get("values"):
                        from src.core.contracts import validate_values
                        writer.close(retain=True)
                        with writer.path.open("rb") as source:
                            source.seek(writer.offset)
                            while True:
                                values = np.fromfile(source, dtype=writer.dtype, count=max(1, chunk // writer.dtype.itemsize))
                                if not values.size:
                                    break
                                validate_values(values, metadata["info"], reader.output_contract, f"reader {type(reader).__name__} output")
                    return metadata
            self.forms.register_source(name, loader, path.resolve(), probe, disk_loader)
        else:
            self[name] = loader()
            self.event("source_loaded", name=name, shape=list(self[name].data.shape), dims=self[name].dims)
            print(f"[source] {name}: shape={self[name].data.shape}, dims={self[name].dims}")

    def __getitem__(self, name: str) -> Form:
        if name not in self.forms:
            raise ContextError(
                f"Form '{name}' not in context. "
                f"Available: {sorted(self.forms)}"
            )
        return self.forms[name]

    def __setitem__(self, name: str, form: Form) -> None:
        form.validate()
        if self.output_validator is not None:
            self.output_validator(name, form)
        self.forms[name] = form
        self.written_outputs.add(name)

    def has(self, name: str) -> bool:
        return name in self.forms

    def event(self, action: str, **details) -> None:
        if self.memory:
            self.memory.event(action, **details)
        else:
            self.run_log.event(action, **details)

    def log(self, message: str, **details) -> None:
        """Write a structured plugin event and a human-readable console line."""
        event = {"message": str(message), **details}
        # Fail early rather than corrupting the JSONL log with arbitrary values.
        json.dumps(event, allow_nan=False)
        self.event("operator_log", **event)
        self.metadata.setdefault("operator_logs", []).append(event)
        print(str(message), flush=True)

    def artifact_path(self, name: str) -> Path:
        """Allocate a run-local artifact path without permitting traversal."""
        from pathlib import PureWindowsPath
        path = Path(name)
        if not name or path.is_absolute() or PureWindowsPath(name).drive or ".." in path.parts or "\\" in name:
            raise ContextError("Artifact names must be relative paths within the run")
        target = (self.out_dir / "artifacts" / path).resolve()
        if not target.is_relative_to((self.out_dir / "artifacts").resolve()):
            raise ContextError("Artifact path escapes the run")
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        return target

    def finalize(self) -> None:
        """Persist the summary and close the event journal in every memory mode."""
        try:
            self._write_summary()
        finally:
            self.run_log.close()

    def _write_summary(self) -> None:
        """Write the run summary.

        Reads ``artifacts["exports"]`` populated by the runner and
        writes two files:

        * ``output/run_summary.json`` — machine-readable index
        * ``output/run_summary.txt``  — human-readable index

        The summary lists step names, elapsed times and the paths of
        per-step JSON files. It does not repeat step details.
        """
        exports = self.artifacts.get("exports", [])
        close_error = None
        memory_report = None
        if self.memory:
            try:
                memory_report = self.memory.close(self._run_status, self._run_error)
            except Exception as exc:
                close_error = exc
                self._run_status, self._run_error = "failed", exc
                memory_report = self.memory.close("failed", exc)
        else:
            self.event("run_finished", status=self._run_status, error=str(self._run_error) if self._run_error else None)

        summary = {
            "task_dir": str(self.task_dir),
            "generated_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
            "n_steps": len(exports),
            "total_elapsed": round(
                sum(e["elapsed"] for e in exports), 4
            ),
            "steps": exports,
            "status": self._run_status,
            "error": str(self._run_error) if self._run_error else None,
            "run_id": self.run_log.run_id,
            "event_log": str(self.run_log.event_path),
        }
        if memory_report:
            summary["run_id"] = self.memory.run_id
            summary["memory_report"] = str(self.memory.run_dir / "memory_report.json")
            summary["event_log"] = str(self.memory.event_path)
            summary["managed_peak_bytes"] = memory_report["managed_peak_bytes"]

        json_path = self.out_dir / "run_summary.json"
        with open(json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(summary, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)

        txt_path = self.out_dir / "run_summary.txt"
        with open(txt_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(self._render_text(summary))
        (self.run_log.run_dir / "run_summary.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(summary, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
        if close_error:
            raise close_error

    @staticmethod
    def _render_text(summary: dict) -> str:
        lines = []
        lines.append("=" * 60)
        lines.append("Run summary")
        lines.append("=" * 60)
        lines.append(f"task      : {summary['task_dir']}")
        lines.append(f"generated : {summary['generated_at']}")
        lines.append(f"steps     : {summary['n_steps']}")
        lines.append(f"elapsed   : {summary['total_elapsed']:.3f}s")
        lines.append(f"status    : {summary.get('status', 'complete')}")
        if summary.get("error"):
            lines.append(f"error     : {summary['error']}")
        lines.append("")
        lines.append("-" * 60)
        lines.append(f"{'#':<4}{'step':<20}{'elapsed':>10}   export")
        lines.append("-" * 60)

        for e in summary["steps"]:
            lines.append(
                f"{e['index']:<4}"
                f"{e['name']:<20}"
                f"{e['elapsed']:>9.3f}s   "
                f"{e['export_path']}"
            )

        lines.append("")
        lines.append("=" * 60)
        lines.append(
            "Per-step details are at the export paths above. "
            "This file is an index only."
        )
        lines.append("=" * 60)
        return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
